# UGEKAZNA 2-reja: Витрина + ETL karkasi + kesh + meta API — implementatsiya rejasi

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** TZ 3-bo'limidagi yulduz sxemani (11 o'lchov, 14 fakt, `fx_rate`) partitsiya, indeks va RLS bilan qurish; `stg → dwh → mart → pub` ETL karkasini (runner, konnektor protokoli, idempotent upsert, qayta urinishlar, 5 sifat tekshiruvi, jurnal, notifier, atomar publish/rollback) `SyntheticConnector` va `FileConnector` bilan ishga tushirish; Redis kesh + ETag pattern'i, meta API, ETL/sozlamalar admin API va `worker` konteynerini qo'shish.

**Architecture:** Витрина PostgreSQL'da: `dwh` (o'lchov va faktlar, xom SQL migratsiyalar, faktlar dasturiy DDL generatori bilan), `stg` (bitta umumiy jsonb staging jadvali), `mart` (build_id bo'yicha oylik agregatlar), `pub` (`security_invoker` view'lar — faqat aktiv build). ETL `ugk_etl` roli bilan, har tranzaksiyada `app.scope_all = 'on'`; pipeline `extract → dims → facts → aggregates → quality → publish`, istalgan qadam xatosida publish yo'q. API `pub` view'larini `ugk_app` roli bilan RLS ostida o'qiydi va javobni `ugk:v{data_version}:…` kaliti bilan Redis'da keshlaydi. Worker — o'sha image, APScheduler `BlockingScheduler`.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12, FastAPI, SQLAlchemy 2.0 (sync) + psycopg 3, Alembic (xom SQL), APScheduler 3.x, redis-py, pydantic 2, smtplib (stdlib); PostgreSQL 16, Redis 7; pytest (haqiqiy PostgreSQL/Redis, Docker).

**Spec:** [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-24-poydevor-design.md](../specs/2026-09-24-poydevor-design.md) — 4, 5, 6-bo'limlar (1-rejada qamralmagan qismlari). Asos: 1-reja [2026-09-24-01-backend-yadro.md](2026-09-24-01-backend-yadro.md) — uning interfeyslari (`get_engine`, `apply_rls`, `get_ctx`/`Ctx`, `require_perm`, `AuditAction`/`record`/`log_event`, `keystore`, `REGISTRY`, `cli`) shu rejada ishlatiladi.

## Global Constraints

- 1-rejaning barcha Global Constraints'lari amal qiladi (Python `>=3.12`, `postgres:16-alpine`, `redis:7-alpine`, hash bilan qulflangan bog'liqliklar, sirlar faqat Docker secrets/test env, UTC vaqt, `/api/v1`, xato formati, ruscha xabarlar, `$TEST` buyrug'i, `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1`, commit trailer).
- Test buyrug'i: `TEST="docker compose -f deploy/compose.test.yml run --rm test"` — keyin `$TEST pytest ...`. Testlar HAQIQIY PostgreSQL 16 va Redis'ga qarshi; DB mock qilinmaydi.
- Commit xabari oxirida bo'sh qatordan keyin: `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Summalar `numeric(20,2)` **mln so'm**; hajmlar `numeric(20,3)` mln kVt·soat; `date_id int` = `YYYYMMDD`, `month_id int` = `YYYYMM`.
- `branch_id`: `1` — «Узбекгидроэнерго» (HQ), `2..16` — stansiya/kaskadlar, `0` — faqat `mart`'da "barcha filiallar jami" (faqat `scope_all` ko'radi).
- Texnik ustunlar: faktlarda `load_id, src_system, doc_type, src_id, updated_at`; o'lchovlarda `load_id, src_system, updated_at` + tabiiy kalit `code UNIQUE`.
- Faktlardan o'lchovlarga FK — `DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED`. `branch_id` ustunli har `dwh`/`mart` jadvalida `ENABLE` + `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY`, policy `core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id)`.
- ETL faqat `ugk_etl` roli bilan (`app.etl.db.etl_transaction()` — `app.scope_all = 'on'`). API faqat `ugk_app` bilan va faqat `pub` view'lari orqali витринани o'qiydi.
- ETL qayta urinishlari: 3 ta (jami 4 urinish), pauza 30 s → 120 s → 480 s, jitter ±10%; `sleep` va `rng` injeksiya qilinadi — testlar uxlamaydi.
- Inkremental oyna: `[min(watermark − 45 kun, joriy oy 1-kuni), bugun]`, `etl.history_start` dan oldinga chiqmaydi (quyidagi Ruling 1).
- Kesh: kalit `ugk:v{data_version}:{endpoint}:{scope_hash}:{filters_hash}`, TTL 3600 s, single-flight lock `SET NX PX 5000`; Redis yo'q bo'lsa fail-open; `Cache-Control: private, no-cache`, `ETag`, `If-None-Match` → 304.
- Freshness chegarasi — `core.settings` `freshness.max_age_hours` = 6. Oxirgi 3 build saqlanadi (`builds.keep` = 3).
- Saqlash: faktlar 5 yil (`retention.fact_years`, eski partitsiyalar o'chiriladi), agregatlar 10 yil — faktlari o'chirilgan oylar yangi build'ga oldingi aktiv build'dan ko'chiriladi (`build_all` carry-forward).
- "Bugun" biznes-mantiqda (davrlar, sintetik ma'lumot) — Toshkent vaqti UTC+5 (DST yo'q): `app.dwh.dates.TASHKENT`, `today_tashkent()`.
- Testlarda sintetik ma'lumot sanasi qat'iy: `tests.etl_helpers.TEST_AS_OF = date(2026, 9, 24)`.
- ПКМ №54 statya kodlari (`PKM54-I01…`, `PKM54-O01…`) — vaqtinchalik sintetik kodlar; haqiqiy klassifikator kelganda almashtiriladi (TZ ochiq savoli). Tizim "Прочие" statyalari: `PKM54-I06` (приход), `PKM54-O09` (расход) — migratsiyada seed qilinadi.
- Spec'dan ongli chetlanishlar (14-vazifa 8-qadamida spec'ga yoziladi):
  1. **Inkremental oyna `min(...)`, `max(...)` emas.** Spec/qarorlardagi `max(watermark − 45 kun, oy boshi)` oxirgi muvaffaqiyatli yuklash eski bo'lsa (masalan, 3 oy oldin) oradagi o'zgarishlarni o'tkazib yuboradi. TZ matni ("последние 45 дней плюс текущий месяц целиком") `min` ga mos.
  2. **SCD-2 alohida versiya jadvallarida:** `dwh.dim_branch` (PK `branch_id`, joriy qiymat) + `dwh.dim_branch_version (branch_id, capacity_mw, valid_from, valid_to, is_current)`; xuddi shunday `dim_loan` + `dim_loan_version (rate)`. Sabab: faktlar va `core.position_branches` dan FK uchun `branch_id` yagona bo'lishi shart — bitta SCD-2 jadvali FK nishoni bo'la olmaydi.
  3. **Partitsiyalangan faktlarda idempotentlik kaliti `(src_system, doc_type, src_id, date_id)`** (PostgreSQL talabi: UNIQUE partitsiya kalitini o'z ichiga oladi). Hujjat sanasi o'zgarsa loader eski nusxani `(src_system, doc_type, src_id)` indeksi bo'yicha oldindan o'chiradi.
  4. **Staging — bitta umumiy `stg.rows (seq, load_id, target, payload jsonb)`** (UNLOGGED) + `stg.batch (load_id, target, source, received_at, row_count)`. Qarorlardagi `stg.batch.doc_type` nomi `target` ga o'zgartirildi — faktlarda `doc_type` allaqachon manba hujjat turini bildiradi.
  5. **Eski fakt partitsiyalari `DETACH` + `DROP`, pg_dump arxivisiz** — arxiv pgBackRest zaxiralarida (4-reja).
  6. **`core.alert_rules` — o'qish va versiyali tahrirlash (qiymat, operator, daraja, yoqilgan/o'chirilgan)**; yangi qoida yaratish/o'chirish yo'q: har qoidaning metrikasi kodda hisoblanadi, "o'chirish" = `enabled=false`.
  7. **Admin "ETL'ni ishga tushirish" so'rovni `core.etl_requests` navbatiga yozadi**, uni worker bajaradi — API konteyneri `ugk_etl` parolini olmaydi.

## Review Focus

1. **Manbada hujjat sanasi o'zgarsa (boshqa oy partitsiyasiga ko'chsa)** — витринада dublikat qolmasligi, faqat yangi sanadagi nusxa: 5-vazifa `test_moved_document_date_does_not_duplicate`.
2. **Bitta extract ichida bir hujjat ikki marta kelsa** — `ON CONFLICT … cannot affect row a second time` bilan yiqilmaslik, oxirgisi yutishi: 5-vazifa `test_duplicate_rows_in_one_batch_last_wins`.
3. **Worker yuklash o'rtasida o'chib qolsa** — `running` holatida qolgan yugurish keyingi ishga tushishda `failed` bo'lishi, lock bo'shashi: 8-vazifa `test_stale_running_run_is_marked_failed`.
4. **Hisoblash paytida publish sodir bo'lsa** — yangi build ma'lumoti eski `v{N}` kaliti ostida keshlanmasligi (rollback'dan keyin noto'g'ri javob bermaslik uchun): 11-vazifa `test_version_change_during_compute_is_not_cached`.
5. **Admin mavjud bo'lmagan filialni lavozimga biriktirsa** — 500 emas, 422 `validation_failed`: 2-vazifa `test_position_with_unknown_branch_is_422`.

### Task 1: Konfiguratsiya kengaytmasi, APScheduler bog'liqligi va Notifier

**Files:**
- Modify: `backend/requirements.in`, `backend/requirements.txt`, `backend/requirements-dev.txt` (qayta qulflash), `backend/pyproject.toml`, `backend/app/core/config.py`, `backend/app/core/db.py`, `scripts/gen_secrets.py`
- Create: `backend/app/etl/__init__.py`, `backend/app/etl/notify.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_notify.py`, `backend/tests/test_config.py` (qo'shimcha testlar)

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Settings`, `get_settings()`, `get_engine(role)`, `get_logger`.
- Produces:
  - `Settings` yangi maydonlari: `etl_statement_timeout_ms: int = 1_800_000`, `etl_file_dir: str = "/data/inbox"`, `synthetic_seed: int = 916202617`, `smtp_host: str = ""`, `smtp_port: int = 25`, `smtp_from: str = "ugekazna@localhost"`, `smtp_to: str = ""`, `smtp_user: str = ""`, `smtp_password: SecretStr`, `smtp_starttls: bool = True`, property `smtp_recipients -> list[str]`.
  - `get_engine("etl")` — `statement_timeout = etl_statement_timeout_ms` (30 daqiqa).
  - `app.etl.notify`: `Notification(kind, subject, body)` (`kind` ∈ `"etl_failed" | "etl_quality" | "control_totals" | "stale_data"`), `Notifier` (Protocol: `notify(notification) -> None`), `LogNotifier`, `EmailNotifier(*, host, port, sender, recipients, username="", password="", starttls=True, timeout=10.0, smtp_factory=smtplib.SMTP)`, `CompositeNotifier(notifiers)`, `build_notifier(settings) -> Notifier`.

- [ ] **Step 2: Testlarni yozish**

`backend/tests/test_notify.py`:
```python
from email.message import EmailMessage
from typing import Any

import pytest

from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.etl.notify import (
    CompositeNotifier,
    EmailNotifier,
    LogNotifier,
    Notification,
    build_notifier,
)


class FakeSMTP:
    instances: list["FakeSMTP"] = []

    def __init__(self, host: str, port: int, timeout: float) -> None:
        self.host, self.port, self.timeout = host, port, timeout
        self.tls = False
        self.login_args: tuple[str, str] | None = None
        self.sent: list[EmailMessage] = []
        FakeSMTP.instances.append(self)

    def __enter__(self) -> "FakeSMTP":
        return self

    def __exit__(self, *exc: object) -> None:
        return None

    def starttls(self, context: Any = None) -> None:
        self.tls = True

    def login(self, user: str, password: str) -> None:
        self.login_args = (user, password)

    def send_message(self, message: EmailMessage) -> None:
        self.sent.append(message)


def _email(**overrides: Any) -> EmailNotifier:
    params: dict[str, Any] = {
        "host": "relay.local", "port": 587, "sender": "ugk@uge.uz",
        "recipients": ["kazna@uge.uz", "it@uge.uz"], "password": "relay-secret",
        "smtp_factory": FakeSMTP,
    }
    params.update(overrides)
    return EmailNotifier(**params)


def test_email_is_sent_with_starttls_and_login() -> None:
    FakeSMTP.instances.clear()
    _email().notify(Notification("etl_failed", "Загрузка 1С завершилась ошибкой", "Шаг: facts"))
    smtp = FakeSMTP.instances[-1]
    assert (smtp.host, smtp.port) == ("relay.local", 587)
    assert smtp.tls is True
    assert smtp.login_args == ("ugk@uge.uz", "relay-secret")
    message = smtp.sent[0]
    assert message["Subject"] == "[UGEKAZNA] Загрузка 1С завершилась ошибкой"
    assert message["To"] == "kazna@uge.uz, it@uge.uz"
    assert "Шаг: facts" in message.get_content()


def test_subject_newlines_cannot_inject_headers() -> None:
    FakeSMTP.instances.clear()
    _email(password="").notify(Notification("etl_failed", "a\r\nBcc: evil@x.uz", "b"))
    smtp = FakeSMTP.instances[-1]
    assert smtp.login_args is None
    assert smtp.sent[0]["Bcc"] is None
    assert "\n" not in smtp.sent[0]["Subject"]


def test_smtp_failure_is_logged_not_raised() -> None:
    def broken(*_a: Any, **_k: Any) -> Any:
        raise ConnectionRefusedError("relay down")

    _email(smtp_factory=broken).notify(Notification("stale_data", "x", "y"))


def test_composite_continues_after_failing_notifier() -> None:
    received: list[str] = []

    class Boom:
        def notify(self, notification: Notification) -> None:
            raise RuntimeError("boom")

    class Collect:
        def notify(self, notification: Notification) -> None:
            received.append(notification.kind)

    CompositeNotifier([Boom(), Collect()]).notify(Notification("etl_quality", "s", "b"))
    assert received == ["etl_quality"]


def test_build_notifier_adds_email_only_when_configured() -> None:
    plain = build_notifier(get_settings().model_copy(update={"smtp_host": ""}))
    assert isinstance(plain, CompositeNotifier)
    assert [type(n) for n in plain.notifiers] == [LogNotifier]
    full = build_notifier(
        get_settings().model_copy(update={"smtp_host": "relay", "smtp_to": "a@uge.uz"})
    )
    assert isinstance(full, CompositeNotifier)
    assert [type(n) for n in full.notifiers] == [LogNotifier, EmailNotifier]


@pytest.mark.parametrize("raw", ["a@uge.uz, b@uge.uz", " a@uge.uz ,b@uge.uz,"])
def test_smtp_recipients_parsing(raw: str) -> None:
    settings = get_settings().model_copy(update={"smtp_to": raw})
    assert settings.smtp_recipients == ["a@uge.uz", "b@uge.uz"]
```

`backend/app/etl/notify.py`:
```python
"""ETL xabarnomalari (spec 5.4): log va ichki SMTP relay. Telegram — keyingi spec."""

from __future__ import annotations

import smtplib
import ssl
from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass
from email.message import EmailMessage
from typing import Any, Literal, Protocol

from app.core.config import Settings
from app.core.logging import get_logger

log = get_logger("ugekazna.notify")
Kind = Literal["etl_failed", "etl_quality", "control_totals", "stale_data"]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Notification:
    kind: Kind
    subject: str
    body: str


class Notifier(Protocol):
    def notify(self, notification: Notification) -> None: ...


class LogNotifier:
    def notify(self, notification: Notification) -> None:
        log.warning(
            "notification",
            kind=notification.kind,
            subject=notification.subject,
            body=notification.body,
        )


def _one_line(value: str) -> str:
    # Sarlavhaga CR/LF tushmasin (header injection).
    return " ".join(value.split())[:200]


class EmailNotifier:
    def __init__(
        self,
        *,
        host: str,
        port: int,
        sender: str,
        recipients: Sequence[str],
        username: str = "",
        password: str = "",
        starttls: bool = True,
        timeout: float = 10.0,
        smtp_factory: Callable[..., Any] = smtplib.SMTP,
    ) -> None:
        self.host = host
        self.port = port
        self.sender = sender
        self.recipients = list(recipients)
        self.username = username
        self.password = password
        self.starttls = starttls
        self.timeout = timeout
        self._factory = smtp_factory

    def notify(self, notification: Notification) -> None:
        message = EmailMessage()
        message["Subject"] = f"[UGEKAZNA] {_one_line(notification.subject)}"
        message["From"] = self.sender
        message["To"] = ", ".join(self.recipients)
        message.set_content(notification.body)
        try:
            with self._factory(self.host, self.port, timeout=self.timeout) as smtp:
                if self.starttls:
                    smtp.starttls(context=ssl.create_default_context())
                if self.password:
                    smtp.login(self.username or self.sender, self.password)
                smtp.send_message(message)
        except (OSError, smtplib.SMTPException) as exc:
            # Xabarnoma xatosi ETL'ni yiqitmasligi kerak.
            log.error("notify_email_failed", kind=notification.kind, error=type(exc).__name__)


class CompositeNotifier:
    def __init__(self, notifiers: Sequence[Notifier]) -> None:
        self.notifiers = tuple(notifiers)

    def notify(self, notification: Notification) -> None:
        for notifier in self.notifiers:
            try:
                notifier.notify(notification)
            except Exception as exc:  # bitta kanal qolganlarini to'xtatmaydi
                log.error("notifier_failed", notifier=type(notifier).__name__,
                          error=type(exc).__name__)


def build_notifier(settings: Settings) -> Notifier:
    notifiers: list[Notifier] = [LogNotifier()]
    if settings.smtp_host and settings.smtp_recipients:
        notifiers.append(
            EmailNotifier(
                host=settings.smtp_host,
                port=settings.smtp_port,
                sender=settings.smtp_from,
                recipients=settings.smtp_recipients,
                username=settings.smtp_user,
                password=settings.smtp_password.get_secret_value(),
                starttls=settings.smtp_starttls,
            )
        )
    return CompositeNotifier(notifiers)
```

### Task 2: `dwh` o'lchovlari, `fx_rate`, filiallar seed'i, shifrlangan rekvizitlar

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/migrations/versions/0005_dwh_dims.py`, `backend/app/dwh/__init__.py`, `backend/app/dwh/dates.py`, `backend/app/etl/db.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/core/reencrypt.py` (`REGISTRY`), `backend/app/admin/service.py` (filial mavjudligi)
- Test: `backend/tests/test_dwh_dims.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `get_engine`, `new_session`, `apply_rls`, `keystore.encrypt_str/decrypt_str/invalidate_keyring`, `blind_index`, `blob_version`, `Purpose`, `cli.main`, `ValidationFailed`, `tests.helpers.login`.
- Produces:
  - Jadvallar: `dwh.dim_date (date_id PK, date, year, quarter, month, month_id, day, iso_dow, is_workday)` 2015-01-01…2040-12-31; `dwh.dim_branch (branch_id PK, code UNIQUE, name, short_name, inn, kind ∈ head|ges|cascade, capacity_mw, lat, lon, sort_order)`; `dwh.dim_branch_version (branch_id, capacity_mw, valid_from, valid_to, is_current)`; `dwh.dim_item (item_id, code, name, activity ∈ centralized|operating|investing, direction ∈ in|out, sort_order)`; `dwh.dim_counterparty (cp_id, code, name, kind ∈ supplier|buyer|bank|budget|ifi|other, inn_enc, inn_bidx)`; `dwh.dim_bank (bank_id, code, bank_name, account_enc, account_bidx, currency)`; `dwh.dim_nomenclature (nom_id, code, name, category, unit, enbt_code)`; `dwh.dim_source (source_id, code, name, kind ∈ budget|ifi|bank|own)`; `dwh.dim_object (object_id, code, name, program_code, work_type ∈ new|reconstruction|modernization, source_id, commissioning_year, total_cost)`; `dwh.dim_contract (contract_id, code, number, contract_date, cp_id, branch_id, amount, end_date, object_id, kind ∈ receivable|payable)`; `dwh.dim_loan (loan_id, code, creditor, project, currency, rate, grace_until, maturity, amount_uzs, branch_id)`; `dwh.dim_loan_version (loan_id, rate, valid_from, valid_to, is_current)`; `dwh.dim_position (pos_id, code, category, department)`; `dwh.fx_rate (currency, date_id, rate)`. `dim_date` va `*_version` dan tashqari hammasida `load_id, src_system, updated_at`.
  - Seed: 16 filial (id 1..16) va ularning 1-versiyasi (`valid_from = 2000-01-01`), tizim statyalari `PKM54-I06`, `PKM54-O09`.
  - FK `core.position_branches.branch_id → dwh.dim_branch`.
  - `app.dwh.dates`: `date_id(d) -> int`, `month_id(d) -> int`, `from_date_id(v) -> date`, `month_start(d) -> date`, `month_end(d) -> date`, `add_months(d, n) -> date`.
  - `app.etl.db`: `etl_transaction() -> ContextManager[Connection]` (`ugk_etl`, `app.scope_all = 'on'`), `SCOPE_ALL_SQL`.
  - `REGISTRY` qo'shimchasi: `EncryptedColumn("dwh.dim_counterparty", "code", "inn_enc", Purpose.PII)`, `EncryptedColumn("dwh.dim_bank", "code", "account_enc", Purpose.BANK)` — AAD `"dwh.dim_counterparty.inn_enc:{code}"`.
  - `app.admin.service._validate_branches(db, branch_ids)` — noma'lum filial → `ValidationFailed("validation_failed", fields=["branch_ids"], missing=[...])`.

`backend/tests/test_dwh_dims.py`:
```python
import uuid
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator
from datetime import date

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import select, text
from sqlalchemy.exc import IntegrityError, ProgrammingError

from app import cli
from app.auth.models import Position, Role
from app.core import keystore
from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.crypto import Purpose, blind_index, blob_version
from app.core.db import apply_rls, new_session
from app.core.reencrypt import REGISTRY
from app.dwh.dates import add_months, date_id, from_date_id, month_end, month_id, month_start
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.main import create_app
from tests.helpers import SeedUser, login


@pytest.fixture
def client() -> Iterator[TestClient]:
    with TestClient(create_app(), base_url="https://testserver") as c:
        yield c


def test_date_helpers() -> None:
    assert date_id(date(2026, 9, 24)) == 20260924
    assert month_id(date(2026, 9, 24)) == 202609
    assert from_date_id(20240229) == date(2024, 2, 29)
    assert month_start(date(2026, 9, 24)) == date(2026, 9, 1)
    assert month_end(date(2024, 2, 10)) == date(2024, 2, 29)
    assert add_months(date(2026, 11, 30), 3) == date(2027, 2, 28)


def test_sixteen_branches_seeded() -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT branch_id, code, kind, capacity_mw FROM dwh.dim_branch "
                 "WHERE branch_id BETWEEN 1 AND 16 ORDER BY branch_id")
        ).all()
        versions = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT count(*) FROM dwh.dim_branch_version WHERE is_current "
                 "AND branch_id BETWEEN 1 AND 16")
        ).scalar_one()
    assert [r.branch_id for r in rows] == list(range(1, 17))
    assert rows[0].kind == "head" and rows[0].capacity_mw is None
    assert rows[1].code == "UGE-02" and float(rows[1].capacity_mw) == 666.0
    assert {r.kind for r in rows[1:]} == {"ges", "cascade"}
    assert versions == 16


def test_dim_date_range_and_workdays() -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        first, last = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT min(date_id), max(date_id) FROM dwh.dim_date")
        ).one()
        thursday, saturday = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT (SELECT is_workday FROM dwh.dim_date WHERE date_id = 20260924), "
                 "(SELECT is_workday FROM dwh.dim_date WHERE date_id = 20260926)")
        ).one()
    assert (first, last) == (20150101, 20401231)
    assert thursday is True and saturday is False


def test_system_other_items_exist() -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        codes = set(conn.execute(text("SELECT code FROM dwh.dim_item")).scalars())
    assert {"PKM54-I06", "PKM54-O09"} <= codes


def test_branch_rls_for_app_role() -> None:
    with new_session() as s:
        assert s.execute(text("SELECT branch_id FROM dwh.dim_branch")).all() == []
    with new_session() as s:
        apply_rls(s, frozenset({3}))
        assert list(s.execute(text("SELECT branch_id FROM dwh.dim_branch")).scalars()) == [3]


def test_app_role_cannot_write_dims() -> None:
    with new_session() as s, pytest.raises(ProgrammingError, match="permission denied"):
        s.execute(text("INSERT INTO dwh.dim_item (code, name, activity, direction) "
                       "VALUES ('X', 'X', 'operating', 'in')"))


def test_position_branch_fk() -> None:
    with new_session() as s:
        role_id = s.execute(select(Role.id).where(Role.code == "economist_filiala")).scalar_one()
        pos = Position(code=f"p_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}", name="X", role_id=role_id)
        s.add(pos)
        s.flush()
        with pytest.raises(IntegrityError):
            s.execute(text("INSERT INTO core.position_branches VALUES (:p, 999)"), {"p": pos.id})
        s.rollback()


def test_position_with_unknown_branch_is_422(
    client: TestClient, make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser]
) -> None:
    login(client, make_user("admin"))
    with new_session() as s:
        econ_id = s.execute(select(Role.id).where(Role.code == "economist_filiala")).scalar_one()
    r = client.post("/api/v1/admin/positions", json={
        "code": f"e_{uuid.uuid4().hex[:6]}", "name": "Экономист", "role_id": econ_id,
        "branch_ids": [3, 999]})
    assert r.status_code == 422
    assert r.json()["error"]["params"]["fields"] == ["branch_ids"]
    assert r.json()["error"]["params"]["missing"] == [999]


def test_registry_includes_dwh_columns() -> None:
    columns = {(c.table, c.column, c.purpose) for c in REGISTRY}
    assert ("dwh.dim_counterparty", "inn_enc", Purpose.PII) in columns
    assert ("dwh.dim_bank", "account_enc", Purpose.BANK) in columns


def test_counterparty_inn_reencrypted_on_pii_rotation() -> None:
    code = f"CP-T{uuid.uuid4().hex[:8]}"
    aad = f"dwh.dim_counterparty.inn_enc:{code}"
    bidx = blind_index(get_settings().key_bytes("blind_index_key"), "inn", "305123456")
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        conn.execute(
            text("INSERT INTO dwh.dim_counterparty (code, name, kind, inn_enc, inn_bidx, "
                 "src_system) VALUES (:c, 'ООО «Тест»', 'supplier', :e, :b, 'test')"),
            {"c": code, "e": keystore.encrypt_str(Purpose.PII, "305123456", aad), "b": bidx},
        )
    try:
        assert cli.main(["rotate-key", "--purpose", "pii"]) == 0
        keystore.invalidate_keyring()
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            blob = conn.execute(
                text("SELECT inn_enc FROM dwh.dim_counterparty WHERE code = :c"), {"c": code}
            ).scalar_one()
            active = conn.execute(
                text("SELECT version FROM core.crypto_keys "
                     "WHERE purpose = 'pii' AND status = 'active'")
            ).scalar_one()
        assert blob_version(bytes(blob)) == active
        assert keystore.decrypt_str(Purpose.PII, bytes(blob), aad) == "305123456"
    finally:
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            conn.execute(text("DELETE FROM dwh.dim_counterparty WHERE code = :c"), {"c": code})
```

```python
def _sql_value(value: object) -> str:
    if value is None:
        return "NULL"
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return "'" + value.replace("'", "''") + "'"
    return str(value)


def _row(values: tuple[object, ...]) -> str:

        f"""
        CREATE TABLE dwh.dim_position (
          pos_id serial PRIMARY KEY,
          code varchar(32) NOT NULL UNIQUE,
          category varchar(100) NOT NULL,
          department varchar(200) NOT NULL,
          {TECH}
        )
        """
    )
    op.execute(
        f"""
        CREATE TABLE dwh.fx_rate (
          currency char(3) NOT NULL,
          date_id integer NOT NULL REFERENCES dwh.dim_date (date_id),
          rate numeric(18,6) NOT NULL CHECK (rate > 0),
          {TECH},
          PRIMARY KEY (currency, date_id)
        )
        """
    )
    op.execute("CREATE INDEX fx_rate_date_idx ON dwh.fx_rate (date_id)")
    # RLS seed'dan keyin yoqiladi: FORCE jadval egasini ham cheklaydi.
    for table in RLS_TABLES:
        op.execute(f"ALTER TABLE dwh.{table} ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY")
        op.execute(f"ALTER TABLE dwh.{table} FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY")
        op.execute(
            f"CREATE POLICY branch_scope ON dwh.{table} USING (core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id)) "
            "WITH CHECK (core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id))"
        )
    tables = ", ".join(f"dwh.{t}" for t in DIM_TABLES)
    op.execute(f"GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON {tables} TO ugk_etl")
    op.execute("GRANT USAGE, SELECT ON ALL SEQUENCES IN SCHEMA dwh TO ugk_etl")
    # ugk_app: filiallar/statyalar ro'yxati (pub view'lar, admin tekshiruvi) va kalit
    # rotatsiyasi uchun faqat shifrlangan ustunlar.
    op.execute("GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA dwh TO ugk_app")
    op.execute("GRANT SELECT ON dwh.dim_branch, dwh.dim_item TO ugk_app")
    op.execute("GRANT SELECT (code, inn_enc), UPDATE (inn_enc) ON dwh.dim_counterparty TO ugk_app")
    op.execute(
        "GRANT SELECT (code, account_enc), UPDATE (account_enc) ON dwh.dim_bank TO ugk_app"
    )
    op.execute(
        "ALTER TABLE core.position_branches ADD CONSTRAINT position_branches_branch_fk "
        "FOREIGN KEY (branch_id) REFERENCES dwh.dim_branch (branch_id)"
    )


def downgrade() -> None:
    op.execute("ALTER TABLE core.position_branches DROP CONSTRAINT position_branches_branch_fk")
    for table in reversed(DIM_TABLES):
        op.execute(f"DROP TABLE dwh.{table}")
```

`DIM_TABLES` tartibi `downgrade` uchun muhim: teskari tartibda `fx_rate` → … → `dim_date` (bog'liqlar avval o'chadi).

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — yordamchilar, REGISTRY, admin tekshiruvi**

`backend/app/dwh/__init__.py`: bo'sh fayl.

`backend/app/dwh/dates.py`:
```python
"""Sana kalitlari: date_id = YYYYMMDD, month_id = YYYYMM."""

from __future__ import annotations

import calendar
from datetime import date


def date_id(d: date) -> int:
    return d.year * 10000 + d.month * 100 + d.day


def month_id(d: date) -> int:
    return d.year * 100 + d.month


def from_date_id(value: int) -> date:
    return date(value // 10000, value // 100 % 100, value % 100)


def month_start(d: date) -> date:
    return d.replace(day=1)


def month_end(d: date) -> date:
    return d.replace(day=calendar.monthrange(d.year, d.month)[1])


def add_months(d: date, n: int) -> date:
    index = d.year * 12 + d.month - 1 + n
    year, month0 = divmod(index, 12)
    last = calendar.monthrange(year, month0 + 1)[1]
    return date(year, month0 + 1, min(d.day, last))
```

`backend/app/etl/db.py`:
```python
"""ETL tranzaksiyasi: ugk_etl roli, barcha filiallar doirasi (RLS scope_all)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Iterator
from contextlib import contextmanager

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from app.core.db import get_engine

SCOPE_ALL_SQL = (
    "SELECT set_config('app.scope_all', 'on', true), set_config('app.branch_ids', '{}', true)"
)


@contextmanager
def etl_transaction() -> Iterator[Connection]:
    with get_engine("etl").begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(text(SCOPE_ALL_SQL))
        yield conn
```

`backend/app/admin/service.py` — birinchi sqlalchemy import qatorini
`from sqlalchemy import Select, and_, delete, func, or_, select, text` ga o'zgartiring va `_set_branches` ni almashtiring:
```python
def _validate_branches(db: Session, branch_ids: list[int]) -> None:
    wanted = sorted(set(branch_ids))
    if not wanted:
        return
    found = set(
        db.execute(
            text("SELECT branch_id FROM dwh.dim_branch WHERE branch_id = ANY(:ids)"),
            {"ids": wanted},
        ).scalars()
    )
    missing = [b for b in wanted if b not in found]
    if missing:
        raise ValidationFailed("validation_failed", fields=["branch_ids"], missing=missing)


def _set_branches(db: Session, position_id: int, branch_ids: list[int]) -> None:
    _validate_branches(db, branch_ids)
    db.execute(delete(PositionBranch).where(PositionBranch.position_id == position_id))
    for branch in sorted(set(branch_ids)):
        db.add(PositionBranch(position_id=position_id, branch_id=branch))
```
(Admin sessiyasida `get_ctx` `scope_all` o'rnatgan — `dim_branch` RLS'i barcha filiallarni ko'rsatadi.)

### Task 3: `dwh` faktlari — partitsiyalar, indekslar, idempotentlik kaliti, RLS

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/migrations/versions/0006_dwh_facts.py`, `backend/app/dwh/partitions.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_dwh_facts.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `etl_transaction()`, `new_session`, `apply_rls`, `app.dwh.dates`.
- Produces:
  - 14 fakt jadvali (ustunlar pastdagi `FACTS` spetsifikatsiyasida): `fact_cashflow` (oylik), `fact_balance` (yillik), `fact_payment_plan`, `fact_payment_order` (oylik), `fact_debt` (yillik), `fact_generation` (yillik), `fact_energy_sales`, `fact_procurement`, `fact_stock` (yillik), `fact_stock_move` (yillik, BRIN), `fact_investment`, `fact_loan_schedule`, `fact_hr`, `fact_target_funds`. Har birida `date_id`, `branch_id`, FK ustunlari (`… DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED`), o'lchovlar, `load_id, src_system, doc_type, src_id, updated_at`, `UNIQUE (src_system, doc_type, src_id[, date_id])` nomi `{table}_src_uq`.
  - SQL: `dwh.ensure_partitions(p_table text, p_from date, p_to date) -> integer` (SECURITY DEFINER, whitelist), `dwh.drop_old_partitions(p_keep_years integer) -> integer` (DETACH + DROP). Partitsiya nomi: `{table}_pYYYYMM` yoki `{table}_pYYYY`.
  - `app.dwh.partitions`: `PARTITIONED: dict[str, str]` (`jadval → "month" | "year"`), `ensure_partitions(conn, table, date_from, date_to) -> int`, `ensure_ahead(conn, today, months_ahead) -> int`, `drop_old(conn, keep_years) -> int`, `list_partitions(conn, table) -> list[str]`.

`backend/tests/test_dwh_facts.py`:
```python
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import date

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection
from sqlalchemy.exc import DBAPIError, IntegrityError, ProgrammingError

from app.core.db import new_session
from app.dwh import partitions
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction

FACTS = (
    "fact_cashflow", "fact_balance", "fact_payment_plan", "fact_payment_order", "fact_debt",
    "fact_generation", "fact_energy_sales", "fact_procurement", "fact_stock", "fact_stock_move",
    "fact_investment", "fact_loan_schedule", "fact_hr", "fact_target_funds",
)
SRC = "test_facts"


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _cleanup() -> Iterator[None]:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        partitions.ensure_partitions(conn, "fact_cashflow", date(2026, 8, 1), date(2026, 9, 30))
    yield
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        conn.execute(text("DELETE FROM dwh.fact_cashflow WHERE src_system = :s"), {"s": SRC})


def _item(conn: Connection) -> int:
    return int(conn.execute(
        text("SELECT item_id FROM dwh.dim_item WHERE code = 'PKM54-O09'")).scalar_one())


def _insert_cash(conn: Connection, src_id: str, day: int, item_id: int) -> None:
    conn.execute(
        text("INSERT INTO dwh.fact_cashflow (date_id, branch_id, item_id, direction, amount, "
             "load_id, src_system, doc_type, src_id) "
             "VALUES (:d, 2, :i, 'out', 10.5, 0, :s, 'bank_entry', :sid)"),
        {"d": day, "i": item_id, "s": SRC, "sid": src_id},
    )


def test_all_facts_exist_with_forced_rls() -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT c.relname, c.relrowsecurity, c.relforcerowsecurity FROM pg_class c "
                 "JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = c.relnamespace "
                 "WHERE n.nspname = 'dwh' AND c.relname = ANY(:t)"),
            {"t": list(FACTS)},
        ).all()
    assert {r.relname for r in rows} == set(FACTS)
    assert all(r.relrowsecurity and r.relforcerowsecurity for r in rows)


def test_partitioned_unique_key_includes_date() -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        item = _item(conn)
        _insert_cash(conn, "doc-1", 20260805, item)
        _insert_cash(conn, "doc-1", 20260910, item)  # boshqa sana — PostgreSQL ruxsat beradi
    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError), etl_transaction() as conn:
        _insert_cash(conn, "doc-1", 20260805, _item(conn))


def test_dimension_fk_is_checked_at_commit() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(IntegrityError), etl_transaction() as conn:
        _insert_cash(conn, "doc-bad", 20260805, 999_999)
        # Deferred FK: shu nuqtagacha xato yo'q, commit'da yiqiladi.
        assert conn.execute(text("SELECT 1")).scalar_one() == 1


def test_ensure_partitions_monthly_and_yearly() -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        first = partitions.ensure_partitions(conn, "fact_cashflow", date(2031, 1, 10),
                                             date(2031, 3, 5))
        again = partitions.ensure_partitions(conn, "fact_cashflow", date(2031, 1, 1),
                                             date(2031, 3, 31))
        yearly = partitions.ensure_partitions(conn, "fact_balance", date(2031, 5, 1),
                                              date(2031, 6, 1))
        names = partitions.list_partitions(conn, "fact_cashflow")
    # Test DB konteyneri qayta ishga tushirishlar orasida saqlanadi: birinchi chaqiruv 0 yoki 3.
    assert first in (0, 3)
    assert again == 0
    assert yearly in (0, 1)
    assert {"fact_cashflow_p203101", "fact_cashflow_p203102", "fact_cashflow_p203103"} <= set(names)


def test_ensure_partitions_rejects_unknown_table() -> None:
    with pytest.raises(DBAPIError, match="partitsiyalanmagan"), etl_transaction() as conn:
        conn.execute(text("SELECT dwh.ensure_partitions('fact_hr', date '2026-01-01', "
                          "date '2026-02-01')"))
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            partitions.ensure_partitions(conn, "core.users", date(2026, 1, 1), date(2026, 1, 2))


def test_drop_old_partitions() -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        partitions.ensure_partitions(conn, "fact_balance", date(2016, 1, 1), date(2016, 12, 31))
        dropped = partitions.drop_old(conn, keep_years=5)
        names = partitions.list_partitions(conn, "fact_balance")
    assert dropped >= 1
    assert "fact_balance_p2016" not in names


def test_ensure_ahead_creates_three_months() -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        partitions.ensure_ahead(conn, date(2032, 11, 15), months_ahead=3)
        names = set(partitions.list_partitions(conn, "fact_payment_order"))
    assert {"fact_payment_order_p203211", "fact_payment_order_p203302"} <= names


def test_app_role_cannot_read_facts() -> None:
    with new_session() as s, pytest.raises(ProgrammingError, match="permission denied"):
        s.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM dwh.fact_cashflow"))


def test_mandatory_indexes_exist() -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        names = set(conn.execute(
            text("SELECT indexname FROM pg_indexes WHERE schemaname = 'dwh'")).scalars())
    expected = {
        "fact_cashflow_date_id_branch_id_idx",
        "fact_cashflow_branch_id_item_id_idx",
        "fact_cashflow_contract_id_idx",
        "fact_cashflow_date_brin",
        "fact_stock_move_date_brin",
        "fact_debt_contract_id_idx",
        "fact_payment_order_branch_id_date_id_p_idx",
        "fact_debt_branch_id_p_idx",
        "fact_procurement_branch_id_p_idx",
        "fact_cashflow_src_system_doc_type_src_id_idx",
    }
    assert expected <= names
```

- [ ] **Step 3: 0006 migratsiyasi**

`backend/migrations/versions/0006_dwh_facts.py`:
```python
"""dwh faktlari: partitsiyalar, indekslar, RLS, idempotentlik kalitlari

Revision ID: 0006
Revises: 0005
Create Date: 2026-09-24

Partitsiyalangan jadvalda UNIQUE partitsiya kalitini (date_id) o'z ichiga olishi SHART
(PostgreSQL cheklovi). Shuning uchun partitsiyalangan faktlarda idempotentlik kaliti
(src_system, doc_type, src_id, date_id). Manbada hujjat sanasi o'zgarsa, loader eski sanadagi
nusxani (src_system, doc_type, src_id) indeksi bo'yicha oldindan o'chiradi (app/etl/merge.py).
"""

from dataclasses import dataclass, field

from alembic import op

revision = "0006"
down_revision = "0005"
branch_labels = None
depends_on = None

MONEY = "numeric(20,2)"
VOLUME = "numeric(20,3)"
FK_TARGETS = {
    "branch_id": "dim_branch", "item_id": "dim_item", "cp_id": "dim_counterparty",
    "contract_id": "dim_contract", "bank_id": "dim_bank", "nom_id": "dim_nomenclature",
    "object_id": "dim_object", "loan_id": "dim_loan", "pos_id": "dim_position",
    "source_id": "dim_source",
}
PO_STATUSES = (
    "'черновик', 'на согласовании', 'утверждено', 'отправлено в банк', 'исполнено', 'отклонено'"
)


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class Fact:
    name: str
    fks: dict[str, bool]  # ustun -> NOT NULL
    measures: tuple[str, ...]
    partition: str | None = None  # 'month' | 'year'
    brin: bool = False
    partial: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = field(default=())  # (ustunlar, WHERE)


FACTS = (
    Fact("fact_cashflow",
         {"branch_id": True, "item_id": True, "cp_id": False, "contract_id": False,
          "bank_id": False},
         ("direction varchar(3) NOT NULL CHECK (direction IN ('in', 'out'))",
          f"amount {MONEY} NOT NULL CHECK (amount >= 0)",
          "gl_account varchar(8)"),
         partition="month", brin=True),
    Fact("fact_balance", {"branch_id": True, "bank_id": True},
         (f"balance {MONEY} NOT NULL",), partition="year"),
    Fact("fact_payment_plan", {"branch_id": True, "item_id": True},
         (f"amount {MONEY} NOT NULL CHECK (amount >= 0)",)),
    Fact("fact_payment_order",
         {"branch_id": True, "item_id": True, "cp_id": False, "bank_id": False},
         (f"amount {MONEY} NOT NULL CHECK (amount >= 0)",
          f"status varchar(24) NOT NULL CHECK (status IN ({PO_STATUSES}))",
          "status_date_id integer"),
         partition="month",
         partial=(("branch_id, date_id", "status IN ('на согласовании', 'утверждено')"),)),
    Fact("fact_debt", {"branch_id": True, "contract_id": True, "cp_id": False},
         (f"receivable {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0", f"payable {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
          f"overdue {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
          "overdue_days integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0 CHECK (overdue_days >= 0)"),
         partition="year", partial=(("branch_id", "overdue_days > 0"),)),
    Fact("fact_generation", {"branch_id": True},
         (f"generation {VOLUME} NOT NULL CHECK (generation >= 0)",
          f"own_needs {VOLUME} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0", f"supply {VOLUME} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
          f"plan {VOLUME}", "work_hours numeric(6,2)"),
         partition="year"),
    Fact("fact_energy_sales", {"branch_id": True, "cp_id": True},
         (f"accrued {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0", f"paid {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
          f"volume {VOLUME} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")),
    Fact("fact_procurement", {"branch_id": True, "nom_id": False, "cp_id": False},
         ("lot_no varchar(64) NOT NULL", "method varchar(32) NOT NULL",
          "status varchar(32) NOT NULL", f"start_price {MONEY} NOT NULL",
          f"contract_price {MONEY}", "procedure_days integer"),
         partial=(("branch_id", "status IN ('объявлен', 'на оценке')"),)),
    Fact("fact_stock", {"branch_id": True, "nom_id": True},
         ("warehouse varchar(100) NOT NULL", f"qty {VOLUME} NOT NULL",
          f"value {MONEY} NOT NULL", f"min_qty {VOLUME}", "last_move_date_id integer"),
         partition="year"),
    Fact("fact_stock_move", {"branch_id": True, "nom_id": True},
         ("direction varchar(3) NOT NULL CHECK (direction IN ('in', 'out'))",
          f"qty {VOLUME} NOT NULL", f"value {MONEY} NOT NULL"),
         partition="year", brin=True),
    Fact("fact_investment", {"branch_id": True, "object_id": True, "source_id": False},
         (f"plan {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0", f"fact {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
          "physical_pct numeric(7,4)", f"advance {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
          "advance_date_id integer")),
    # Kredit grafigi summalari kredit valyutasida (mln); so'mga fx_rate bilan o'giriladi.
    Fact("fact_loan_schedule", {"branch_id": True, "loan_id": True},
         (f"principal {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0", f"interest {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
          f"balance {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")),
    Fact("fact_hr", {"branch_id": True, "pos_id": True},
         ("headcount_plan numeric(10,1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
          "headcount_fact numeric(10,1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
          f"payroll {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0", "hired integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
          "fired integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0",
          "overtime_hours numeric(12,1) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")),
    Fact("fact_target_funds", {"branch_id": True, "source_id": True, "object_id": False},
         (f"allocated {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0", f"used {MONEY} NOT NULL DEFAULT 0")),
)


def _create_statements(f: Fact) -> list[str]:
    cols = ["date_id integer NOT NULL REFERENCES dwh.dim_date (date_id) "
            "DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED"]
    for col, required in f.fks.items():
        null = " NOT NULL" if required else ""
        cols.append(f"{col} integer{null} REFERENCES dwh.{FK_TARGETS[col]} ({col}) "
                    "DEFERRABLE INITIALLY DEFERRED")
    cols += list(f.measures)
    cols += [
        "load_id bigint NOT NULL",
        "src_system varchar(32) NOT NULL",
        "doc_type varchar(32) NOT NULL",
        "src_id varchar(128) NOT NULL",
        "updated_at timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()",
    ]
    key = "src_system, doc_type, src_id" + (", date_id" if f.partition else "")
    cols.append(f"CONSTRAINT {f.name}_src_uq UNIQUE ({key})")
    part = " PARTITION BY RANGE (date_id)" if f.partition else ""
    out = [f"CREATE TABLE dwh.{f.name} (\n  " + ",\n  ".join(cols) + f"\n){part}"]

    # TZ majburiy indekslari + har FK ustunida B-tree (spec 4.5).
    indexes: list[tuple[str, str | None]] = [("date_id, branch_id", None)]
    if "item_id" in f.fks:
        indexes += [("branch_id, item_id", None), ("item_id", None)]
    else:
        indexes.append(("branch_id", None))
    indexes += [(col, None) for col in f.fks if col not in ("branch_id", "item_id")]
    if f.partition:
        indexes.append(("src_system, doc_type, src_id", None))
    indexes += list(f.partial)
    for columns, where in indexes:
        suffix = "_p" if where else ""
        name = f"{f.name}_{columns.replace(', ', '_')}{suffix}_idx"
        where_sql = f" WHERE {where}" if where else ""
        out.append(f"CREATE INDEX {name} ON dwh.{f.name} ({columns}){where_sql}")
    if f.brin:
        out.append(f"CREATE INDEX {f.name}_date_brin ON dwh.{f.name} USING brin (date_id)")

    out += [
        f"ALTER TABLE dwh.{f.name} ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY",
        f"ALTER TABLE dwh.{f.name} FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY",
        f"CREATE POLICY branch_scope ON dwh.{f.name} USING (core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id)) "
        "WITH CHECK (core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id))",
        f"GRANT SELECT, INSERT, UPDATE, DELETE ON dwh.{f.name} TO ugk_etl",
    ]
    return out


ENSURE_PARTITIONS = """
CREATE FUNCTION dwh.ensure_partitions(p_table text, p_from date, p_to date) RETURNS integer
LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = pg_catalog, dwh AS $$
DECLARE
  grain text;
  cur date;
  nxt date;
  part text;
  created integer := 0;
BEGIN
  grain := CASE p_table
    WHEN 'fact_cashflow' THEN 'month' WHEN 'fact_payment_order' THEN 'month'
    WHEN 'fact_balance' THEN 'year' WHEN 'fact_debt' THEN 'year'
    WHEN 'fact_generation' THEN 'year' WHEN 'fact_stock' THEN 'year'
    WHEN 'fact_stock_move' THEN 'year' END;
  IF grain IS NULL THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'partitsiyalanmagan jadval: %', p_table;
  END IF;
  -- Worker va ETL bir vaqtda bir xil partitsiyani yaratmasin.
  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('ugk_partitions'));
  cur := date_trunc(grain, p_from)::date;
  WHILE cur <= p_to LOOP
    nxt := (cur + CASE grain WHEN 'month' THEN interval '1 month'
                             ELSE interval '1 year' END)::date;
    part := p_table || '_p' || to_char(cur, CASE grain WHEN 'month' THEN 'YYYYMM'
                                                       ELSE 'YYYY' END);
    IF to_regclass('dwh.' || part) IS NULL THEN
      EXECUTE format('CREATE TABLE dwh.%I PARTITION OF dwh.%I FOR VALUES FROM (%s) TO (%s)',
                     part, p_table, to_char(cur, 'YYYYMMDD'), to_char(nxt, 'YYYYMMDD'));
      created := created + 1;
    END IF;
    cur := nxt;
  END LOOP;
  RETURN created;
END
$$
"""

DROP_OLD_PARTITIONS = """
CREATE FUNCTION dwh.drop_old_partitions(p_keep_years integer) RETURNS integer
LANGUAGE plpgsql SECURITY DEFINER SET search_path = pg_catalog, dwh AS $$
DECLARE
  cutoff integer;
  dropped integer := 0;
  r record;
  suffix text;
  upper_bound integer;
BEGIN
  IF p_keep_years < 1 THEN
    RAISE EXCEPTION 'p_keep_years kamida 1 bo''lishi kerak';
  END IF;
  PERFORM pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('ugk_partitions'));
  cutoff := to_char(date_trunc('month', now() AT TIME ZONE 'UTC')
                    - make_interval(years => p_keep_years), 'YYYYMMDD')::integer;
  FOR r IN
    SELECT c.relname AS part, p.relname AS parent
    FROM pg_inherits i
    JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = i.inhrelid
    JOIN pg_class p ON p.oid = i.inhparent
    JOIN pg_namespace n ON n.oid = p.relnamespace
    WHERE n.nspname = 'dwh' AND c.relname ~ '^fact_[a-z_]+_p[0-9]{4}([0-9]{2})?$'
  LOOP
    suffix := substring(r.part FROM '_p([0-9]+)$');
    IF length(suffix) = 6 THEN
      upper_bound := to_char(to_date(suffix, 'YYYYMM') + interval '1 month', 'YYYYMMDD')::integer;
    ELSE
      upper_bound := to_char(to_date(suffix, 'YYYY') + interval '1 year', 'YYYYMMDD')::integer;
    END IF;
    IF upper_bound <= cutoff THEN
      EXECUTE format('ALTER TABLE dwh.%I DETACH PARTITION dwh.%I', r.parent, r.part);
      EXECUTE format('DROP TABLE dwh.%I', r.part);
      dropped := dropped + 1;
    END IF;
  END LOOP;
  RETURN dropped;
END
$$
"""


def upgrade() -> None:
    for fact in FACTS:
        for statement in _create_statements(fact):
            op.execute(statement)
    op.execute(ENSURE_PARTITIONS)
    op.execute(DROP_OLD_PARTITIONS)
    for fn in ("ensure_partitions(text, date, date)", "drop_old_partitions(integer)"):
        op.execute(f"REVOKE ALL ON FUNCTION dwh.{fn} FROM PUBLIC")
        op.execute(f"GRANT EXECUTE ON FUNCTION dwh.{fn} TO ugk_etl")
    for fact in FACTS:
        if fact.partition:
            op.execute(
                f"SELECT dwh.ensure_partitions('{fact.name}', date_trunc('month', now())::date, "
                "(now() + interval '3 months')::date)"
            )


def downgrade() -> None:
    op.execute("DROP FUNCTION dwh.drop_old_partitions(integer)")
    op.execute("DROP FUNCTION dwh.ensure_partitions(text, date, date)")
    for fact in reversed(FACTS):
        op.execute(f"DROP TABLE dwh.{fact.name}")
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — partitsiya yordamchilari**

`backend/app/dwh/partitions.py`:
```python
"""Fakt partitsiyalari (spec 4.6).

DDL SECURITY DEFINER funksiyalar orqali — ugk_etl jadval egasi emas.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from app.dwh.dates import add_months, month_start

PARTITIONED: dict[str, str] = {
    "fact_cashflow": "month",
    "fact_payment_order": "month",
    "fact_balance": "year",
    "fact_debt": "year",
    "fact_generation": "year",
    "fact_stock": "year",
    "fact_stock_move": "year",
}


def ensure_partitions(conn: Connection, table: str, date_from: date, date_to: date) -> int:
    if table not in PARTITIONED:
        raise ValueError(f"partitsiyalanmagan jadval: {table}")
    return int(
        conn.execute(
            text("SELECT dwh.ensure_partitions(:t, :f, :to)"),
            {"t": table, "f": date_from, "to": date_to},
        ).scalar_one()
    )


def ensure_ahead(conn: Connection, today: date, months_ahead: int) -> int:
    """Joriy oydan boshlab `months_ahead` oy oldinga partitsiyalar."""
    end = add_months(month_start(today), months_ahead)
    return sum(ensure_partitions(conn, t, month_start(today), end) for t in PARTITIONED)


def drop_old(conn: Connection, keep_years: int) -> int:
    return int(
        conn.execute(text("SELECT dwh.drop_old_partitions(:k)"), {"k": keep_years}).scalar_one()
    )


def list_partitions(conn: Connection, table: str) -> list[str]:
    return list(
        conn.execute(
            text(
                "SELECT c.relname FROM pg_inherits i JOIN pg_class c ON c.oid = i.inhrelid "
                "WHERE i.inhparent = to_regclass('dwh.' || :t) ORDER BY c.relname"
            ),
            {"t": table},
        ).scalars()
    )
```

### Task 4: ETL jurnali, buildlar, `stg`, sozlamalar, 20 qoida va 4 ssenariy seed'i

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/migrations/versions/0007_etl_core.py`, `backend/app/dwh/reference.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/bootstrap.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_reference.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `get_engine`, `new_session`, `etl_transaction()`, APScheduler `CronTrigger`.
- Produces:
  - `core.builds (build_id bigserial, created_at, status ∈ building|ready|published|superseded|failed, load_id, published_at)`; `core.data_version (id=1, active_build_id → core.builds, published_at)`.
  - `core.etl_run (id, source, mode ∈ full|incremental, started_at, finished_at, status ∈ running|success|failed|failed_quality, window_from, window_to, watermark, rows_in, rows_upserted, build_id, triggered_by, error)`; `core.etl_step (id, run_id, name ∈ extract|dims|facts|aggregates|quality|publish, started_at, finished_at, status ∈ running|success|failed, rows, detail jsonb)`; `core.etl_error (id, run_id, step, severity ∈ warning|error, target, src_id, code, message, created_at)`; `core.etl_requests (id, source, mode, requested_by, requested_at, status ∈ pending|running|done|failed, run_id, error)`.
  - Versiyali jadvallar (har o'zgarish — yangi `version` qatori, eskisi `is_current = false`): `core.settings (key, version, value jsonb, is_current, updated_by, updated_at)`, `core.alert_rules (code, version, title, section, page, threshold jsonb, level ∈ critical|warning, enabled, is_current, updated_by, updated_at)`, `core.fin_assumptions (scenario ∈ base|water|tariff|fx, year, key, version, value numeric, is_current, updated_by, updated_at)`.
  - `stg.batch (load_id, target, source, received_at, row_count)`, `stg.rows (seq bigserial, load_id, target, payload jsonb)` — UNLOGGED.
  - `app.dwh.reference`: `DEFAULT_SETTINGS: dict[str, Any]`, `SETTING_TYPES: dict[str, TypeAdapter[Any]]`, `validate_setting(key, value) -> Any` (noma'lum → `KeyError`, noto'g'ri → `ValueError`), `AlertRuleSeed(code, title, section, page, level, threshold)`, `ALERT_RULES` (20 ta), `SCENARIOS`, `SCENARIO_TITLES`, `FIN_YEARS`, `FIN_KEYS`, `fin_assumption_seeds() -> list[tuple[str, int, str, Decimal]]`, `ensure_reference(conn) -> None` (idempotent), `current_setting(conn, key) -> Any`.
  - `bootstrap_runtime()` endi `ensure_reference` ni ham chaqiradi.

- [ ] **Step 1: Testlarni yozish**

`backend/tests/test_reference.py`:
```python
from decimal import Decimal

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.exc import ProgrammingError

from app.core.db import get_engine, new_session
from app.dwh.reference import (
    ALERT_RULES,
    DEFAULT_SETTINGS,
    current_setting,
    ensure_reference,
    validate_setting,
)
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction


def _rules() -> dict[str, dict[str, object]]:
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT code, level, threshold, section, enabled FROM core.alert_rules "
                 "WHERE is_current")
        ).mappings().all()
    return {r["code"]: dict(r) for r in rows}


def test_twenty_alert_rules_with_tz_thresholds() -> None:
    rules = _rules()
    assert len(rules) == 20 == len(ALERT_RULES)
    assert sum(1 for r in rules.values() if r["level"] == "critical") == 5
    assert rules["cash_below_monthly_outflow"]["threshold"] == {
        "metric": "balance_to_avg_monthly_outflow", "op": "<", "value": 1.0}
    assert rules["payment_plan_execution"]["threshold"]["value"] == 0.9
    assert rules["contracts_overdue_90d"]["threshold"]["value"] == 90
    assert rules["contracts_overdue_90d"]["level"] == "critical"
    assert rules["procurement_overdue_lots"]["threshold"] == {
        "metric": "overdue_lots_count", "op": ">", "value": 3}
    assert rules["stock_below_minimum"]["level"] == "critical"
    assert rules["generation_plan"]["threshold"]["per"] == "station"
    assert rules["overtime"]["threshold"]["value"] == 20
    assert all(r["enabled"] for r in rules.values())


def test_fin_assumptions_four_scenarios() -> None:
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT scenario, year, key, value FROM core.fin_assumptions WHERE is_current")
        ).all()
    values = {(s, y, k): v for s, y, k, v in rows}
    assert len(values) == 4 * 7 * 6
    assert {s for s, _, _ in values} == {"base", "water", "tariff", "fx"}
    assert values[("base", 2027, "tariff_growth")] == Decimal("0.08")
    assert values[("tariff", 2027, "tariff_growth")] == Decimal("0.04")
    assert values[("water", 2028, "generation_factor")] == Decimal("0.85")
    assert values[("water", 2026, "generation_factor")] == Decimal("1")
    assert values[("water", 2030, "generation_factor")] == Decimal("1")
    assert values[("fx", 2032, "fx_rate_factor")] == Decimal("1.2")


def test_settings_defaults_and_validation() -> None:
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        assert current_setting(conn, "freshness.max_age_hours") == 6
        assert current_setting(conn, "builds.keep") == 3
        assert current_setting(conn, "etl.history_start") == "2024-01-01"
    assert validate_setting("etl.synthetic_cron", "30 1 * * *") == "30 1 * * *"
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_setting("etl.synthetic_cron", "every night")
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_setting("freshness.max_age_hours", 0)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError):
        validate_setting("freshness.max_age_hours", True)
    with pytest.raises(KeyError):
        validate_setting("no.such", 1)
    assert set(DEFAULT_SETTINGS) == {
        "freshness.max_age_hours", "etl.history_start", "etl.synthetic_cron", "builds.keep",
        "retention.fact_years", "retention.audit_months", "partitions.months_ahead",
    }


def test_ensure_reference_is_idempotent_and_restores() -> None:
    with get_engine("migrator").begin() as conn:
        conn.execute(text("DELETE FROM core.alert_rules WHERE code = 'overtime'"))
    with get_engine("app").begin() as conn:
        ensure_reference(conn)
        ensure_reference(conn)
    rules = _rules()
    assert len(rules) == 20
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        dv = conn.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM core.data_version WHERE id = 1")).scalar_one()
    assert dv == 1


def test_app_role_cannot_write_etl_journal() -> None:
    with new_session() as s, pytest.raises(ProgrammingError, match="permission denied"):
        s.execute(text("INSERT INTO core.etl_run (source, mode, status) "
                       "VALUES ('x', 'full', 'running')"))


def test_etl_role_can_update_data_version_but_not_settings() -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.data_version SET published_at = published_at WHERE id = 1"))
    with pytest.raises(ProgrammingError, match="permission denied"), etl_transaction() as conn:
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.settings SET is_current = is_current"))


def test_staging_rows_table_is_unlogged() -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        persistence = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT relpersistence FROM pg_class WHERE oid = 'stg.rows'::regclass")
        ).scalar_one()
    assert persistence == "u"
```

```python
def upgrade() -> None:
    for statement in STATEMENTS:
        op.execute(statement)


def downgrade() -> None:
    for table in ("stg.rows", "stg.batch", "core.fin_assumptions", "core.alert_rules",
                  "core.settings", "core.etl_requests", "core.etl_error", "core.etl_step",
                  "core.etl_run", "core.data_version", "core.builds"):
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — seed'lar va bootstrap**

`backend/app/dwh/reference.py`:
```python
"""Tizim sozlamalari, «Требует внимания» qoidalari va финмодель допущения (spec 4.8, TZ 4).

Seed idempotent: faqat yo'q kalitlar qo'shiladi, administrator o'zgartirgan qiymatlarga
tegilmaydi. Chegaralar va допущения — TZ ochiq savoli: mijoz tasdiqlagach admin panelda
yangilanadi (har o'zgarish yangi versiya + audit).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Annotated, Any

from apscheduler.triggers.cron import CronTrigger
from pydantic import AfterValidator, Field, TypeAdapter
from pydantic import ValidationError as PydanticValidationError
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection


def _cron(value: str) -> str:
    CronTrigger.from_crontab(value)  # noto'g'ri bo'lsa ValueError
    return value


def _iso_date(value: str) -> str:
    return date.fromisoformat(value).isoformat()


DEFAULT_SETTINGS: dict[str, Any] = {
    "freshness.max_age_hours": 6,
    "etl.history_start": "2024-01-01",
    "etl.synthetic_cron": "0 2 * * *",
    "builds.keep": 3,
    "retention.fact_years": 5,
    "retention.audit_months": 12,
    "partitions.months_ahead": 3,
}

SETTING_TYPES: dict[str, TypeAdapter[Any]] = {
    "freshness.max_age_hours": TypeAdapter(Annotated[int, Field(strict=True, ge=1, le=168)]),
    "etl.history_start": TypeAdapter(Annotated[str, AfterValidator(_iso_date)]),
    "etl.synthetic_cron": TypeAdapter(Annotated[str, AfterValidator(_cron)]),
    "builds.keep": TypeAdapter(Annotated[int, Field(strict=True, ge=2, le=10)]),
    "retention.fact_years": TypeAdapter(Annotated[int, Field(strict=True, ge=1, le=10)]),
    "retention.audit_months": TypeAdapter(Annotated[int, Field(strict=True, ge=12, le=120)]),
    "partitions.months_ahead": TypeAdapter(Annotated[int, Field(strict=True, ge=1, le=12)]),
}


def validate_setting(key: str, value: Any) -> Any:
    adapter = SETTING_TYPES[key]  # noma'lum kalit → KeyError
    try:
        return adapter.validate_python(value)
    except PydanticValidationError as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{key}: noto'g'ri qiymat") from exc


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class AlertRuleSeed:
    code: str
    title: str
    section: str
    page: str
    level: str
    threshold: dict[str, Any]


def _t(metric: str, op: str, value: float | int, **extra: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"metric": metric, "op": op, "value": value, **extra}


# TZ 4-bo'lim, «Панель „Требует внимания“» jadvali — 20 qoida, aynan shu chegaralar.
ALERT_RULES: tuple[AlertRuleSeed, ...] = (
    AlertRuleSeed("cash_below_monthly_outflow", "Остаток ниже месячного расходования", "kazna",
                  "svod", "critical", _t("balance_to_avg_monthly_outflow", "<", 1.0)),
    AlertRuleSeed("payments_14d_exceed_balance", "Платежи 14 дней превышают остаток", "kazna",
                  "calendar", "critical", _t("calendar_14d_to_balance", ">", 1.0)),
    AlertRuleSeed("payment_plan_execution", "Исполнение плана платежей", "kazna", "platezhi",
                  "warning", _t("payment_plan_execution", "<", 0.9)),
    AlertRuleSeed("contracts_overdue_90d", "Просроченные договоры", "kazna", "dogovory",
                  "critical", _t("contract_overdue_days", ">", 90)),
    AlertRuleSeed("overdue_payables_share", "Просроченная кредиторская задолженность", "kazna",
                  "dogovory", "warning", _t("overdue_payables_share", ">", 0.2)),
    AlertRuleSeed("loan_payments_30d", "Платежи по кредитам", "kredity", "grafik", "warning",
                  _t("loan_payment_days_ahead", "<=", 30)),
    AlertRuleSeed("fx_loans_share", "Доля валютных кредитов", "kredity", "svod", "warning",
                  _t("fx_loans_share", ">", 0.85)),
    AlertRuleSeed("procurement_overdue_lots", "Просроченные лоты закупок", "zakupki", "loty",
                  "critical", _t("overdue_lots_count", ">", 3)),
    AlertRuleSeed("direct_contracts_share", "Доля прямых договоров", "zakupki", "postavshiki",
                  "warning", _t("direct_contracts_share", ">", 0.25)),
    AlertRuleSeed("procurement_plan", "План закупок", "zakupki", "svod", "warning",
                  _t("procurement_plan_execution", "<", 0.6)),
    AlertRuleSeed("stock_below_minimum", "Позиции ниже минимума", "sklad", "minimum",
                  "critical", _t("below_min_positions", ">", 5)),
    AlertRuleSeed("illiquid_stock_share", "Неликвиды", "sklad", "nelikvid", "warning",
                  _t("illiquid_share", ">", 0.2)),
    AlertRuleSeed("generation_plan", "Выполнение плана выработки", "proizvodstvo", "stancii",
                  "warning", _t("generation_plan_execution", "<", 0.95, per="station")),
    AlertRuleSeed("energy_payment_level", "Уровень оплаты за энергию", "proizvodstvo",
                  "realiz", "warning", _t("energy_payment_level", "<", 0.85)),
    AlertRuleSeed("investment_schedule", "Адресная программа", "investicii", "svod", "warning",
                  _t("investment_schedule_execution", "<", 0.9)),
    AlertRuleSeed("fin_phys_gap", "Разрыв финансового и физического выполнения", "investicii",
                  "riski", "warning", _t("fin_phys_gap_pp", ">", 20)),
    AlertRuleSeed("advances_90d", "Незакрытые авансы", "investicii", "riski", "warning",
                  _t("advance_age_days", ">", 90)),
    AlertRuleSeed("vacancies_share", "Вакансии", "kadry", "chislennost", "warning",
                  _t("vacancy_share", ">", 0.1)),
    AlertRuleSeed("turnover", "Текучесть", "kadry", "dvizhenie", "warning",
                  _t("turnover_rate", ">", 0.08)),
    AlertRuleSeed("overtime", "Сверхурочные", "kadry", "dvizhenie", "warning",
                  _t("overtime_hours_per_employee", ">", 20)),

SCENARIOS = ("base", "water", "tariff", "fx")
SCENARIO_TITLES = {
    "base": "Базовый",
    "water": "Маловодье",
    "tariff": "Сдержанный тариф",
    "fx": "Ослабление сума",
}
FIN_YEARS = range(2026, 2033)
FIN_KEYS = ("tariff_growth", "generation_factor", "fx_rate_factor", "opex_index", "tax_rate",
            "kium")
# Bazaviy допущения — vaqtinchalik (TZ ochiq savoli), mijoz tasdiqlagach yangilanadi.
_BASE = {
    "tariff_growth": Decimal("0.08"),
    "generation_factor": Decimal("1"),
    "fx_rate_factor": Decimal("1"),
    "opex_index": Decimal("0.07"),
    "tax_rate": Decimal("0.15"),
    "kium": Decimal("0.40"),
}


def fin_assumption_seeds() -> list[tuple[str, int, str, Decimal]]:
    rows: list[tuple[str, int, str, Decimal]] = []
    for scenario in SCENARIOS:
        for year in FIN_YEARS:
            for key in FIN_KEYS:
                value = _BASE[key]
                if scenario == "water" and key == "generation_factor" and 2027 <= year <= 2029:
                    value = Decimal("0.85")  # маловодье: выработка −15% в 2027–2029
                if scenario == "tariff" and key == "tariff_growth":
                    value = Decimal("0.04")  # рост тарифа 4% вместо 8%
                if scenario == "fx" and key == "fx_rate_factor":
                    value = Decimal("1.2")  # курс +20%
                rows.append((scenario, year, key, value))
    return rows


def ensure_reference(conn: Connection) -> None:
    """Bootstrap'da chaqiriladi (ugk_app). Faqat yo'q yozuvlarni qo'shadi."""
    conn.execute(text("SELECT pg_advisory_xact_lock(hashtext('ugk_reference'))"))
    conn.execute(text("INSERT INTO core.data_version (id) VALUES (1) ON CONFLICT DO NOTHING"))
    for key, value in DEFAULT_SETTINGS.items():
        conn.execute(
            text(
                "INSERT INTO core.settings (key, version, value) "
                "SELECT :k, 1, CAST(:v AS jsonb) "
                "WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM core.settings WHERE key = :k)"
            ),
            {"k": key, "v": json.dumps(value)},
        )
    for rule in ALERT_RULES:
        conn.execute(
            text(
                "INSERT INTO core.alert_rules (code, version, title, section, page, threshold, "
                "level) SELECT :c, 1, :t, :s, :p, CAST(:th AS jsonb), :l "
                "WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM core.alert_rules WHERE code = :c)"
            ),
            {"c": rule.code, "t": rule.title, "s": rule.section, "p": rule.page,
             "th": json.dumps(rule.threshold), "l": rule.level},
        )
    conn.execute(
        text(
            "INSERT INTO core.fin_assumptions (scenario, year, key, version, value) "
            "SELECT :s, :y, :k, 1, :v WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM core.fin_assumptions "
            "WHERE scenario = :s AND year = :y AND key = :k)"
        ),
        [{"s": s, "y": y, "k": k, "v": v} for s, y, k, v in fin_assumption_seeds()],
    )


def current_setting(conn: Connection, key: str) -> Any:
    if key not in DEFAULT_SETTINGS:
        raise KeyError(key)
    value = conn.execute(
        text("SELECT value FROM core.settings WHERE key = :k AND is_current"), {"k": key}
    ).scalar_one_or_none()
    return DEFAULT_SETTINGS[key] if value is None else value
```
