### Task 5: Konnektor tiplari, staging maqsadlari reyestri, `stg → dwh` merge

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/etl/types.py`, `backend/app/dwh/targets.py`, `backend/app/etl/staging.py`, `backend/app/etl/merge.py`, `backend/tests/etl_helpers.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_etl_merge.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `etl_transaction()`, `app.dwh.dates`, `app.dwh.partitions.ensure_partitions`, `keystore.encrypt_str`, `blind_index`, `get_settings`, `Purpose`.
- Produces:
  - `app.etl.types`: `Mode` (StrEnum `FULL="full"`, `INCREMENTAL="incremental"`), `Window(date_from, date_to)` + `.from_id`, `.to_id`, `.contains(d)`, `.contains_id(date_id)`, `.month_ids() -> list[int]`; `Watermark(value: datetime | None)`; `HealthResult(ok: bool, detail: str = "")`; `ExtractStats(rows: int, by_target: Mapping[str, int])`; `ControlTotals(cashflow_by_month: Mapping[int, Decimal])`; `StagingSink` (Protocol: `add(target: str, row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None`); `SourceConnector` (Protocol — spec 5.2 aynan: `source: str`, `healthcheck() -> HealthResult`, `watermark() -> Watermark`, `extract(window, sink) -> ExtractStats`, `control_totals(window) -> ControlTotals | None`); `ConnectorUnavailableError`.
  - `app.dwh.targets`: `OTHER_IN_ITEM = "PKM54-I06"`, `OTHER_OUT_ITEM = "PKM54-O09"`, `Lookup(column, dim, nullable=False, fallback_sql=None)` + `.key` (`"item_id" → "item_code"`), `.error_code` (`missing_branch|missing_item|missing_dim`), `.severity`; `Scd(table, key, column)`; `Sensitive(field, enc_column, bidx_column, purpose, index_label)`; `Target(name, kind: "dim"|"fact", columns, conflict, lookups=(), partition=None, windowed=False, scd=None, sensitive=())`; `TARGETS: dict[str, Target]` (tartib: avval o'lchovlar bog'liqlik tartibida, keyin `fx_rate` va 14 fakt); `DIM_TARGETS`, `FACT_TARGETS` (tuple[str, ...]).
  - `app.etl.staging`: `DbStagingSink(conn, load_id, source, *, batch_size=2000)` + `.add(target, row)`, `.flush()`, `.close() -> dict[str, int]`, `.counts`; `protect(target, payload) -> dict` (INN/hisob → `*_enc` + `*_bidx`, ochiq qiymat staging'ga tushmaydi); `clear_load(conn, load_id) -> None`.
  - `app.etl.merge`: `MergeStats(target, upserted=0, deleted=0, errors=0, warnings=0)`, `ERROR_LIMIT = 1000`, `upsert_sql(name) -> str`, `staged_targets(conn, load_id) -> set[str]`, `merge_target(conn, name, *, load_id, source, step, as_of, window=None, replace=False) -> MergeStats`, `merge_dims(conn, *, load_id, source, as_of) -> list[MergeStats]`, `merge_facts(conn, *, load_id, source, window, complete, as_of) -> list[MergeStats]`.
  - `tests.etl_helpers`: `TEST_AS_OF`, `new_source() -> str`, `new_tag() -> str`, `open_run(source) -> int`, `stage(load_id, source, rows) -> dict[str, int]`, `merge_all(load_id, source, window, *, complete=(), as_of=TEST_AS_OF) -> list[MergeStats]`, `purge_source(source) -> None`, `branch_row(tag, **overrides) -> dict`, `cash_row(src_id, date_id, branch_code, amount, *, direction="out", item_code=OTHER_OUT_ITEM, **extra) -> dict`, `FULL_WINDOW`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Test yordamchilari**

`backend/tests/etl_helpers.py`:
```python
"""ETL testlari uchun yordamchilar: har test o'z `src_system` manbasi va test filiali bilan
ishlaydi va oxirida o'zi yaratgan qatorlarni o'chiradi (sintetik ma'lumotga tegmaydi)."""

from __future__ import annotations

import uuid
from collections.abc import Collection, Mapping, Sequence
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import text

from app.dwh.targets import OTHER_OUT_ITEM
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.etl.merge import MergeStats, merge_dims, merge_facts
from app.etl.staging import DbStagingSink, clear_load
from app.etl.types import Window

TEST_AS_OF = date(2026, 9, 24)
FULL_WINDOW = Window(date(2024, 1, 1), TEST_AS_OF)

# O'chirish tartibi: faktlar → bog'liq o'lchovlar → asosiy o'lchovlar.
_PURGE_ORDER = (
    "fact_cashflow", "fact_balance", "fact_payment_plan", "fact_payment_order", "fact_debt",
    "fact_generation", "fact_energy_sales", "fact_procurement", "fact_stock", "fact_stock_move",
    "fact_investment", "fact_loan_schedule", "fact_hr", "fact_target_funds", "fx_rate",
    "dim_contract", "dim_loan", "dim_object", "dim_branch", "dim_item", "dim_counterparty",
    "dim_bank", "dim_nomenclature", "dim_source", "dim_position",
)


def new_source() -> str:
    return f"t{uuid.uuid4().hex[:10]}"


def new_tag() -> str:
    return uuid.uuid4().hex[:6].upper()


def open_run(source: str) -> int:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        return int(conn.execute(
            text("INSERT INTO core.etl_run (source, mode, status) "
                 "VALUES (:s, 'full', 'running') RETURNING id"), {"s": source}).scalar_one())


def stage(
    load_id: int, source: str, rows: Mapping[str, Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]]
) -> dict[str, int]:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        clear_load(conn, load_id)
        sink = DbStagingSink(conn, load_id, source)
        for target, items in rows.items():
            for row in items:
                sink.add(target, row)
        return sink.close()


def merge_all(load_id: int, source: str, window: Window = FULL_WINDOW, *,
              complete: Collection[str] = (), as_of: date = TEST_AS_OF) -> list[MergeStats]:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        stats = merge_dims(conn, load_id=load_id, source=source, as_of=as_of)
        stats += merge_facts(conn, load_id=load_id, source=source, window=window,
                             complete=complete, as_of=as_of)
    return stats


def purge_source(source: str) -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        conn.execute(text(
            "DELETE FROM dwh.dim_branch_version WHERE branch_id IN "
            "(SELECT branch_id FROM dwh.dim_branch WHERE src_system = :s)"), {"s": source})
        conn.execute(text(
            "DELETE FROM dwh.dim_loan_version WHERE loan_id IN "
            "(SELECT loan_id FROM dwh.dim_loan WHERE src_system = :s)"), {"s": source})
        for table in _PURGE_ORDER:
            conn.execute(text(f"DELETE FROM dwh.{table} WHERE src_system = :s"), {"s": source})
        conn.execute(text(
            "DELETE FROM core.builds b WHERE b.load_id IN "
            "(SELECT id FROM core.etl_run WHERE source = :s) AND b.build_id IS DISTINCT FROM "
            "(SELECT active_build_id FROM core.data_version WHERE id = 1)"), {"s": source})
        conn.execute(text(
            "DELETE FROM stg.rows WHERE load_id IN "
            "(SELECT id FROM core.etl_run WHERE source = :s)"), {"s": source})


def branch_row(tag: str, **overrides: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    row: dict[str, Any] = {
        "code": f"T-{tag}", "name": f"Тестовая ГЭС {tag}", "short_name": f"Т{tag}",
        "inn": "123456789", "kind": "ges", "capacity_mw": 100, "lat": 41.0, "lon": 69.0,
        "sort_order": 99,
    }
    row.update(overrides)
    return row


def cash_row(
    src_id: str, date_id: int, branch_code: str, amount: float, *,
    direction: str = "out", item_code: str = OTHER_OUT_ITEM, **extra: Any,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    row: dict[str, Any] = {
        "doc_type": "bank_entry", "src_id": src_id, "date_id": date_id,
        "branch_code": branch_code, "item_code": item_code, "direction": direction,
        "amount": amount, "gl_account": "5110",
    }
    row.update(extra)
    return row
```

- [ ] **Step 2: Testlarni yozish**

`backend/tests/test_etl_merge.py`:
```python
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core import keystore
from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.crypto import Purpose, blind_index
from app.dwh.targets import OTHER_OUT_ITEM, TARGETS
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.etl.staging import DbStagingSink
from app.etl.types import Window
from tests.etl_helpers import (
    branch_row,
    cash_row,
    merge_all,
    new_source,
    new_tag,
    open_run,
    purge_source,
    stage,
)

AUG = Window(date(2026, 8, 1), date(2026, 8, 31))


@pytest.fixture
def source() -> Iterator[str]:
    name = new_source()
    yield name
    purge_source(name)


def _load(source: str, rows: dict[str, list[dict[str, object]]], **kwargs: Any):
    run_id = open_run(source)
    stage(run_id, source, rows)
    return run_id, {s.target: s for s in merge_all(run_id, source, **kwargs)}


def _cash(source: str) -> list[tuple[str, int, float, str]]:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT f.src_id, f.date_id, f.amount, i.code FROM dwh.fact_cashflow f "
                 "JOIN dwh.dim_item i USING (item_id) WHERE f.src_system = :s ORDER BY 1, 2"),
            {"s": source},
        ).all()
    return [(r[0], r[1], float(r[2]), r[3]) for r in rows]


def _errors(run_id: int) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        return [tuple(r) for r in conn.execute(
            text("SELECT severity, code FROM core.etl_error WHERE run_id = :r ORDER BY id"),
            {"r": run_id})]


def test_targets_match_database_columns() -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        for name, target in TARGETS.items():
            cols = set(conn.execute(
                text("SELECT column_name FROM information_schema.columns "
                     "WHERE table_schema = 'dwh' AND table_name = :t"), {"t": name}).scalars())
            wanted = set(target.columns) | {lk.column for lk in target.lookups}
            wanted |= {"load_id", "src_system", "updated_at"}
            assert wanted <= cols, (name, wanted - cols)


def test_upsert_is_idempotent_and_updates_only_changes(source: str) -> None:
    tag = new_tag()
    branch = branch_row(tag)
    _, stats = _load(source, {"dim_branch": [branch],
                              "fact_cashflow": [cash_row("d1", 20260805, branch["code"], 100)]})
    assert stats["fact_cashflow"].upserted == 1
    _, stats = _load(source, {"fact_cashflow": [cash_row("d1", 20260805, branch["code"], 100)]})
    assert stats["fact_cashflow"].upserted == 0
    _, stats = _load(source, {"fact_cashflow": [cash_row("d1", 20260805, branch["code"], 120)]})
    assert stats["fact_cashflow"].upserted == 1
    assert _cash(source) == [("d1", 20260805, 120.0, OTHER_OUT_ITEM)]


def test_duplicate_rows_in_one_batch_last_wins(source: str) -> None:
    branch = branch_row(new_tag())
    _load(source, {"dim_branch": [branch], "fact_cashflow": [
        cash_row("dup", 20260805, branch["code"], 10),
        cash_row("dup", 20260805, branch["code"], 30),
    ]})
    assert _cash(source) == [("dup", 20260805, 30.0, OTHER_OUT_ITEM)]


def test_moved_document_date_does_not_duplicate(source: str) -> None:
    branch = branch_row(new_tag())
    _load(source, {"dim_branch": [branch],
                   "fact_cashflow": [cash_row("mv", 20260805, branch["code"], 10)]})
    _load(source, {"fact_cashflow": [cash_row("mv", 20260910, branch["code"], 10)]})
    assert _cash(source) == [("mv", 20260910, 10.0, OTHER_OUT_ITEM)]


def test_unknown_item_goes_to_other_with_warning(source: str) -> None:
    branch = branch_row(new_tag())
    run_id, stats = _load(source, {"dim_branch": [branch], "fact_cashflow": [
        cash_row("in1", 20260805, branch["code"], 5, direction="in", item_code="NO-SUCH"),
        cash_row("out1", 20260805, branch["code"], 7, item_code="NO-SUCH"),
    ]})
    assert stats["fact_cashflow"].warnings == 2
    assert _cash(source) == [("in1", 20260805, 5.0, "PKM54-I06"),
                             ("out1", 20260805, 7.0, "PKM54-O09")]
    assert _errors(run_id) == [("warning", "missing_item"), ("warning", "missing_item")]


def test_unknown_branch_is_skipped_with_error(source: str) -> None:
    run_id, stats = _load(source, {"fact_cashflow": [cash_row("x", 20260805, "NO-BRANCH", 5)]})
    assert stats["fact_cashflow"].upserted == 0
    assert stats["fact_cashflow"].errors == 1
    assert _errors(run_id) == [("error", "missing_branch")]


def test_unknown_counterparty_is_missing_dim(source: str) -> None:
    branch = branch_row(new_tag())
    run_id, stats = _load(source, {"dim_branch": [branch], "fact_cashflow": [
        cash_row("c1", 20260805, branch["code"], 5, cp_code="NO-CP"),
        cash_row("c2", 20260805, branch["code"], 5),
    ]})
    assert stats["fact_cashflow"].upserted == 1
    assert _errors(run_id) == [("error", "missing_dim")]


def test_date_outside_calendar_is_missing_dim(source: str) -> None:
    branch = branch_row(new_tag())
    run_id, _ = _load(source, {"dim_branch": [branch],
                               "fact_cashflow": [cash_row("old", 20100105, branch["code"], 5)]})
    assert _cash(source) == []
    assert ("error", "missing_dim") in _errors(run_id)


def test_window_replace_deletes_missing_documents(source: str) -> None:
    branch = branch_row(new_tag())
    _load(source, {"dim_branch": [branch], "fact_cashflow": [
        cash_row("a", 20260805, branch["code"], 1),
        cash_row("b", 20260806, branch["code"], 2),
        cash_row("c", 20260915, branch["code"], 3),
    ]})
    _, stats = _load(source, {"fact_cashflow": [cash_row("a", 20260805, branch["code"], 1)]},
                     window=AUG, complete=["fact_cashflow"])
    assert stats["fact_cashflow"].deleted == 1
    assert [r[0] for r in _cash(source)] == ["a", "c"]  # c oynadan tashqarida — tegilmaydi


def test_counterparty_inn_encrypted_with_blind_index(source: str) -> None:
    code = f"CP-{new_tag()}"
    run_id = open_run(source)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        sink = DbStagingSink(conn, run_id, source)
        sink.add("dim_counterparty", {"code": code, "name": "ООО «Тест»", "kind": "supplier",
                                      "inn": "305 123 456"})
        sink.close()
        staged = conn.execute(text("SELECT payload::text FROM stg.rows WHERE load_id = :l"),
                              {"l": run_id}).scalar_one()
    assert "305 123 456" not in staged and "305123456" not in staged
    merge_all(run_id, source)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        enc, bidx = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT inn_enc, inn_bidx FROM dwh.dim_counterparty WHERE code = :c"),
            {"c": code}).one()
    aad = f"dwh.dim_counterparty.inn_enc:{code}"
    assert keystore.decrypt_str(Purpose.PII, bytes(enc), aad) == "305 123 456"
    expected = blind_index(get_settings().key_bytes("blind_index_key"), "inn", "305123456")
    assert bytes(bidx) == expected


def test_branch_capacity_change_creates_scd_version(source: str) -> None:
    tag = new_tag()
    _load(source, {"dim_branch": [branch_row(tag, capacity_mw=100)]}, as_of=date(2026, 9, 1))
    _load(source, {"dim_branch": [branch_row(tag, capacity_mw=100)]}, as_of=date(2026, 9, 10))
    _load(source, {"dim_branch": [branch_row(tag, capacity_mw=120)]}, as_of=date(2026, 9, 24))
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT v.capacity_mw, v.valid_from, v.valid_to, v.is_current "
                 "FROM dwh.dim_branch_version v JOIN dwh.dim_branch b USING (branch_id) "
                 "WHERE b.code = :c ORDER BY v.valid_from"), {"c": f"T-{tag}"}).all()
    assert [(float(r[0]), r[1], r[2], r[3]) for r in rows] == [
        (100.0, date(2000, 1, 1), date(2026, 9, 23), False),
        (120.0, date(2026, 9, 24), None, True),
    ]


def test_same_day_scd_change_replaces_version(source: str) -> None:
    tag = new_tag()
    _load(source, {"dim_branch": [branch_row(tag, capacity_mw=100)]}, as_of=date(2026, 9, 1))
    _load(source, {"dim_branch": [branch_row(tag, capacity_mw=110)]}, as_of=date(2026, 9, 24))
    _load(source, {"dim_branch": [branch_row(tag, capacity_mw=130)]}, as_of=date(2026, 9, 24))
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT v.capacity_mw, v.is_current FROM dwh.dim_branch_version v "
                 "JOIN dwh.dim_branch b USING (branch_id) WHERE b.code = :c "
                 "ORDER BY v.valid_from"), {"c": f"T-{tag}"}).all()
    assert [(float(r[0]), r[1]) for r in rows] == [(100.0, False), (130.0, True)]


def test_unknown_target_rejected_by_sink(source: str) -> None:
    run_id = open_run(source)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="staging"), etl_transaction() as conn:
        DbStagingSink(conn, run_id, source).add("users", {"x": 1})
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — tiplar va maqsadlar reyestri**

`backend/app/etl/types.py`:
```python
"""Konnektor protokoli va ETL qiymat tiplari (spec 5.2)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from enum import StrEnum
from typing import Any, Protocol

from app.dwh.dates import add_months, date_id, month_id, month_start


class Mode(StrEnum):
    FULL = "full"
    INCREMENTAL = "incremental"


class ConnectorUnavailableError(Exception):
    """Manba vaqtincha ishlamayapti — qayta urinish mumkin."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Window:
    date_from: date
    date_to: date

    @property
    def from_id(self) -> int:
        return date_id(self.date_from)

    @property
    def to_id(self) -> int:
        return date_id(self.date_to)

    def contains(self, d: date) -> bool:
        return self.date_from <= d <= self.date_to

    def contains_id(self, value: int) -> bool:
        return self.from_id <= value <= self.to_id

    def month_ids(self) -> list[int]:
        out: list[int] = []
        cur = month_start(self.date_from)
        while cur <= self.date_to:
            out.append(month_id(cur))
            cur = add_months(cur, 1)
        return out


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Watermark:
    value: datetime | None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class HealthResult:
    ok: bool
    detail: str = ""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ExtractStats:
    rows: int
    by_target: Mapping[str, int]  # to'liq berilgan maqsadlar (0 qatorlilari ham)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ControlTotals:
    cashflow_by_month: Mapping[int, Decimal]  # month_id → 5110/5210/5530 oborotlari


class StagingSink(Protocol):
    def add(self, target: str, row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None: ...


class SourceConnector(Protocol):
    source: str

    def healthcheck(self) -> HealthResult: ...

    def watermark(self) -> Watermark: ...

    def extract(self, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> ExtractStats: ...

    def control_totals(self, window: Window) -> ControlTotals | None: ...
```

`backend/app/dwh/targets.py`:
```python
"""Staging maqsadlari reyestri: har dwh jadvali uchun ustunlar, idempotentlik kaliti,
o'lcham kodlarini id'ga aylantirish (lookup), SCD-2 va shifrlanadigan maydonlar.

Barcha SQL identifikatorlari shu fayldan olinadi (whitelist) — tashqi kiritishdan emas.
Keyingi rejalar yangi fakt qo'shganda faqat shu yerga Target qo'shadi.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Literal

from app.core.crypto import Purpose

# Tizim "Прочие" statyalari (0005 migratsiyasida seed qilingan).
OTHER_IN_ITEM = "PKM54-I06"
OTHER_OUT_ITEM = "PKM54-O09"
_ITEM_BY_DIRECTION = (
    f"CASE WHEN s.payload->>'direction' = 'in' THEN '{OTHER_IN_ITEM}' "
    f"ELSE '{OTHER_OUT_ITEM}' END"
)
_ITEM_OUT = f"'{OTHER_OUT_ITEM}'"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Lookup:
    column: str  # dwh ustuni va o'lcham PK nomi, masalan "item_id"
    dim: str  # "dim_item"
    nullable: bool = False
    fallback_sql: str | None = None  # topilmasa ishlatiladigan KOD ifodasi (statya → «Прочие»)

    @property
    def key(self) -> str:
        return self.column.removesuffix("_id") + "_code"

    @property
    def error_code(self) -> str:
        if self.column == "branch_id":
            return "missing_branch"
        return "missing_item" if self.fallback_sql else "missing_dim"

    @property
    def severity(self) -> str:
        return "warning" if self.fallback_sql else "error"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Scd:
    table: str
    key: str
    column: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Sensitive:
    field: str  # manbadagi ochiq maydon
    enc_column: str
    bidx_column: str
    purpose: Purpose
    index_label: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Target:
    name: str
    kind: Literal["dim", "fact"]
    columns: tuple[str, ...]  # payload'dan to'g'ridan-to'g'ri olinadigan ustunlar
    conflict: tuple[str, ...]
    lookups: tuple[Lookup, ...] = ()
    partition: Literal["month", "year"] | None = None
    windowed: bool = False  # oyna ichida to'liq almashtiriladi
    scd: Scd | None = None
    sensitive: tuple[Sensitive, ...] = ()


BRANCH = Lookup("branch_id", "dim_branch")
_FACT_TECH = ("doc_type", "src_id", "date_id")


def _dim(name: str, columns: tuple[str, ...], **kw: object) -> Target:
    return Target(name, "dim", columns, ("code",), **kw)  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def _fact(name: str, measures: tuple[str, ...], lookups: tuple[Lookup, ...],
          partition: Literal["month", "year"] | None = None) -> Target:
    conflict = ("src_system", "doc_type", "src_id") + (("date_id",) if partition else ())
    return Target(name, "fact", _FACT_TECH + measures, conflict, lookups, partition,
                  windowed=True)


_ALL: tuple[Target, ...] = (
    _dim("dim_branch",
         ("code", "name", "short_name", "inn", "kind", "capacity_mw", "lat", "lon", "sort_order"),
         scd=Scd("dim_branch_version", "branch_id", "capacity_mw")),
    _dim("dim_item", ("code", "name", "activity", "direction", "sort_order")),
    _dim("dim_counterparty", ("code", "name", "kind", "inn_enc", "inn_bidx"),
         sensitive=(Sensitive("inn", "inn_enc", "inn_bidx", Purpose.PII, "inn"),)),
    _dim("dim_bank", ("code", "bank_name", "account_enc", "account_bidx", "currency"),
         sensitive=(Sensitive("account_number", "account_enc", "account_bidx", Purpose.BANK,
                              "account"),)),
    _dim("dim_nomenclature", ("code", "name", "category", "unit", "enbt_code")),
    _dim("dim_source", ("code", "name", "kind")),
    _dim("dim_object",
         ("code", "name", "program_code", "work_type", "commissioning_year", "total_cost"),
         lookups=(Lookup("source_id", "dim_source", nullable=True),)),
    _dim("dim_contract", ("code", "number", "contract_date", "amount", "end_date", "kind"),
         lookups=(BRANCH, Lookup("cp_id", "dim_counterparty"),
                  Lookup("object_id", "dim_object", nullable=True))),
    _dim("dim_loan",
         ("code", "creditor", "project", "currency", "rate", "grace_until", "maturity",
          "amount_uzs"),
         lookups=(BRANCH,), scd=Scd("dim_loan_version", "loan_id", "rate")),
    _dim("dim_position", ("code", "category", "department")),
    Target("fx_rate", "fact", ("currency", "date_id", "rate"), ("currency", "date_id")),
    _fact("fact_cashflow", ("direction", "amount", "gl_account"),
          (BRANCH, Lookup("item_id", "dim_item", fallback_sql=_ITEM_BY_DIRECTION),
           Lookup("cp_id", "dim_counterparty", nullable=True),
           Lookup("contract_id", "dim_contract", nullable=True),
           Lookup("bank_id", "dim_bank", nullable=True)), "month"),
    _fact("fact_balance", ("balance",), (BRANCH, Lookup("bank_id", "dim_bank")), "year"),
    _fact("fact_payment_plan", ("amount",),
          (BRANCH, Lookup("item_id", "dim_item", fallback_sql=_ITEM_OUT))),
    _fact("fact_payment_order", ("amount", "status", "status_date_id"),
          (BRANCH, Lookup("item_id", "dim_item", fallback_sql=_ITEM_OUT),
           Lookup("cp_id", "dim_counterparty", nullable=True),
           Lookup("bank_id", "dim_bank", nullable=True)), "month"),
    _fact("fact_debt", ("receivable", "payable", "overdue", "overdue_days"),
          (BRANCH, Lookup("contract_id", "dim_contract"),
           Lookup("cp_id", "dim_counterparty", nullable=True)), "year"),
    _fact("fact_generation", ("generation", "own_needs", "supply", "plan", "work_hours"),
          (BRANCH,), "year"),
    _fact("fact_energy_sales", ("accrued", "paid", "volume"),
          (BRANCH, Lookup("cp_id", "dim_counterparty"))),
    _fact("fact_procurement",
          ("lot_no", "method", "status", "start_price", "contract_price", "procedure_days"),
          (BRANCH, Lookup("nom_id", "dim_nomenclature", nullable=True),
           Lookup("cp_id", "dim_counterparty", nullable=True))),
    _fact("fact_stock", ("warehouse", "qty", "value", "min_qty", "last_move_date_id"),
          (BRANCH, Lookup("nom_id", "dim_nomenclature")), "year"),
    _fact("fact_stock_move", ("direction", "qty", "value"),
          (BRANCH, Lookup("nom_id", "dim_nomenclature")), "year"),
    _fact("fact_investment", ("plan", "fact", "physical_pct", "advance", "advance_date_id"),
          (BRANCH, Lookup("object_id", "dim_object"),
           Lookup("source_id", "dim_source", nullable=True))),
    _fact("fact_loan_schedule", ("principal", "interest", "balance"),
          (BRANCH, Lookup("loan_id", "dim_loan"))),
    _fact("fact_hr",
          ("headcount_plan", "headcount_fact", "payroll", "hired", "fired", "overtime_hours"),
          (BRANCH, Lookup("pos_id", "dim_position"))),
    _fact("fact_target_funds", ("allocated", "used"),
          (BRANCH, Lookup("source_id", "dim_source"),
           Lookup("object_id", "dim_object", nullable=True))),
)

TARGETS: dict[str, Target] = {t.name: t for t in _ALL}
DIM_TARGETS: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(t.name for t in _ALL if t.kind == "dim")
FACT_TARGETS: tuple[str, ...] = tuple(t.name for t in _ALL if t.kind == "fact")
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implementatsiya — staging**

`backend/app/etl/staging.py`:
```python
"""StagingSink: konnektor qatorlarini stg.rows ga yozadi.

Sezgir maydonlar (INN, hisob raqami) staging'ga yozilishidan OLDIN shifrlanadi — ochiq qiymat
bazaga hech qachon tushmaydi. AAD = "dwh.{jadval}.{ustun}:{code}" (REGISTRY bilan bir xil).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from collections import Counter
from collections.abc import Mapping
from datetime import date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from app.core import keystore
from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.crypto import blind_index
from app.dwh.targets import TARGETS, Target

_INSERT = text("INSERT INTO stg.rows (load_id, target, payload) VALUES (:l, :t, CAST(:p AS jsonb))")


def _json_default(value: object) -> str:
    if isinstance(value, Decimal):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, date | datetime):
        return value.isoformat()
    raise TypeError(f"JSON'ga aylantirib bo'lmaydi: {type(value).__name__}")


def protect(target: Target, payload: dict[str, Any]) -> dict[str, Any]:
    if not target.sensitive:
        return payload
    code = str(payload.get("code") or "")
    if not code:
        raise ValueError(f"{target.name}: shifrlanadigan qator uchun 'code' majburiy")
    index_key = get_settings().key_bytes("blind_index_key")
    for spec in target.sensitive:
        value = payload.pop(spec.field, None)
        if value is None or str(value).strip() == "":
            payload[spec.enc_column] = None
            payload[spec.bidx_column] = None
            continue
        plain = str(value)
        aad = f"dwh.{target.name}.{spec.enc_column}:{code}"
        payload[spec.enc_column] = "\\x" + keystore.encrypt_str(spec.purpose, plain, aad).hex()
        payload[spec.bidx_column] = "\\x" + blind_index(index_key, spec.index_label, plain).hex()
    return payload


def clear_load(conn: Connection, load_id: int) -> None:
    conn.execute(text("DELETE FROM stg.rows WHERE load_id = :l"), {"l": load_id})
    conn.execute(text("DELETE FROM stg.batch WHERE load_id = :l"), {"l": load_id})


class DbStagingSink:
    def __init__(self, conn: Connection, load_id: int, source: str, *,
                 batch_size: int = 2000) -> None:
        self.conn = conn
        self.load_id = load_id
        self.source = source
        self.batch_size = batch_size
        self.counts: Counter[str] = Counter()
        self._buffer: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

    def add(self, target: str, row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
        spec = TARGETS.get(target)
        if spec is None:
            raise ValueError(f"noma'lum staging maqsadi: {target}")
        payload = protect(spec, dict(row))
        self._buffer.append({
            "l": self.load_id,
            "t": target,
            "p": json.dumps(payload, default=_json_default, ensure_ascii=False),
        })
        self.counts[target] += 1
        if len(self._buffer) >= self.batch_size:
            self.flush()

    def flush(self) -> None:
        if self._buffer:
            self.conn.execute(_INSERT, self._buffer)
            self._buffer = []

    def close(self) -> dict[str, int]:
        self.flush()
        for target, count in self.counts.items():
            self.conn.execute(
                text("INSERT INTO stg.batch (load_id, target, source, row_count) "
                     "VALUES (:l, :t, :s, :n)"),
                {"l": self.load_id, "t": target, "s": self.source, "n": count},
            )
        return dict(self.counts)
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implementatsiya — merge**

`backend/app/etl/merge.py`:
```python
"""stg → dwh (spec 5.2): idempotent upsert (faqat o'zgargan qatorlar), o'lcham kodlari → id,
statyasiz → «Прочие» (+ ogohlantirish), noma'lum filial/o'lcham → etl_error va qator
o'tkazib yuboriladi, oyna ichida to'liq almashtirish, SCD-2 versiyalari.

Barcha identifikatorlar app.dwh.targets reyestridan (whitelist).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Collection
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date
from functools import cache

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from app.dwh import partitions
from app.dwh.targets import DIM_TARGETS, FACT_TARGETS, TARGETS, Lookup, Target
from app.etl.types import Window

ERROR_LIMIT = 1000


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class MergeStats:
    target: str
    upserted: int = 0
    deleted: int = 0
    errors: int = 0
    warnings: int = 0


def _lookup_value(i: int, lk: Lookup) -> str:
    if lk.fallback_sql is None:
        return f"l{i}.{lk.column}"
    return (f"coalesce(l{i}.{lk.column}, (SELECT fb.{lk.column} FROM dwh.{lk.dim} fb "
            f"WHERE fb.code = {lk.fallback_sql}))")


def _valid(i: int, lk: Lookup) -> str:
    if lk.fallback_sql is not None:
        return "true"
    if lk.nullable:
        return f"(s.payload->>'{lk.key}' IS NULL OR l{i}.{lk.column} IS NOT NULL)"
    return f"l{i}.{lk.column} IS NOT NULL"


def _updatable(t: Target) -> list[str]:
    data = [*t.columns, *(lk.column for lk in t.lookups)]
    cols = [c for c in data if c not in t.conflict]
    if "src_system" not in t.conflict:
        cols.append("src_system")
    return cols


@cache
def upsert_sql(name: str) -> str:
    t = TARGETS[name]
    insert_cols = [*t.columns, *(lk.column for lk in t.lookups), "src_system", "load_id",
                   "updated_at"]
    exprs = [f"r.{c}" for c in t.columns]
    exprs += [_lookup_value(i, lk) for i, lk in enumerate(t.lookups)]
    exprs += [":source", ":load_id", "now()"]
    joins = "".join(
        f"\nLEFT JOIN dwh.{lk.dim} l{i} ON l{i}.code = s.payload->>'{lk.key}'"
        for i, lk in enumerate(t.lookups)
    )
    conds = ["s.load_id = :load_id", "s.target = :target"]
    conds += [_valid(i, lk) for i, lk in enumerate(t.lookups)]
    if "date_id" in t.columns:
        conds.append("EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dwh.dim_date dd WHERE dd.date_id = r.date_id)")
    keys = [f"r.{c}" for c in t.conflict if c != "src_system"]
    updatable = _updatable(t)
    set_list = [f"{c} = EXCLUDED.{c}" for c in updatable]
    set_list += ["load_id = EXCLUDED.load_id", "updated_at = now()"]
    # Shifrmatn har safar boshqacha (tasodifiy nonce) — o'zgarishni blind index bo'yicha aniqlaymiz.
    encrypted = {s.enc_column for s in t.sensitive}
    compared = [c for c in updatable if c not in encrypted]
    old = ", ".join(f"t.{c}" for c in compared)
    new = ", ".join(f"EXCLUDED.{c}" for c in compared)
    return (
        f"INSERT INTO dwh.{t.name} AS t ({', '.join(insert_cols)})\n"
        f"SELECT DISTINCT ON ({', '.join(keys)}) {', '.join(exprs)}\n"
        "FROM stg.rows s\n"
        f"CROSS JOIN LATERAL jsonb_populate_record(NULL::dwh.{t.name}, s.payload) r{joins}\n"
        f"WHERE {' AND '.join(conds)}\n"
        f"ORDER BY {', '.join(keys)}, s.seq DESC\n"
        f"ON CONFLICT ({', '.join(t.conflict)}) DO UPDATE SET {', '.join(set_list)}\n"
        f"WHERE ({old}) IS DISTINCT FROM ({new})"
    )


@cache
def _error_sql(name: str, index: int) -> str:
    lk = TARGETS[name].lookups[index]
    present = f"s.payload->>'{lk.key}' IS NOT NULL" if lk.nullable else "true"
    return (
        "INSERT INTO core.etl_error (run_id, step, severity, target, src_id, code, message)\n"
        f"SELECT :load_id, :step, '{lk.severity}', :target, "
        "left(coalesce(s.payload->>'src_id', s.payload->>'code'), 128), "
        f"'{lk.error_code}', left('Не найден справочник {lk.dim}: ' || "
        f"coalesce(s.payload->>'{lk.key}', '—'), 500)\n"
        f"FROM stg.rows s LEFT JOIN dwh.{lk.dim} d ON d.code = s.payload->>'{lk.key}'\n"
        f"WHERE s.load_id = :load_id AND s.target = :target AND d.{lk.column} IS NULL "
        f"AND {present}\nLIMIT {ERROR_LIMIT}"
    )


_DATE_ERROR_SQL = (
    "INSERT INTO core.etl_error (run_id, step, severity, target, src_id, code, message)\n"
    "SELECT :load_id, :step, 'error', :target, left(s.payload->>'src_id', 128), 'missing_dim', "
    "left('Дата вне справочника dim_date: ' || coalesce(s.payload->>'date_id', '—'), 500)\n"
    "FROM stg.rows s WHERE s.load_id = :load_id AND s.target = :target AND NOT EXISTS "
    "(SELECT 1 FROM dwh.dim_date dd WHERE dd.date_id::text = s.payload->>'date_id')\n"
    f"LIMIT {ERROR_LIMIT}"
)


@cache
def _moved_sql(name: str) -> str:
    # Hujjat sanasi o'zgargan bo'lsa, eski partitsiyadagi nusxani o'chiramiz.
    return (
        f"DELETE FROM dwh.{name} t USING stg.rows s "
        "WHERE s.load_id = :load_id AND s.target = :target AND t.src_system = :source "
        "AND t.doc_type = s.payload->>'doc_type' AND t.src_id = s.payload->>'src_id' "
        "AND t.date_id <> (s.payload->>'date_id')::int"
    )


@cache
def _replace_sql(name: str) -> str:
    return (
        f"DELETE FROM dwh.{name} t WHERE t.src_system = :source "
        "AND t.date_id BETWEEN :from_id AND :to_id AND NOT EXISTS ("
        "SELECT 1 FROM stg.rows s WHERE s.load_id = :load_id AND s.target = :target "
        "AND s.payload->>'src_id' = t.src_id AND s.payload->>'doc_type' = t.doc_type)"
    )


@cache
def _scd_sql(name: str) -> tuple[str, str, str]:
    t = TARGETS[name]
    assert t.scd is not None
    v, k, c = t.scd.table, t.scd.key, t.scd.column
    changed = (f"v.{k} = d.{k} AND v.is_current AND d.load_id = :load_id "
               f"AND v.{c} IS DISTINCT FROM d.{c}")
    same_day = (f"DELETE FROM dwh.{v} v USING dwh.{name} d WHERE {changed} "
                "AND v.valid_from >= CAST(:as_of AS date)")
    close = (f"UPDATE dwh.{v} v SET valid_to = CAST(:as_of AS date) - 1, is_current = false "
             f"FROM dwh.{name} d WHERE {changed}")
    insert = (
        f"INSERT INTO dwh.{v} ({k}, {c}, valid_from, valid_to, is_current) "
        f"SELECT d.{k}, d.{c}, CASE WHEN EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM dwh.{v} p WHERE p.{k} = d.{k}) "
        "THEN CAST(:as_of AS date) ELSE DATE '2000-01-01' END, NULL, true "
        f"FROM dwh.{name} d WHERE d.load_id = :load_id AND NOT EXISTS "
        f"(SELECT 1 FROM dwh.{v} cur WHERE cur.{k} = d.{k} AND cur.is_current)"
    )
    return same_day, close, insert


def staged_targets(conn: Connection, load_id: int) -> set[str]:
    return set(conn.execute(
        text("SELECT target FROM stg.batch WHERE load_id = :l"), {"l": load_id}).scalars())


def merge_target(conn: Connection, name: str, *, load_id: int, source: str, step: str,
                 as_of: date, window: Window | None = None, replace: bool = False) -> MergeStats:
    t = TARGETS[name]
    params = {"load_id": load_id, "source": source, "target": name, "step": step}
    errors = warnings = deleted = 0
    for i, lk in enumerate(t.lookups):
        count = conn.execute(text(_error_sql(name, i)), params).rowcount
        if lk.fallback_sql:
            warnings += count
        else:
            errors += count
    if "date_id" in t.columns:
        errors += conn.execute(text(_DATE_ERROR_SQL), params).rowcount
    if t.partition is not None:
        deleted += conn.execute(text(_moved_sql(name)), params).rowcount
    upserted = conn.execute(text(upsert_sql(name)), params).rowcount
    if t.scd is not None:
        for sql in _scd_sql(name):
            conn.execute(text(sql), {"load_id": load_id, "as_of": as_of})
    if replace and t.windowed and window is not None:
        deleted += conn.execute(
            text(_replace_sql(name)),
            {**params, "from_id": window.from_id, "to_id": window.to_id},
        ).rowcount
    return MergeStats(name, upserted, deleted, errors, warnings)


def merge_dims(conn: Connection, *, load_id: int, source: str, as_of: date) -> list[MergeStats]:
    staged = staged_targets(conn, load_id)
    return [
        merge_target(conn, name, load_id=load_id, source=source, step="dims", as_of=as_of)
        for name in DIM_TARGETS
        if name in staged
    ]


def merge_facts(conn: Connection, *, load_id: int, source: str, window: Window,
                complete: Collection[str], as_of: date) -> list[MergeStats]:
    staged = staged_targets(conn, load_id)
    out: list[MergeStats] = []
    for name in FACT_TARGETS:
        if name not in staged and name not in complete:
            continue
        if TARGETS[name].partition and name in staged:
            lo, hi = conn.execute(
                text("SELECT min(dd.date), max(dd.date) FROM stg.rows s JOIN dwh.dim_date dd "
                     "ON dd.date_id::text = s.payload->>'date_id' "
                     "WHERE s.load_id = :l AND s.target = :t"),
                {"l": load_id, "t": name},
            ).one()
            if lo is not None:
                partitions.ensure_partitions(conn, name, lo, hi)
        out.append(merge_target(conn, name, load_id=load_id, source=source, step="facts",
                                as_of=as_of, window=window, replace=name in complete))
    return out
```

```python
import json
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core.db import apply_rls, new_session
from app.core.errors import ConflictError
from app.dwh import builds
from app.dwh.aggregates import BuildParams, build_all
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from tests.etl_helpers import (
    TEST_AS_OF,
    branch_row,
    cash_row,
    merge_all,
    new_source,
    new_tag,
    open_run,
    purge_source,
    stage,
)

PARAMS = BuildParams(as_of=TEST_AS_OF, date_from=date(2024, 1, 1))


@pytest.fixture
def source() -> Iterator[str]:
    name = new_source()
    yield name
    purge_source(name)


def _scenario(source: str) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Test filiali: avgust 2026 — kirim 100, chiqim 40; qoldiq 1000 → 1060."""
    tag = new_tag()
    branch = branch_row(tag)
    code = branch["code"]
    bank = {"code": f"ACC-{tag}", "bank_name": "Тест банк", "account_number": f"2021{tag}",
            "currency": "UZS"}
    rows: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = {
        "dim_branch": [branch],
        "dim_bank": [bank],
        "fact_cashflow": [
            cash_row("in", 20260810, code, 100, direction="in", item_code="PKM54-I06"),
            cash_row("out", 20260811, code, 40),
            cash_row("ly1", 20250920, code, 7),
            cash_row("ly2", 20250928, code, 5),
        ],
        "fact_balance": [
            {"doc_type": "statement", "src_id": "b1", "date_id": 20260731, "branch_code": code,
             "bank_code": bank["code"], "balance": 1000},
            {"doc_type": "statement", "src_id": "b2", "date_id": 20260831, "branch_code": code,
             "bank_code": bank["code"], "balance": 1060},
        ],
        "fact_payment_plan": [
            {"doc_type": "payment_plan", "src_id": "p1", "date_id": 20260801, "branch_code": code,
             "item_code": "PKM54-O09", "amount": 50},
        ],
    }
    run_id = open_run(source)
    stage(run_id, source, rows)
    merge_all(run_id, source)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        branch_id = int(conn.execute(
            text("SELECT branch_id FROM dwh.dim_branch WHERE code = :c"), {"c": code}
        ).scalar_one())
        build_id = builds.create_build(conn, run_id)
        build_all(conn, build_id, PARAMS)
        builds.mark_ready(conn, build_id)
    return branch_id, build_id


def _cash(build_id: int, branch_id: int, month: int, item_code: str | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    item_sql = "0" if item_code is None else \
        "(SELECT item_id FROM dwh.dim_item WHERE code = :i)"
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        row = conn.execute(
            text(f"SELECT * FROM mart.agg_cash_month WHERE build_id = :b AND branch_id = :br "
                 f"AND month_id = :m AND item_id = {item_sql}"),
            {"b": build_id, "br": branch_id, "m": month, "i": item_code},
        ).mappings().one()
    return dict(row)


def test_cash_aggregate_items_totals_and_balances(source: str) -> None:
    branch_id, build_id = _scenario(source)
    out = _cash(build_id, branch_id, 202608, "PKM54-O09")
    assert float(out["outflow"]) == 40 and float(out["inflow"]) == 0
    total = _cash(build_id, branch_id, 202608, None)
    assert float(total["inflow"]) == 100 and float(total["outflow"]) == 40
    assert float(total["opening_balance"]) == 1000
    assert float(total["closing_balance"]) == 1060
    assert float(total["diff"]) == 0
    assert total["days_in_period"] == 31
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        all_branches = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT inflow FROM mart.agg_cash_month WHERE build_id = :b AND branch_id = 0 "
                 "AND month_id = 202608 AND item_id = 0"), {"b": build_id}).scalar_one()
        current_days = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT max(days_in_period) FROM mart.agg_cash_month WHERE build_id = :b "
                 "AND month_id = 202609"), {"b": build_id}).scalar_one()
    assert float(all_branches) >= 100
    assert current_days in (None, 24)


def test_cmp_columns_use_as_of_day(source: str) -> None:
    branch_id, build_id = _scenario(source)
    last_year = _cash(build_id, branch_id, 202509, "PKM54-O09")
    assert float(last_year["outflow"]) == 12
    assert float(last_year["outflow_cmp"]) == 7  # faqat 1..24-kunlar


def test_payments_aggregate_plan_vs_fact(source: str) -> None:
    branch_id, build_id = _scenario(source)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        row = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT plan_amount, fact_amount FROM mart.agg_payments_month "
                 "WHERE build_id = :b AND branch_id = :br AND month_id = 202608 AND item_id = "
                 "(SELECT item_id FROM dwh.dim_item WHERE code = 'PKM54-O09')"),
            {"b": build_id, "br": branch_id}).one()
    assert (float(row[0]), float(row[1])) == (50.0, 40.0)


def test_old_months_are_carried_forward_from_active_build(source: str) -> None:
    """Faktlar 5 yil, agregatlar 10 yil: date_from dan oldingi oylar oldingi build'dan olinadi."""
    branch_id, first = _scenario(source)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        builds.publish(conn, first, keep=3)
        second = builds.create_build(conn, None)
        build_all(conn, second, BuildParams(as_of=TEST_AS_OF, date_from=date(2026, 1, 1),
                                            keep_from=date(2016, 1, 1)))
    carried = _cash(second, branch_id, 202509, "PKM54-O09")
    assert float(carried["outflow"]) == 12 and float(carried["outflow_cmp"]) == 7
    assert float(_cash(second, branch_id, 202608, None)["outflow"]) == 40


def test_publish_switches_version_and_keeps_three(source: str) -> None:
    created: list[int] = []
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        for _ in range(4):
            build_id = builds.create_build(conn, None)
            if not created:  # prune mart qatorlarini ham o'chirishini tekshirish uchun
                build_all(conn, build_id, PARAMS)
            builds.mark_ready(conn, build_id)
            builds.publish(conn, build_id, keep=3)
            created.append(build_id)
        assert builds.active_build(conn) == created[-1]
        statuses = dict(conn.execute(
            text("SELECT build_id, status FROM core.builds WHERE build_id = ANY(:ids)"),
            {"ids": created}).all())
        leftovers = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT count(*) FROM mart.agg_cash_month WHERE build_id = :b"),
            {"b": created[0]}).scalar_one()
    assert created[0] not in statuses and leftovers == 0
    assert statuses[created[-1]] == "published"
    assert statuses[created[1]] == statuses[created[2]] == "superseded"


def test_rollback_to_previous_and_explicit(source: str) -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        first = builds.create_build(conn, None)
        builds.mark_ready(conn, first)
        builds.publish(conn, first, keep=3)
        second = builds.create_build(conn, None)
        builds.mark_ready(conn, second)
        builds.publish(conn, second, keep=3)
        assert builds.rollback(conn) == first
        assert builds.active_build(conn) == first
        assert builds.rollback(conn, second) == second
        with pytest.raises(ConflictError):
            builds.rollback(conn, 99_999_999)


def test_failed_build_rows_removed(source: str) -> None:
    _, build_id = _scenario(source)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        builds.mark_failed(conn, build_id)
        count = conn.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM mart.agg_cash_month WHERE build_id = :b"),
                             {"b": build_id}).scalar_one()
        status = conn.execute(text("SELECT status FROM core.builds WHERE build_id = :b"),
                              {"b": build_id}).scalar_one()
        with pytest.raises(ConflictError):
            builds.publish(conn, build_id, keep=3)
    assert (count, status) == (0, "failed")


def test_pub_view_reads_only_active_build(source: str) -> None:
    _, build_id = _scenario(source)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        builds.publish(conn, build_id, keep=3)
        expected = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT count(*) FROM mart.agg_cash_month WHERE build_id = :b"),
            {"b": build_id}).scalar_one()
    with new_session() as s:
        apply_rls(s, None)
        seen = s.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM pub.agg_cash_month")).scalar_one()
    assert seen == expected


def _plan_nodes(node: dict[str, Any]) -> Iterator[dict[str, Any]]:
    yield node
    for child in node.get("Plans", []):
        yield from _plan_nodes(child)


def test_pub_query_uses_mart_index_not_facts() -> None:
    """UI so'rovi faktlarga tegmaydi va mart PK indeksidan foydalana oladi.

    mart jadvallari kichik (build'ga ~17 filial × 33 oy × ~16 statya ≈ 9 ming qator), shuning
    uchun planner haqiqiy hajmda seq scan tanlashi ham maqbul; bu yerda enable_seqscan=off
    bilan indeks so'rov shakliga mosligini qotiramiz.
    """
    with new_session() as s:
        apply_rls(s, frozenset({3}))
        s.execute(text("SET LOCAL enable_seqscan = off"))
        plan = s.execute(text(
            "EXPLAIN (FORMAT JSON) SELECT month_id, sum(outflow) FROM pub.agg_cash_month "
            "WHERE branch_id = 3 AND item_id = 0 AND month_id BETWEEN 202601 AND 202609 "
            "GROUP BY month_id")).scalar_one()
    root = (plan if isinstance(plan, list) else json.loads(plan))[0]["Plan"]
    nodes = list(_plan_nodes(root))
    relations = {n.get("Relation Name") for n in nodes} - {None}
    assert not any(r.startswith("fact_") for r in relations)
    mart_nodes = [n for n in nodes if n.get("Relation Name") == "agg_cash_month"]
    index_scans = {"Index Scan", "Index Only Scan", "Bitmap Heap Scan"}
    assert mart_nodes and all(n["Node Type"] in index_scans for n in mart_nodes)
```

```python
"""mart oylik agregatlari va pub view'lari (faqat aktiv build)

Revision ID: 0008
Revises: 0007
Create Date: 2026-09-24
"""

from alembic import op

revision = "0008"
down_revision = "0007"
branch_labels = None
depends_on = None

MONEY = "numeric(20,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0"
ACTIVE = "(SELECT d.active_build_id FROM core.data_version d WHERE d.id = 1)"

STATEMENTS = [
    f"""
    CREATE TABLE mart.agg_cash_month (
      build_id bigint NOT NULL REFERENCES core.builds (build_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      branch_id integer NOT NULL CHECK (branch_id >= 0),
      month_id integer NOT NULL,
      item_id integer NOT NULL CHECK (item_id >= 0),
      inflow {MONEY},
      outflow {MONEY},
      inflow_cmp {MONEY},
      outflow_cmp {MONEY},
      days_in_period smallint NOT NULL,
      opening_balance numeric(20,2),
      closing_balance numeric(20,2),
      diff numeric(20,2),
      PRIMARY KEY (build_id, branch_id, month_id, item_id)
    )
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE TABLE mart.agg_payments_month (
      build_id bigint NOT NULL REFERENCES core.builds (build_id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
      branch_id integer NOT NULL CHECK (branch_id >= 0),
      month_id integer NOT NULL,
      item_id integer NOT NULL,
      plan_amount {MONEY},
      fact_amount {MONEY},
      fact_cmp {MONEY},
      days_in_period smallint NOT NULL,
      PRIMARY KEY (build_id, branch_id, month_id, item_id)
    )
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE VIEW pub.agg_cash_month WITH (security_invoker = true) AS
    SELECT a.branch_id, a.month_id, a.item_id, a.inflow, a.outflow, a.inflow_cmp, a.outflow_cmp,
           a.days_in_period, a.opening_balance, a.closing_balance, a.diff
    FROM mart.agg_cash_month a WHERE a.build_id = {ACTIVE}
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE VIEW pub.agg_payments_month WITH (security_invoker = true) AS
    SELECT a.branch_id, a.month_id, a.item_id, a.plan_amount, a.fact_amount, a.fact_cmp,
           a.days_in_period
    FROM mart.agg_payments_month a WHERE a.build_id = {ACTIVE}
    """,
    """
    CREATE VIEW pub.branches WITH (security_invoker = true) AS
    SELECT branch_id, code, name, short_name, kind, capacity_mw, lat, lon, sort_order
    FROM dwh.dim_branch
    """,
    """
    CREATE VIEW pub.items WITH (security_invoker = true) AS
    SELECT item_id, code, name, activity, direction, sort_order FROM dwh.dim_item
    """,
    "GRANT USAGE ON SCHEMA mart TO ugk_app",
    "GRANT SELECT ON mart.agg_cash_month, mart.agg_payments_month TO ugk_app",
    "GRANT SELECT, INSERT, DELETE ON mart.agg_cash_month, mart.agg_payments_month TO ugk_etl",
    "GRANT SELECT ON pub.agg_cash_month, pub.agg_payments_month, pub.branches, pub.items TO ugk_app",
]


def upgrade() -> None:
    for statement in STATEMENTS:
        op.execute(statement)
    for table in ("agg_cash_month", "agg_payments_month"):
        op.execute(f"ALTER TABLE mart.{table} ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY")
        op.execute(f"ALTER TABLE mart.{table} FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY")
        op.execute(
            f"CREATE POLICY branch_scope ON mart.{table} USING (core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id)) "
            "WITH CHECK (core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id))"
        )


def downgrade() -> None:
    for view in ("items", "branches", "agg_payments_month", "agg_cash_month"):
        op.execute(f"DROP VIEW pub.{view}")
    op.execute("DROP TABLE mart.agg_payments_month")
    op.execute("DROP TABLE mart.agg_cash_month")
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — agregat freymvorki**

`backend/app/dwh/aggregates.py`:
```python
"""Oylik agregatlar freymvorki (spec 4.7). Keyingi rejalar yangi oila qo'shganda bitta
`register(Aggregate(...))` yozadi: jadval + tartiblangan SQL'lar. Parametrlar:
:build_id, :as_of, :date_from, :from_id, :as_of_id, :cmp_day, :from_month, :keep_month.

Saqlash (spec 4.7): faktlar 5 yil, agregatlar 10 yil — `date_from` dan oldingi oylar
(faktlari o'chirilgan) yangi build'ga oldingi aktiv build'dan ko'chiriladi.

UI faqat shu agregatlarni (pub view orqali) o'qiydi — faktlarga so'rov yubormaydi (TZ 9).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from app.dwh.dates import date_id, month_id


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Aggregate:
    table: str  # mart sxemasidagi jadval
    statements: tuple[str, ...]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BuildParams:
    as_of: date
    date_from: date
    keep_from: date | None = None  # agregatlar saqlanadigan eng eski sana (10 yil)

    def bind(self, build_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {
            "build_id": build_id,
            "as_of": self.as_of,
            "date_from": self.date_from,
            "from_id": date_id(self.date_from),
            "as_of_id": date_id(self.as_of),
            "cmp_day": self.as_of.day,
            "from_month": month_id(self.date_from),
            "keep_month": month_id(self.keep_from or self.date_from),
        }


AGGREGATES: list[Aggregate] = []


def register(aggregate: Aggregate) -> Aggregate:
    if any(a.table == aggregate.table for a in AGGREGATES):
        raise ValueError(f"agregat allaqachon ro'yxatda: {aggregate.table}")
    AGGREGATES.append(aggregate)
    return aggregate


# Faktlari yo'q eski oylar: aktiv build qatorlari build_id almashtirilib ko'chiriladi.
_CARRY_FORWARD = (
    "INSERT INTO mart.{table} SELECT (jsonb_populate_record(NULL::mart.{table}, "
    "to_jsonb(a) || jsonb_build_object('build_id', :build_id))).* FROM mart.{table} a "
    "WHERE a.build_id = (SELECT active_build_id FROM core.data_version WHERE id = 1) "
    "AND a.month_id < :from_month AND a.month_id >= :keep_month"
)


def build_all(conn: Connection, build_id: int, params: BuildParams) -> dict[str, int]:
    bound = params.bind(build_id)
    out: dict[str, int] = {}
    for aggregate in AGGREGATES:
        carried = conn.execute(text(_CARRY_FORWARD.format(table=aggregate.table)),
                               bound).rowcount
        out[aggregate.table] = carried + sum(
            conn.execute(text(sql), bound).rowcount for sql in aggregate.statements
        )
    return out


# Davr oylari: days = as_of gacha bo'lgan kunlar (yopilmagan oy uchun haqiqiy kunlar).
_MONTHS = """months AS (
  SELECT month_id, min(date_id) AS first_id, max(date_id) AS end_id, count(*)::smallint AS days
  FROM dwh.dim_date WHERE date BETWEEN CAST(:date_from AS date) AND CAST(:as_of AS date)
  GROUP BY month_id)"""

_CASH_ITEMS = f"""
INSERT INTO mart.agg_cash_month (build_id, branch_id, month_id, item_id, inflow, outflow,
                                 inflow_cmp, outflow_cmp, days_in_period)
WITH {_MONTHS}
SELECT :build_id, coalesce(x.branch_id, 0), x.month_id, x.item_id, sum(x.inflow),
       sum(x.outflow), sum(x.inflow_cmp), sum(x.outflow_cmp), max(x.days)
FROM (
  SELECT f.branch_id, d.month_id, f.item_id, m.days,
         CASE WHEN f.direction = 'in' THEN f.amount ELSE 0 END AS inflow,
         CASE WHEN f.direction = 'out' THEN f.amount ELSE 0 END AS outflow,
         CASE WHEN f.direction = 'in' AND d.day <= :cmp_day THEN f.amount ELSE 0 END AS inflow_cmp,
         CASE WHEN f.direction = 'out' AND d.day <= :cmp_day THEN f.amount ELSE 0 END AS outflow_cmp
  FROM dwh.fact_cashflow f
  JOIN dwh.dim_date d ON d.date_id = f.date_id
  JOIN months m ON m.month_id = d.month_id
  WHERE f.date_id BETWEEN :from_id AND :as_of_id
) x
GROUP BY GROUPING SETS ((x.branch_id, x.month_id, x.item_id), (x.month_id, x.item_id))
"""

# Filial × oy jami qatori (item_id = 0): oqimlar + hisob bo'yicha oxirgi qoldiqlar yig'indisi.
_CASH_TOTALS = f"""
INSERT INTO mart.agg_cash_month (build_id, branch_id, month_id, item_id, inflow, outflow,
                                 inflow_cmp, outflow_cmp, days_in_period, opening_balance,
                                 closing_balance, diff)
WITH {_MONTHS},
flows AS (
  SELECT branch_id, month_id, sum(inflow) AS inflow, sum(outflow) AS outflow,
         sum(inflow_cmp) AS inflow_cmp, sum(outflow_cmp) AS outflow_cmp
  FROM mart.agg_cash_month
  WHERE build_id = :build_id AND branch_id <> 0 AND item_id <> 0 AND month_id >= :from_month
  GROUP BY branch_id, month_id),
bal AS (
  SELECT b.branch_id, m.month_id, m.days, ob.total AS opening, cb.total AS closing
  FROM dwh.dim_branch b
  CROSS JOIN months m
  LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT sum(z.balance) AS total FROM (
      SELECT DISTINCT ON (fb.bank_id) fb.balance FROM dwh.fact_balance fb
      WHERE fb.branch_id = b.branch_id AND fb.date_id < m.first_id
      ORDER BY fb.bank_id, fb.date_id DESC) z) ob ON true
  LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT sum(z.balance) AS total FROM (
      SELECT DISTINCT ON (fb.bank_id) fb.balance FROM dwh.fact_balance fb
      WHERE fb.branch_id = b.branch_id AND fb.date_id <= m.end_id
      ORDER BY fb.bank_id, fb.date_id DESC) z) cb ON true)
SELECT :build_id, bal.branch_id, bal.month_id, 0, coalesce(f.inflow, 0), coalesce(f.outflow, 0),
       coalesce(f.inflow_cmp, 0), coalesce(f.outflow_cmp, 0), bal.days, bal.opening, bal.closing,
       bal.closing - (bal.opening + coalesce(f.inflow, 0) - coalesce(f.outflow, 0))
FROM bal LEFT JOIN flows f ON f.branch_id = bal.branch_id AND f.month_id = bal.month_id
WHERE f.branch_id IS NOT NULL OR bal.closing IS NOT NULL
"""

_CASH_ALL = """
INSERT INTO mart.agg_cash_month (build_id, branch_id, month_id, item_id, inflow, outflow,
                                 inflow_cmp, outflow_cmp, days_in_period, opening_balance,
                                 closing_balance, diff)
SELECT :build_id, 0, month_id, 0, sum(inflow), sum(outflow), sum(inflow_cmp), sum(outflow_cmp),
       max(days_in_period), sum(opening_balance), sum(closing_balance), sum(diff)
FROM mart.agg_cash_month
WHERE build_id = :build_id AND item_id = 0 AND branch_id <> 0 AND month_id >= :from_month
GROUP BY month_id
"""

_PAYMENTS = f"""
INSERT INTO mart.agg_payments_month (build_id, branch_id, month_id, item_id, plan_amount,
                                     fact_amount, fact_cmp, days_in_period)
WITH {_MONTHS},
plan AS (
  SELECT p.branch_id, d.month_id, p.item_id, sum(p.amount) AS amount
  FROM dwh.fact_payment_plan p
  JOIN dwh.dim_date d ON d.date_id = p.date_id
  JOIN months m ON m.month_id = d.month_id
  GROUP BY p.branch_id, d.month_id, p.item_id),
fact AS (
  SELECT f.branch_id, d.month_id, f.item_id, sum(f.amount) AS amount,
         sum(f.amount) FILTER (WHERE d.day <= :cmp_day) AS cmp
  FROM dwh.fact_cashflow f
  JOIN dwh.dim_date d ON d.date_id = f.date_id
  JOIN months m ON m.month_id = d.month_id
  WHERE f.direction = 'out' AND f.date_id BETWEEN :from_id AND :as_of_id
  GROUP BY f.branch_id, d.month_id, f.item_id),
j AS (
  SELECT coalesce(p.branch_id, f.branch_id) AS branch_id,
         coalesce(p.month_id, f.month_id) AS month_id,
         coalesce(p.item_id, f.item_id) AS item_id,
         coalesce(p.amount, 0) AS plan_amount, coalesce(f.amount, 0) AS fact_amount,
         coalesce(f.cmp, 0) AS fact_cmp
  FROM plan p FULL JOIN fact f
    ON f.branch_id = p.branch_id AND f.month_id = p.month_id AND f.item_id = p.item_id)
SELECT :build_id, coalesce(j.branch_id, 0), j.month_id, j.item_id, sum(j.plan_amount),
       sum(j.fact_amount), sum(j.fact_cmp), max(m.days)
FROM j JOIN months m ON m.month_id = j.month_id
GROUP BY GROUPING SETS ((j.branch_id, j.month_id, j.item_id), (j.month_id, j.item_id))
"""

CASH_MONTH = register(Aggregate("agg_cash_month", (_CASH_ITEMS, _CASH_TOTALS, _CASH_ALL)))
PAYMENTS_MONTH = register(Aggregate("agg_payments_month", (_PAYMENTS,)))
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implementatsiya — buildlar**

`backend/app/core/errors.py` — `MESSAGES` ga qo'shing (`"internal_error"` qatoridan oldin):
```python
    "build_not_available": "Сборка недоступна",
```

`backend/app/dwh/builds.py`:
```python
"""Buildlar: agregatlar snapshot'i. Publish = bitta tranzaksiyada data_version almashtirish;
oxirgi `keep` ta muvaffaqiyatli build saqlanadi (spec 4.7)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from app.core.errors import ConflictError
from app.dwh.aggregates import AGGREGATES


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BuildInfo:
    build_id: int
    created_at: datetime
    status: str
    load_id: int | None
    published_at: datetime | None
    active: bool


def create_build(conn: Connection, load_id: int | None) -> int:
    return int(conn.execute(
        text("INSERT INTO core.builds (status, load_id) VALUES ('building', :l) "
             "RETURNING build_id"), {"l": load_id}).scalar_one())


def mark_ready(conn: Connection, build_id: int) -> None:
    conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.builds SET status = 'ready' WHERE build_id = :b"),
                 {"b": build_id})


def mark_failed(conn: Connection, build_id: int) -> None:
    for aggregate in AGGREGATES:
        # Jadval nomi reyestrdan (whitelist).
        conn.execute(text(f"DELETE FROM mart.{aggregate.table} WHERE build_id = :b"),
                     {"b": build_id})
    conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.builds SET status = 'failed' WHERE build_id = :b"),
                 {"b": build_id})


def active_build(conn: Connection) -> int | None:
    value = conn.execute(
        text("SELECT active_build_id FROM core.data_version WHERE id = 1")).scalar_one_or_none()
    return None if value is None else int(value)


def publish(conn: Connection, build_id: int, *, keep: int | None) -> None:
    conn.execute(text("SELECT 1 FROM core.data_version WHERE id = 1 FOR UPDATE"))
    status = conn.execute(
        text("SELECT status FROM core.builds WHERE build_id = :b FOR UPDATE"),
        {"b": build_id}).scalar_one_or_none()
    if status not in ("ready", "published", "superseded"):
        raise ConflictError("build_not_available", build_id=build_id)
    conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.builds SET status = 'superseded' "
                      "WHERE status = 'published' AND build_id <> :b"), {"b": build_id})
    conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.builds SET status = 'published', published_at = now() "
                      "WHERE build_id = :b"), {"b": build_id})
    conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.data_version SET active_build_id = :b, published_at = now() "
                      "WHERE id = 1"), {"b": build_id})
    if keep is not None:
        prune(conn, keep)


def prune(conn: Connection, keep: int) -> list[int]:
    """Aktivdan tashqari eng yangi `keep` ta muvaffaqiyatli build qoladi; qolganlari (va
    muvaffaqiyatsizlar) o'chiriladi — mart qatorlari ON DELETE CASCADE bilan ketadi."""
    kept = list(conn.execute(
        text("SELECT build_id FROM core.builds WHERE status IN ('published', 'superseded') "
             "ORDER BY build_id DESC LIMIT :k"), {"k": keep}).scalars())
    return list(conn.execute(
        text("DELETE FROM core.builds WHERE status IN ('superseded', 'failed') "
             "AND NOT (build_id = ANY(:kept)) AND build_id IS DISTINCT FROM "
             "(SELECT active_build_id FROM core.data_version WHERE id = 1) "
             "RETURNING build_id"), {"kept": kept}).scalars())


def rollback(conn: Connection, build_id: int | None = None) -> int:
    """Aktivdan oldingi (yoki ko'rsatilgan) saqlangan buildga qaytish."""
    current = active_build(conn)
    target = build_id
    if target is None:
        if current is None:
            raise ConflictError("build_not_available")
        target = conn.execute(
            text("SELECT build_id FROM core.builds WHERE status = 'superseded' "
                 "AND build_id < :a ORDER BY build_id DESC LIMIT 1"),
            {"a": current}).scalar_one_or_none()
        if target is None:
            raise ConflictError("build_not_available")
    publish(conn, int(target), keep=None)
    return int(target)


def list_builds(conn: Connection) -> list[BuildInfo]:
    active = active_build(conn)
    rows = conn.execute(text(
        "SELECT build_id, created_at, status, load_id, published_at FROM core.builds "
        "ORDER BY build_id DESC LIMIT 50")).all()
    return [BuildInfo(r.build_id, r.created_at, r.status, r.load_id, r.published_at,
                      r.build_id == active) for r in rows]
```

  - `app.etl.quality`: `CheckResult(code, ok, detail: dict)`, `CHECK_CODES = ("control_totals", "balance_identity", "mandatory_dims", "capacity_limit", "dangling_refs")`, `CONTROL_TOLERANCE = Decimal("0.001")`, `BALANCE_TOLERANCE = Decimal("0.01")`, `run_checks(conn, *, run_id, build_id, source, window, totals) -> list[CheckResult]`, `failed(results) -> list[CheckResult]`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Test yordamchisi**

`backend/tests/etl_helpers.py` — import'larga qo'shing:
```python
from app.dwh import builds
from app.dwh.aggregates import BuildParams, build_all
```
va fayl oxiriga:
```python
def load_and_build(
    source: str, rows: Mapping[str, Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]], *,
    window: Window = FULL_WINDOW,
) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Staging → merge → yangi build (publish qilinmaydi). (run_id, build_id) qaytaradi."""
    run_id = open_run(source)
    stage(run_id, source, rows)
    merge_all(run_id, source, window)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        build_id = builds.create_build(conn, run_id)
        build_all(conn, build_id, BuildParams(as_of=TEST_AS_OF, date_from=window.date_from))
        builds.mark_ready(conn, build_id)
    return run_id, build_id
```

```python
from collections.abc import Iterator
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any

import pytest

from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.etl.quality import CHECK_CODES, CheckResult, failed, run_checks
from app.etl.types import ControlTotals
from tests.etl_helpers import (
    FULL_WINDOW,
    branch_row,
    cash_row,
    load_and_build,
    new_source,
    new_tag,
    purge_source,
)


@pytest.fixture
def source() -> Iterator[str]:
    name = new_source()
    yield name
    purge_source(name)


def _check(source: str, rows: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]],
           totals: ControlTotals | None = None) -> dict[str, CheckResult]:
    run_id, build_id = load_and_build(source, rows)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        results = run_checks(conn, run_id=run_id, build_id=build_id, source=source,
                             window=FULL_WINDOW, totals=totals)
    assert [r.code for r in results] == list(CHECK_CODES)
    return {r.code: r for r in results}


def _clean(tag: str, **branch: Any) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    b = branch_row(tag, **branch)
    return {"dim_branch": [b],
            "fact_cashflow": [cash_row("c1", 20260805, b["code"], 100),
                              cash_row("c2", 20260906, b["code"], 50, direction="in",
                                       item_code="PKM54-I06")]}


def test_clean_data_passes_all_checks(source: str) -> None:
    results = _check(source, _clean(new_tag()),
                     ControlTotals({202608: Decimal("100"), 202609: Decimal("50")}))
    assert failed(list(results.values())) == []


def test_control_totals_mismatch_fails(source: str) -> None:
    results = _check(source, _clean(new_tag()),
                     ControlTotals({202608: Decimal("101"), 202609: Decimal("50")}))
    assert not results["control_totals"].ok
    assert results["control_totals"].detail["mismatches"][0]["month"] == 202608


def test_control_totals_within_tolerance_pass(source: str) -> None:
    results = _check(source, _clean(new_tag()),
                     ControlTotals({202608: Decimal("100.05"), 202609: Decimal("50")}))
    assert results["control_totals"].ok


def test_missing_month_in_source_totals_fails(source: str) -> None:
    results = _check(source, _clean(new_tag()), ControlTotals({202608: Decimal("100")}))
    assert not results["control_totals"].ok


def test_balance_identity_violation_fails(source: str) -> None:
    tag = new_tag()
    rows = _clean(tag)
    code = rows["dim_branch"][0]["code"]
    rows["dim_bank"] = [{"code": f"ACC-{tag}", "bank_name": "Тест", "currency": "UZS"}]
    rows["fact_balance"] = [
        {"doc_type": "statement", "src_id": "o", "date_id": 20260731, "branch_code": code,
         "bank_code": f"ACC-{tag}", "balance": 1000},
        # To'g'risi 900 (1000 − 100); 800 — farq 100, oborot 100 ning 1% idan katta.
        {"doc_type": "statement", "src_id": "c", "date_id": 20260831, "branch_code": code,
         "bank_code": f"ACC-{tag}", "balance": 800},
        {"doc_type": "statement", "src_id": "s", "date_id": 20260924, "branch_code": code,
         "bank_code": f"ACC-{tag}", "balance": 850},
    ]
    results = _check(source, rows)
    assert not results["balance_identity"].ok
    assert results["balance_identity"].detail["violations"][0]["month_id"] == 202608


def test_balance_identity_small_difference_passes(source: str) -> None:
    tag = new_tag()
    rows = _clean(tag)
    code = rows["dim_branch"][0]["code"]
    rows["dim_bank"] = [{"code": f"ACC-{tag}", "bank_name": "Тест", "currency": "UZS"}]
    rows["fact_balance"] = [
        {"doc_type": "statement", "src_id": "o", "date_id": 20260731, "branch_code": code,
         "bank_code": f"ACC-{tag}", "balance": 1000},
        {"doc_type": "statement", "src_id": "c", "date_id": 20260831, "branch_code": code,
         "bank_code": f"ACC-{tag}", "balance": 899.5},  # |Разница| 0,5 ≤ 1% × 100
        {"doc_type": "statement", "src_id": "s", "date_id": 20260924, "branch_code": code,
         "bank_code": f"ACC-{tag}", "balance": 949.5},  # sentabr: +50 kirim
    ]
    assert _check(source, rows)["balance_identity"].ok


def test_missing_branch_fails_mandatory_dims(source: str) -> None:
    rows = _clean(new_tag())

def test_unknown_item_only_warns(source: str) -> None:

        cash_row("y", 20260805, rows["dim_branch"][0]["code"], 5, item_code="NO-ITEM"))
    results = _check(source, rows)
    assert results["mandatory_dims"].ok
    assert results["mandatory_dims"].detail["warnings"] == 1


def test_generation_above_capacity_fails(source: str) -> None:
    tag = new_tag()
    rows = _clean(tag, capacity_mw=10)  # maksimum 10 × 24 = 240 MVt·soat = 0,24 mln kVt·soat
    code = rows["dim_branch"][0]["code"]
    rows["fact_generation"] = [
        {"doc_type": "askue_day", "src_id": "g1", "date_id": 20260805, "branch_code": code,
         "generation": 0.2},
        {"doc_type": "askue_day", "src_id": "g2", "date_id": 20260806, "branch_code": code,
         "generation": 0.5},
    ]
    results = _check(source, rows)
    assert not results["capacity_limit"].ok
    assert [v["date_id"] for v in results["capacity_limit"].detail["violations"]] == [20260806]


def test_missing_counterparty_fails_dangling_refs(source: str) -> None:
    rows = _clean(new_tag())
    rows["fact_cashflow"].append(
        cash_row("z", 20260805, rows["dim_branch"][0]["code"], 5, cp_code="NO-CP"))
    results = _check(source, rows)
    assert not results["dangling_refs"].ok
    assert results["dangling_refs"].detail["errors"] == 1
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya**

`backend/app/etl/quality.py`:
```python
"""Sifat tekshiruvlari (spec 5.3, TZ 3). Istalgan buzilish → failed_quality, publish yo'q.

1. control_totals   — fact_cashflow oylik summasi = manba nazorat summasi (5110/5210/5530) ±0,1%
2. balance_identity — konechniy = nachalniy + ЧДП + Разница, |Разница| ≤ 1% oborot
3. mandatory_dims   — filial majburiy (statyasiz → «Прочие» + ogohlantirish, xato emas)
4. capacity_limit   — sutkalik ishlab chiqarish ≤ quvvat × 24
5. dangling_refs    — mavjud bo'lmagan o'lchovga havola yo'q (FK + etl_error hisoboti)
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from app.etl.types import ControlTotals, Window

CHECK_CODES = ("control_totals", "balance_identity", "mandatory_dims", "capacity_limit",
               "dangling_refs")
CONTROL_TOLERANCE = Decimal("0.001")
BALANCE_TOLERANCE = Decimal("0.01")
SAMPLE = 50


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CheckResult:
    code: str
    ok: bool
    detail: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)


def failed(results: list[CheckResult]) -> list[CheckResult]:
    return [r for r in results if not r.ok]


def _control_totals(conn: Connection, source: str, window: Window,
                    totals: ControlTotals | None) -> CheckResult:
    if totals is None:
        return CheckResult("control_totals", True, {"skipped": True})
    ours = {
        int(m): Decimal(v)
        for m, v in conn.execute(
            text("SELECT d.month_id, sum(f.amount) FROM dwh.fact_cashflow f "
                 "JOIN dwh.dim_date d ON d.date_id = f.date_id "
                 "WHERE f.src_system = :s AND f.date_id BETWEEN :f AND :t GROUP BY d.month_id"),
            {"s": source, "f": window.from_id, "t": window.to_id},
        ).all()
    }
    mismatches = []
    for month in sorted(set(ours) | set(totals.cashflow_by_month)):
        expected = Decimal(totals.cashflow_by_month.get(month, Decimal(0)))
        actual = ours.get(month, Decimal(0))
        if abs(actual - expected) > abs(expected) * CONTROL_TOLERANCE:
            mismatches.append({"month": month, "source": str(expected), "dwh": str(actual)})
    return CheckResult("control_totals", not mismatches, {"mismatches": mismatches[:SAMPLE]})


def _balance_identity(conn: Connection, build_id: int, window: Window) -> CheckResult:
    months = window.month_ids()
    rows = conn.execute(
        text("SELECT branch_id, month_id, diff, inflow + outflow AS turnover "
             "FROM mart.agg_cash_month WHERE build_id = :b AND item_id = 0 AND branch_id <> 0 "
             "AND month_id BETWEEN :mf AND :mt AND opening_balance IS NOT NULL "
             "AND closing_balance IS NOT NULL "
             "AND abs(diff) > greatest(:tol * (inflow + outflow), 0.01) "
             "ORDER BY month_id, branch_id LIMIT :n"),
        {"b": build_id, "mf": months[0], "mt": months[-1], "tol": BALANCE_TOLERANCE, "n": SAMPLE},
    ).all()
    violations = [{"branch_id": r.branch_id, "month_id": r.month_id, "diff": str(r.diff),
                   "turnover": str(r.turnover)} for r in rows]
    return CheckResult("balance_identity", not violations, {"violations": violations})


def _error_counts(conn: Connection, run_id: int) -> dict[str, int]:
    return {
        code: int(n)
        for code, n in conn.execute(
            text("SELECT code, count(*) FROM core.etl_error WHERE run_id = :r GROUP BY code"),
            {"r": run_id},
        ).all()
    }


def _capacity(conn: Connection, window: Window) -> CheckResult:
    # generation — mln kVt·soat; ×1000 = MVt·soat; chegara capacity_mw × 24.
    rows = conn.execute(
        text("SELECT g.branch_id, g.date_id, g.generation, v.capacity_mw "
             "FROM dwh.fact_generation g "
             "JOIN dwh.dim_date d ON d.date_id = g.date_id "
             "JOIN dwh.dim_branch_version v ON v.branch_id = g.branch_id "
             "AND d.date >= v.valid_from AND (v.valid_to IS NULL OR d.date <= v.valid_to) "
             "WHERE g.date_id BETWEEN :f AND :t AND v.capacity_mw IS NOT NULL "
             "AND g.generation * 1000 > v.capacity_mw * 24 "
             "ORDER BY g.date_id, g.branch_id LIMIT :n"),
        {"f": window.from_id, "t": window.to_id, "n": SAMPLE},
    ).all()
    violations = [{"branch_id": r.branch_id, "date_id": r.date_id,
                   "generation": str(r.generation), "capacity_mw": str(r.capacity_mw)}
                  for r in rows]
    return CheckResult("capacity_limit", not violations, {"violations": violations})


def run_checks(conn: Connection, *, run_id: int, build_id: int, source: str, window: Window,
               totals: ControlTotals | None) -> list[CheckResult]:
    counts = _error_counts(conn, run_id)
    missing_branch = counts.get("missing_branch", 0)
    missing_dim = counts.get("missing_dim", 0)
    return [
        _control_totals(conn, source, window, totals),
        _balance_identity(conn, build_id, window),
        CheckResult("mandatory_dims", missing_branch == 0,
                    {"errors": missing_branch, "warnings": counts.get("missing_item", 0)}),
        _capacity(conn, window),
        # FK'lar DEFERRABLE — noto'g'ri havola commit'ga yetib bormaydi; merge ularni
        # etl_error'ga yozib o'tkazib yuboradi. Shu yerda hisobot bo'yicha yuklash yiqiladi.
        CheckResult("dangling_refs", missing_dim == 0, {"errors": missing_dim}),
    ]
```

### Task 8: ETL runner — advisory lock, pipeline, qayta urinishlar, jurnal, xabarnomalar

- [ ] **Step 1: Test yordamchilari**

`backend/tests/etl_helpers.py` — import'larga qo'shing:
```python
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from decimal import Decimal

from app.etl.notify import Notification
from app.etl.types import ControlTotals, ExtractStats, HealthResult, StagingSink, Watermark
```
va fayl oxiriga:
```python
STEP_NAMES = ("extract", "dims", "facts", "aggregates", "quality", "publish")


@dataclass
class ListConnector:
    """Oldindan berilgan qatorlarni qaytaruvchi konnektor; nosozliklarni simulyatsiya qiladi."""

    source: str
    rows: dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]] = field(default_factory=dict)
    totals: dict[int, Decimal] | None = None
    fail_times: int = 0
    error: type[Exception] = ConnectionError
    healthy: bool = True
    mark: datetime = field(default_factory=lambda: datetime(2026, 9, 24, 3, 0, tzinfo=UTC))
    calls: int = 0

    def healthcheck(self) -> HealthResult:
        return HealthResult(self.healthy, "test")

    def watermark(self) -> Watermark:
        return Watermark(self.mark)

    def extract(self, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> ExtractStats:
        self.calls += 1
        if self.calls <= self.fail_times:
            raise self.error("источник недоступен")
        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        for target, items in self.rows.items():
            counts[target] = 0
            for row in items:
                if target.startswith("fact_") and not window.contains_id(int(row["date_id"])):
                    continue
                sink.add(target, row)
                counts[target] += 1
        return ExtractStats(sum(counts.values()), counts)

    def control_totals(self, window: Window) -> ControlTotals | None:
        return None if self.totals is None else ControlTotals(self.totals)


class RecordingNotifier:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.items: list[Notification] = []

    def notify(self, notification: Notification) -> None:
        self.items.append(notification)

    @property
    def kinds(self) -> list[str]:
        return [n.kind for n in self.items]
```
