`backend/tests/test_etl_runner.py`:
```python
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import UTC, date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core.db import get_engine
from app.dwh import builds
from app.dwh.dates import today_tashkent
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.etl.retry import call_with_retries
from app.etl.runner import EtlBusyError, Runner, compute_window
from app.etl.types import Mode, Window
from tests.etl_helpers import (
    STEP_NAMES,
    TEST_AS_OF,
    ListConnector,
    RecordingNotifier,
    branch_row,
    cash_row,
    new_source,
    new_tag,
    open_run,
    purge_source,
)

HISTORY = date(2024, 1, 1)


@pytest.fixture
def source() -> Iterator[str]:
    name = new_source()
    yield name
    purge_source(name)


def _rows(tag: str) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    b = branch_row(tag)
    return {"dim_branch": [b], "fact_cashflow": [cash_row("r1", 20260805, b["code"], 100)]}


def _run(connector: ListConnector, *, mode: Mode = Mode.FULL,
         notifier: RecordingNotifier | None = None, sleeps: list[float] | None = None):
    return Runner(connector, mode=mode, today=TEST_AS_OF, notifier=notifier or RecordingNotifier(),
                  sleep=(sleeps if sleeps is not None else []).append, rng=lambda: 0.5).run()


def _active() -> int | None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        return builds.active_build(conn)


def _run_row(run_id: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        return dict(conn.execute(text("SELECT * FROM core.etl_run WHERE id = :r"),
                                 {"r": run_id}).mappings().one())


def test_today_tashkent_is_utc_plus_five() -> None:
    assert today_tashkent(datetime(2026, 9, 30, 19, 30, tzinfo=UTC)) == date(2026, 10, 1)
    assert today_tashkent(datetime(2026, 9, 30, 18, 59, tzinfo=UTC)) == date(2026, 9, 30)


def test_compute_window_rules() -> None:
    today = date(2026, 9, 24)
    full = compute_window(Mode.FULL, today=today, history_start=HISTORY,
                          last_watermark=datetime(2026, 9, 20, tzinfo=UTC))
    assert full == Window(HISTORY, today)
    assert compute_window(Mode.INCREMENTAL, today=today, history_start=HISTORY,
                          last_watermark=None) == Window(HISTORY, today)
    recent = compute_window(Mode.INCREMENTAL, today=today, history_start=HISTORY,
                            last_watermark=datetime(2026, 9, 20, 3, tzinfo=UTC))
    assert recent == Window(date(2026, 8, 6), today)
    assert compute_window(Mode.INCREMENTAL, today=date(2024, 1, 10), history_start=HISTORY,
                          last_watermark=datetime(2024, 1, 9, tzinfo=UTC)).date_from == HISTORY


def test_window_covers_gap_since_old_watermark() -> None:
    """Oxirgi muvaffaqiyatli yuklash 3 oy oldin — oraliq o'tkazib yuborilmasligi kerak."""
    window = compute_window(Mode.INCREMENTAL, today=date(2026, 9, 24), history_start=HISTORY,
                            last_watermark=datetime(2026, 6, 10, tzinfo=UTC))
    assert window.date_from == date(2026, 4, 26)


def test_call_with_retries_pause_sequence_and_jitter() -> None:
    sleeps: list[float] = []
    calls = {"n": 0}

    def flaky() -> str:
        calls["n"] += 1
        if calls["n"] < 3:
            raise TimeoutError("timeout")
        return "ok"

    assert call_with_retries(flaky, sleep=sleeps.append, rng=lambda: 1.0) == "ok"
    assert sleeps == pytest.approx([33.0, 132.0])


def test_successful_run_publishes_and_journals(source: str) -> None:
    notifier = RecordingNotifier()
    connector = ListConnector(source, _rows(new_tag()), totals={202608: Decimal("100")})
    result = _run(connector, notifier=notifier)
    assert result.status == "success" and result.build_id is not None
    assert _active() == result.build_id
    run = _run_row(result.run_id)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    assert run["rows_in"] == 2 and run["rows_upserted"] >= 2
    assert run["watermark"] == connector.mark
    assert (run["window_from"], run["window_to"]) == (HISTORY, TEST_AS_OF)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        steps = conn.execute(text("SELECT name, status FROM core.etl_step WHERE run_id = :r "
                                  "ORDER BY id"), {"r": result.run_id}).all()
        staged = conn.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM stg.rows WHERE load_id = :r"),
                              {"r": result.run_id}).scalar_one()
    assert [tuple(s) for s in steps] == [(n, "success") for n in STEP_NAMES]
    assert staged == 0
    assert notifier.items == []
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        actions = list(conn.execute(
            text("SELECT action FROM core.audit_log WHERE object_type = 'etl_run' "
                 "AND object_id = :r ORDER BY id"), {"r": str(result.run_id)}).scalars())
    assert actions == ["etl.run.started", "etl.run.finished"]


def test_quality_failure_keeps_previous_build(source: str) -> None:
    tag = new_tag()
    first = _run(ListConnector(source, _rows(tag)))
    assert first.status == "success"
    notifier = RecordingNotifier()
    bad = ListConnector(source, _rows(tag), totals={202608: Decimal("150")})
    result = _run(bad, notifier=notifier)
    assert result.status == "failed_quality"
    assert _active() == first.build_id
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        status = conn.execute(text("SELECT status FROM core.builds WHERE build_id = :b"),
                              {"b": result.build_id}).scalar_one()
        quality = conn.execute(text("SELECT status FROM core.etl_step WHERE run_id = :r "
                                    "AND name = 'quality'"), {"r": result.run_id}).scalar_one()
    assert (status, quality) == ("failed", "failed")
    assert notifier.kinds == ["etl_quality", "control_totals"]


def test_retries_with_injected_delays(source: str) -> None:
    sleeps: list[float] = []
    connector = ListConnector(source, _rows(new_tag()), fail_times=2)
    result = _run(connector, sleeps=sleeps)
    assert result.status == "success"
    assert sleeps == [30.0, 120.0]
    assert connector.calls == 3


def test_gives_up_after_three_retries(source: str) -> None:
    before = _active()
    sleeps: list[float] = []
    notifier = RecordingNotifier()
    connector = ListConnector(source, _rows(new_tag()), fail_times=10)
    result = _run(connector, sleeps=sleeps, notifier=notifier)
    assert result.status == "failed"
    assert sleeps == [30.0, 120.0, 480.0]
    assert connector.calls == 4
    assert notifier.kinds == ["etl_failed"]
    assert _active() == before
    assert "ConnectionError" in (_run_row(result.run_id)["error"] or "")  # type: ignore[arg-type]


def test_non_retryable_error_fails_immediately(source: str) -> None:
    sleeps: list[float] = []
    result = _run(ListConnector(source, _rows(new_tag()), fail_times=1, error=ValueError),
                  sleeps=sleeps)
    assert result.status == "failed" and sleeps == []
    assert "ValueError" in (result.error or "")


def test_unhealthy_source_is_retried_then_fails(source: str) -> None:
    sleeps: list[float] = []
    connector = ListConnector(source, _rows(new_tag()), healthy=False)
    result = _run(connector, sleeps=sleeps)
    assert result.status == "failed" and len(sleeps) == 3 and connector.calls == 0
    assert "ConnectorUnavailableError" in (result.error or "")


def test_concurrent_run_is_rejected(source: str) -> None:
    with get_engine("etl").connect() as other:
        other = other.execution_options(isolation_level="AUTOCOMMIT")
        other.execute(text("SELECT pg_advisory_lock(hashtext('ugk_etl'))"))
        try:
            with pytest.raises(EtlBusyError):
                _run(ListConnector(source, _rows(new_tag())))
        finally:
            other.execute(text("SELECT pg_advisory_unlock(hashtext('ugk_etl'))"))


def test_stale_running_run_is_marked_failed(source: str) -> None:
    stale = open_run(source)
    result = _run(ListConnector(source, _rows(new_tag())))
    assert result.status == "success"
    row = _run_row(stale)
    assert row["status"] == "failed"
    assert "Прервано" in row["error"]


def test_incremental_uses_last_success_watermark(source: str) -> None:
    tag = new_tag()
    mark = datetime(2026, 9, 20, 3, 0, tzinfo=UTC)
    connector = ListConnector(source, _rows(tag), mark=mark)
    assert _run(connector).status == "success"
    result = _run(connector, mode=Mode.INCREMENTAL)
    assert result.status == "success"
    run = _run_row(result.run_id)  # type: ignore[arg-type]
    assert run["window_from"] == date(2026, 8, 6)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        kept = conn.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM dwh.fact_cashflow WHERE src_system = :s"),
                            {"s": source}).scalar_one()
    assert kept == 1  # 2026-08-05 oynadan tashqarida — o'chirilmaydi
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implementatsiya — qayta urinishlar**

`backend/app/etl/retry.py`:
```python
"""Qayta urinishlar (spec 5.2, TZ 8): 3 ta, pauza 30 s → 2 min → 8 min, ±jitter.

`sleep` va `rng` injeksiya qilinadi — testlar haqiqatda uxlamaydi.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import random
import time
from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from typing import TypeVar

from sqlalchemy.exc import OperationalError

from app.etl.types import ConnectorUnavailableError

T = TypeVar("T")
RETRY_DELAYS: tuple[float, ...] = (30.0, 120.0, 480.0)
# Faqat vaqtinchalik xatolar. Ma'lumot/format xatosi (ValueError) qayta urinilmaydi.
RETRYABLE: tuple[type[BaseException], ...] = (
    ConnectionError, TimeoutError, OSError, OperationalError, ConnectorUnavailableError,
)


def call_with_retries(
    fn: Callable[[], T],
    *,
    delays: Sequence[float] = RETRY_DELAYS,
    sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
    jitter: float = 0.1,
    rng: Callable[[], float] = random.random,  # kriptografik emas
    retry_on: tuple[type[BaseException], ...] = RETRYABLE,
    on_retry: Callable[[int, BaseException, float], None] | None = None,
) -> T:
    attempt = 0
    while True:
        try:
            return fn()
        except retry_on as exc:
            if attempt >= len(delays):
                raise
            pause = delays[attempt] * (1 + jitter * (2 * rng() - 1))
            if on_retry is not None:
                on_retry(attempt + 1, exc, pause)
            sleep(pause)
            attempt += 1
```

- [ ] **Step 6: Implementatsiya — runner**

`backend/app/etl/runner.py`:
```python
"""ETL runner (spec 5.1): PostgreSQL advisory lock bilan bitta vaqtda bitta yuklash.

Pipeline: extract → dims → facts → aggregates → quality → publish. Istalgan qadam xatosi
yoki sifat buzilishi → publish yo'q, oldingi build aktiv qoladi, notifier chaqiriladi.
Har qadam core.etl_step ga, yuklash core.etl_run ga, boshlanish/tugash auditga yoziladi.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import random
import time
from collections.abc import Callable, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date, datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import text

from app.audit.service import AuditAction, AuditEvent, record
from app.core.db import get_engine
from app.core.logging import get_logger
from app.dwh import builds
from app.dwh.aggregates import BuildParams, build_all
from app.dwh.dates import TASHKENT, add_months, month_start, today_tashkent
from app.dwh.reference import current_setting
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.etl.merge import MergeStats, merge_dims, merge_facts
from app.etl.notify import LogNotifier, Notification, Notifier
from app.etl.quality import CheckResult, failed, run_checks
from app.etl.retry import RETRY_DELAYS, call_with_retries
from app.etl.staging import DbStagingSink, clear_load
from app.etl.types import (
    ConnectorUnavailableError,
    ExtractStats,
    Mode,
    SourceConnector,
    Watermark,
    Window,
)

ETL_LOCK = "ugk_etl"
INCREMENTAL_LOOKBACK = timedelta(days=45)
AGGREGATE_YEARS = 10  # spec 4.7: agregatlar 10 yil saqlanadi
log = get_logger("ugekazna.etl")


class EtlBusyError(Exception):
    """Boshqa yuklash bajarilmoqda."""


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RunResult:
    run_id: int | None
    status: str
    build_id: int | None = None
    error: str | None = None


@dataclass
class StepResult:
    rows: int = 0
    detail: dict[str, Any] = field(default_factory=dict)
    value: Any = None
    ok: bool = True


def compute_window(mode: Mode, *, today: date, history_start: date,
                   last_watermark: datetime | None) -> Window:
    """To'liq: tarix boshidan. Inkremental: min(watermark − 45 kun, joriy oy boshi) — TZ:
    "последние 45 дней плюс текущий месяц целиком"; eski watermark oraliqni ham qamraydi."""
    if mode == Mode.FULL or last_watermark is None:
        return Window(history_start, today)
    wm_day = last_watermark.astimezone(TASHKENT).date()
    start = min(wm_day - INCREMENTAL_LOOKBACK, month_start(today))
    return Window(max(start, history_start), today)


def _describe(exc: BaseException) -> str:
    return f"{type(exc).__name__}: {str(exc)[:400]}"


def _merge_result(stats: list[MergeStats]) -> StepResult:
    detail = {
        s.target: {"upserted": s.upserted, "deleted": s.deleted, "errors": s.errors,
                   "warnings": s.warnings}
        for s in stats
    }
    return StepResult(sum(s.upserted for s in stats), detail, stats)


class Runner:
    def __init__(
        self,
        connector: SourceConnector,
        *,
        mode: Mode = Mode.INCREMENTAL,
        notifier: Notifier | None = None,
        today: date | None = None,
        sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
        delays: Sequence[float] = RETRY_DELAYS,
        jitter: float = 0.1,
        rng: Callable[[], float] = random.random,  # kriptografik emas
        triggered_by: int | None = None,
    ) -> None:
        self.connector = connector
        self.mode = mode
        self.notifier = notifier or LogNotifier()
        self.today = today or today_tashkent()
        self.sleep = sleep
        self.delays = tuple(delays)
        self.jitter = jitter
        self.rng = rng
        self.triggered_by = triggered_by

    # ── Kirish nuqtasi ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    def run(self) -> RunResult:
        with get_engine("etl").connect() as raw:
            lock = raw.execution_options(isolation_level="AUTOCOMMIT")
            got = lock.execute(text("SELECT pg_try_advisory_lock(hashtext(:k))"),
                               {"k": ETL_LOCK}).scalar_one()
            if not got:
                raise EtlBusyError()
            try:
                return self._run_locked()
            finally:
                lock.execute(text("SELECT pg_advisory_unlock(hashtext(:k))"), {"k": ETL_LOCK})

    # ── Pipeline ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _run_locked(self) -> RunResult:
        source = self.connector.source
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            # Lock bizda — "running" holatidagi har qanday yugurish o'lik (worker to'xtagan).
            conn.execute(text(
                "UPDATE core.etl_run SET status = 'failed', finished_at = now(), "
                "error = 'Прервано: процесс загрузки был остановлен' WHERE status = 'running'"))
            history_start = date.fromisoformat(str(current_setting(conn, "etl.history_start")))
            keep = int(current_setting(conn, "builds.keep"))
            fact_years = int(current_setting(conn, "retention.fact_years"))
            last = conn.execute(text(
                "SELECT watermark FROM core.etl_run WHERE source = :s AND status = 'success' "
                "AND watermark IS NOT NULL ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1"), {"s": source}).scalar()
            window = compute_window(self.mode, today=self.today, history_start=history_start,
                                    last_watermark=last)
            run_id = int(conn.execute(text(
                "INSERT INTO core.etl_run (source, mode, status, window_from, window_to, "
                "triggered_by) VALUES (:s, :m, 'running', :f, :t, :u) RETURNING id"),
                {"s": source, "m": self.mode.value, "f": window.date_from, "t": window.date_to,
                 "u": self.triggered_by}).scalar_one())
        self._audit(AuditAction.ETL_RUN_STARTED, run_id, "ok",
                    {"source": source, "mode": self.mode.value,
                     "window": [window.date_from.isoformat(), window.date_to.isoformat()]})

        build_id: int | None = None
        rows_in = rows_upserted = 0
        try:
            watermark: Watermark = self.connector.watermark()
            extract = self._step(run_id, "extract", lambda: self._extract(run_id, window))
            stats: ExtractStats = extract.value
            rows_in = stats.rows
            dims = self._step(run_id, "dims", lambda: self._dims(run_id))
            facts = self._step(run_id, "facts",
                               lambda: self._facts(run_id, window, stats.by_target.keys()))
            rows_upserted = dims.rows + facts.rows
            params = BuildParams(
                as_of=self.today,
                # Faktlar `fact_years` yil saqlanadi; undan eski oylar oldingi build'dan.
                date_from=max(history_start,
                              add_months(month_start(self.today), -12 * fact_years)),
                keep_from=add_months(month_start(self.today), -12 * AGGREGATE_YEARS),
            )
            aggregates = self._step(run_id, "aggregates",
                                    lambda: self._aggregates(run_id, params))
            build_id = int(aggregates.value)
            quality = self._step(run_id, "quality",
                                 lambda: self._quality(run_id, int(aggregates.value), window))
            bad: list[CheckResult] = quality.value
            if bad:
                return self._fail_quality(run_id, build_id, bad, rows_in, rows_upserted)
            self._step(run_id, "publish", lambda: self._publish(run_id, int(aggregates.value),
                                                                keep))
        except Exception as exc:  # yuklash xatosi jurnalga yoziladi, qayta ko'tarilmaydi
            log.error("etl_failed", run_id=run_id, source=source, error=type(exc).__name__,
                      exc_info=exc)
            if build_id is not None:
                self._safe_mark_failed(build_id)
            error = _describe(exc)
            self._finish(run_id, "failed", build_id, rows_in, rows_upserted, None, error)
            self.notifier.notify(Notification(
                "etl_failed", f"Загрузка «{source}» завершилась ошибкой",
                f"Запуск №{run_id}, режим {self.mode.value}.\nОшибка: {error}"))
            return RunResult(run_id, "failed", build_id, error)
        self._finish(run_id, "success", build_id, rows_in, rows_upserted, watermark.value, None)
        return RunResult(run_id, "success", build_id)

    def _step(self, run_id: int, name: str, fn: Callable[[], StepResult]) -> StepResult:
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            step_id = conn.execute(text(
                "INSERT INTO core.etl_step (run_id, name, status) VALUES (:r, :n, 'running') "
                "RETURNING id"), {"r": run_id, "n": name}).scalar_one()
        try:
            result = fn()
        except Exception as exc:
            with etl_transaction() as conn:
                conn.execute(text(
                    "UPDATE core.etl_step SET status = 'failed', finished_at = now(), "
                    "detail = CAST(:d AS jsonb) WHERE id = :i"),
                    {"i": step_id, "d": json.dumps({"error": _describe(exc)}, ensure_ascii=False)})
            raise
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            conn.execute(text(
                "UPDATE core.etl_step SET status = :st, finished_at = now(), rows = :n, "
                "detail = CAST(:d AS jsonb) WHERE id = :i"),
                {"i": step_id, "st": "success" if result.ok else "failed", "n": result.rows,
                 "d": json.dumps(result.detail, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)})
        return result

    def _extract(self, run_id: int, window: Window) -> StepResult:
        retries: list[dict[str, Any]] = []

        def once() -> ExtractStats:
            health = self.connector.healthcheck()
            if not health.ok:
                raise ConnectorUnavailableError(health.detail or "источник недоступен")
            with etl_transaction() as conn:
                clear_load(conn, run_id)
                sink = DbStagingSink(conn, run_id, self.connector.source)
                stats = self.connector.extract(window, sink)
                sink.close()
            return stats

        def on_retry(attempt: int, exc: BaseException, pause: float) -> None:
            retries.append({"attempt": attempt, "error": _describe(exc), "pause": round(pause, 1)})
            log.warning("etl_retry", run_id=run_id, attempt=attempt, pause=round(pause, 1),
                        error=type(exc).__name__)

        stats = call_with_retries(once, delays=self.delays, sleep=self.sleep, jitter=self.jitter,
                                  rng=self.rng, on_retry=on_retry)
        return StepResult(stats.rows, {"by_target": dict(stats.by_target), "retries": retries},
                          stats)

    def _dims(self, run_id: int) -> StepResult:
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            return _merge_result(merge_dims(conn, load_id=run_id, source=self.connector.source,
                                            as_of=self.today))

    def _facts(self, run_id: int, window: Window, complete: Any) -> StepResult:
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            return _merge_result(merge_facts(conn, load_id=run_id, source=self.connector.source,
                                             window=window, complete=set(complete),
                                             as_of=self.today))

    def _aggregates(self, run_id: int, params: BuildParams) -> StepResult:
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            build_id = builds.create_build(conn, run_id)
            counts = build_all(conn, build_id, params)
            builds.mark_ready(conn, build_id)
        return StepResult(sum(counts.values()), {"build_id": build_id, **counts}, build_id)

    def _quality(self, run_id: int, build_id: int, window: Window) -> StepResult:
        totals = self.connector.control_totals(window)
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            results = run_checks(conn, run_id=run_id, build_id=build_id,
                                 source=self.connector.source, window=window, totals=totals)
        bad = failed(results)
        detail = {r.code: {"ok": r.ok, **r.detail} for r in results}
        return StepResult(len(bad), detail, bad, ok=not bad)

    def _publish(self, run_id: int, build_id: int, keep: int) -> StepResult:
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            builds.publish(conn, build_id, keep=keep)
            conn.execute(text("DELETE FROM stg.rows WHERE load_id = :l"), {"l": run_id})
        return StepResult(0, {"build_id": build_id})

    # ── Yakunlash ───────────────────────────────────────────────────
    def _fail_quality(self, run_id: int, build_id: int, bad: list[CheckResult], rows_in: int,
                      rows_upserted: int) -> RunResult:
        self._safe_mark_failed(build_id)
        codes = ", ".join(r.code for r in bad)
        error = f"Проверки качества не пройдены: {codes}"
        self._finish(run_id, "failed_quality", build_id, rows_in, rows_upserted, None, error)
        source = self.connector.source
        self.notifier.notify(Notification(
            "etl_quality", f"Загрузка «{source}» не опубликована: проверки качества",
            f"Запуск №{run_id}. {error}.\n"
            + json.dumps({r.code: r.detail for r in bad}, ensure_ascii=False, default=str)[:3000]))
        if any(r.code == "control_totals" for r in bad):
            self.notifier.notify(Notification(
                "control_totals", f"Расхождение контрольных сумм с источником «{source}»",
                f"Запуск №{run_id}: отклонение от оборотов 1С более 0,1%."))
        return RunResult(run_id, "failed_quality", build_id, error)

    def _safe_mark_failed(self, build_id: int) -> None:
        try:
            with etl_transaction() as conn:
                builds.mark_failed(conn, build_id)
        except Exception as exc:
            log.error("build_mark_failed_error", build_id=build_id, error=type(exc).__name__)

    def _finish(self, run_id: int, status: str, build_id: int | None, rows_in: int,
                rows_upserted: int, watermark: datetime | None, error: str | None) -> None:
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            conn.execute(text(
                "UPDATE core.etl_run SET status = :st, finished_at = now(), build_id = :b, "
                "rows_in = :ri, rows_upserted = :ru, watermark = :w, error = :e WHERE id = :r"),
                {"st": status, "b": build_id, "ri": rows_in, "ru": rows_upserted,
                 "w": watermark, "e": error, "r": run_id})
        self._audit(AuditAction.ETL_RUN_FINISHED, run_id, "ok" if status == "success" else "fail",
                    {"source": self.connector.source, "status": status, "build_id": build_id,
                     "rows_upserted": rows_upserted})

    def _audit(self, action: AuditAction, run_id: int, result: str,
               details: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
        record(AuditEvent(action=action, result=result, user_id=self.triggered_by,
                          object_type="etl_run", object_id=str(run_id), details=details))
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `Window`, `ExtractStats`, `ControlTotals`, `HealthResult`, `Watermark`, `StagingSink`, `app.dwh.dates.*` (`TASHKENT`, `today_tashkent`), `get_settings().synthetic_seed`, `Runner`, `Mode`, `builds`, `build_all`, `BuildParams`.
- Produces:

  - `app.etl.synthetic.rng.rnd(seed, *parts) -> random.Random` (sha256 asosidagi deterministik oqim).
  - `app.etl.synthetic.model`: `HISTORY_START = date(2024, 1, 1)`, `money(x) -> Decimal`, `workdays(month) -> list[date]`, `Posting`, `BalanceRow`, `ContractRef`, `CashModel(postings, full_amounts, balances)`, `SynthModel(seed, as_of, history_start=HISTORY_START)` + `.months()`, `.period_end(month)`, `.bank_shares`, `.contracts`, `.cash`.
  - `app.etl.synthetic.families`: `Family = Callable[[SynthModel, Window, StagingSink], dict[str, int]]`, `FAMILIES: list[tuple[str, Family]]` (`dims`, `fx`, `cash`, `payments`, `debt`, `funds`) — keyingi rejalar o'z oilasini shu ro'yxatga qo'shadi.
  - `app.etl.synthetic.SyntheticConnector(*, seed=None, as_of=None, history_start=HISTORY_START, source="synthetic")` — `SourceConnector` protokoli.

- [ ] **Step 1: Testlarni yozish**

`backend/tests/test_synthetic.py`:
```python
from collections import defaultdict
from collections.abc import Mapping
from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import text

from app.dwh.dates import date_id, month_id
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.etl.runner import RunResult, Runner
from app.etl.synthetic import SyntheticConnector
from app.etl.synthetic.model import HISTORY_START, SynthModel
from app.etl.synthetic.reference import (
    BANKS,
    BRANCHES,
    EXPENSE_ITEMS,
    FUNDS,
    INCOME_ITEMS,
    PO_STATUSES,
)
from app.etl.types import Mode, Window
from tests.etl_helpers import TEST_AS_OF


class CollectSink:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.rows: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]] = []

    def add(self, target: str, row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
        self.rows.append((target, dict(row)))


def test_reference_mirrors_prototype() -> None:
    assert [b.id for b in BRANCHES] == list(range(1, 17))
    assert BRANCHES[0].kind == "head" and BRANCHES[0].weight == 0
    capacity = {b.id: b.capacity for b in BRANCHES}
    assert (capacity[2], capacity[13], capacity[16]) == (666, 175, 21)
    assert len(BANKS) == 5 and len(INCOME_ITEMS) == 6 and len(EXPENSE_ITEMS) == 9
    assert [s for s, _ in PO_STATUSES] == [
        "черновик", "на согласовании", "утверждено", "отправлено в банк", "исполнено", "отклонено"]
    assert abs(sum(w for _, w in PO_STATUSES) - 1) < 1e-9
    assert len(FUNDS) == 4
    assert {e.activity for e in EXPENSE_ITEMS} == {"centralized", "operating", "investing"}
    assert all(i.code.startswith("PKM54-") for i in (*INCOME_ITEMS, *EXPENSE_ITEMS))


def _august(model: SynthModel) -> list[Any]:
    return [p for p in model.cash.postings if (p.day.year, p.day.month) == (2026, 8)]


def test_generation_is_deterministic_and_independent_of_as_of() -> None:
    a = SynthModel(7, date(2026, 9, 24))
    b = SynthModel(7, date(2026, 9, 30))
    assert _august(a) and _august(a) == _august(b)
    assert _august(SynthModel(8, date(2026, 9, 24))) != _august(a)


def test_current_month_is_partial() -> None:
    model = SynthModel(7, date(2026, 9, 10))
    assert max(p.day for p in model.cash.postings) <= date(2026, 9, 10)
    assert max(r.day for r in model.cash.balances) == date(2026, 9, 10)


def test_balance_identity_holds_in_model() -> None:
    model = SynthModel(7, TEST_AS_OF)
    totals: dict[tuple[int, date], Decimal] = defaultdict(Decimal)
    for row in model.cash.balances:
        totals[(row.branch_id, row.day)] += row.balance
    net: dict[tuple[int, int], Decimal] = defaultdict(Decimal)
    turnover: dict[tuple[int, int], Decimal] = defaultdict(Decimal)
    for p in model.cash.postings:
        net[(p.branch_id, month_id(p.day))] += p.amount if p.direction == "in" else -p.amount
        turnover[(p.branch_id, month_id(p.day))] += p.amount
    for branch in BRANCHES:
        days = sorted(d for (b, d) in totals if b == branch.id)
        for prev, cur in zip(days, days[1:], strict=False):
            key = (branch.id, month_id(cur))
            diff = totals[(branch.id, cur)] - totals[(branch.id, prev)] - net[key]
            assert abs(diff) <= turnover[key] * Decimal("0.004") + Decimal("0.05"), (branch.id, cur)


def test_extract_respects_window_and_reports_all_targets() -> None:
    connector = SyntheticConnector(seed=7, as_of=TEST_AS_OF)
    window = Window(date(2026, 8, 1), TEST_AS_OF)
    sink = CollectSink()
    stats = connector.extract(window, sink)
    assert set(stats.by_target) == {
        "dim_branch", "dim_item", "dim_counterparty", "dim_bank", "dim_nomenclature",
        "dim_source", "dim_object", "dim_contract", "dim_loan", "dim_position", "fx_rate",
        "fact_cashflow", "fact_balance", "fact_payment_plan", "fact_payment_order", "fact_debt",
        "fact_target_funds",
    }
    assert stats.by_target["dim_branch"] == 16 and stats.by_target["dim_bank"] == 80
    assert stats.rows == len(sink.rows)
    for target, row in sink.rows:
        if target == "fact_balance":
            assert date_id(date(2026, 7, 31)) <= row["date_id"] <= window.to_id
        elif target.startswith("fact_") or target == "fx_rate":
            assert window.contains_id(row["date_id"]), (target, row)
    totals = connector.control_totals(window)
    assert totals is not None and sorted(totals.cashflow_by_month) == [202608, 202609]


def test_full_synthetic_pipeline(synthetic_run: RunResult) -> None:
    assert synthetic_run.status == "success"
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        branches = conn.execute(text(
            "SELECT count(*) FROM dwh.dim_branch WHERE src_system = 'synthetic'")).scalar_one()
        cash = conn.execute(text(
            "SELECT count(*) FROM dwh.fact_cashflow WHERE src_system = 'synthetic'")).scalar_one()
        encrypted = conn.execute(text(
            "SELECT count(*) FROM dwh.dim_counterparty WHERE src_system = 'synthetic' "
            "AND inn_enc IS NOT NULL AND inn_bidx IS NOT NULL")).scalar_one()
        statuses = set(conn.execute(text(
            "SELECT DISTINCT status FROM dwh.fact_payment_order "
            "WHERE src_system = 'synthetic'")).scalars())
        quality = conn.execute(text(
            "SELECT detail FROM core.etl_step WHERE run_id = :r AND name = 'quality'"),
            {"r": synthetic_run.run_id}).scalar_one()
        overdue = conn.execute(text(
            "SELECT count(*) FROM dwh.fact_debt WHERE src_system = 'synthetic' "
            "AND overdue_days > 90")).scalar_one()
    assert branches == 16 and encrypted == 16
    assert cash > 5000
    assert statuses == {s for s, _ in PO_STATUSES}
    assert all(v["ok"] for v in quality.values())
    assert overdue > 0  # «Просроченные договоры > 90 дней» qoidasi uchun ma'lumot bor


def test_incremental_rerun_changes_nothing(synthetic_run: RunResult) -> None:
    result = Runner(SyntheticConnector(seed=7, as_of=TEST_AS_OF, source="synthetic"),
                    mode=Mode.INCREMENTAL, today=TEST_AS_OF, sleep=lambda _: None).run()
    assert result.status == "success"
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        run = conn.execute(text("SELECT window_from, rows_upserted FROM core.etl_run "
                                "WHERE id = :r"), {"r": result.run_id}).one()
    assert run.window_from == date(2026, 8, 10)
    assert run.rows_upserted == 0
    assert HISTORY_START == date(2024, 1, 1)
```

`test_incremental_rerun_changes_nothing` `synthetic_run` bilan bir xil seed'ni ishlatishi uchun fixture seed'i `7` (quyidagi conftest).

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — ma'lumotnoma va RNG**

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/reference.py`:
```python
"""Sintetik ma'lumot ma'lumotnomalari — tasdiqlangan prototip bilan bir xil ("reference",
"cash flow data", "2025", "treasury data", CAP va LOANS). Summalar mln so'm/oy.

Prototipda filial indeksi 0..15; bu yerda id = indeks + 1 (0 — mart'dagi "hammasi").
Statya kodlari PKM54-… — vaqtinchalik; haqiqiy ПКМ №54 klassifikatori (TZ ochiq savoli)
kelganda almashtiriladi.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BranchRef:
    id: int
    code: str
    name: str
    short: str
    kind: str
    capacity: int | None
    lat: float
    lon: float
    weight: float


BRANCHES: tuple[BranchRef, ...] = (
    BranchRef(1, "UGE-01", "АО «Узбекгидроэнерго»", "Узбекгидроэнерго", "head", None,
              41.3111, 69.2797, 0.0),
    BranchRef(2, "UGE-02", "Чарвакская ГЭС", "Чарвак", "ges", 666, 41.6260, 70.0290, 1.3),
    BranchRef(3, "UGE-03", "Каскад Чирчикских ГЭС", "Чирчик", "cascade", 190, 41.4700, 69.5800,
              1.1),
    BranchRef(4, "UGE-04", "Андижанская ГЭС", "Андижан", "ges", 190, 40.7700, 73.0800, 0.9),
    BranchRef(5, "UGE-05", "Фархадская ГЭС", "Фархад", "ges", 126, 40.2300, 69.1300, 0.8),
    BranchRef(6, "UGE-06", "Туямуюнская ГЭС", "Туямуюн", "ges", 150, 41.2400, 61.4100, 0.8),
    BranchRef(7, "UGE-07", "Каскад Кадырьинских ГЭС", "Кадырья", "cascade", 44, 41.3300,
              69.4200, 0.8),
    BranchRef(8, "UGE-08", "Ходжикентская ГЭС", "Ходжикент", "ges", 165, 41.5700, 69.8900, 0.7),
    BranchRef(9, "UGE-09", "Каскад Ташкентских ГЭС", "Ташкент", "cascade", 29, 41.2900, 69.2500,
              0.7),
    BranchRef(10, "UGE-10", "Каскад Самаркандских ГЭС", "Самарканд", "cascade", 36, 39.6600,
              66.9800, 0.7),
    BranchRef(11, "UGE-11", "Газалкентская ГЭС", "Газалкент", "ges", 120, 41.5600, 69.7700, 0.6),
    BranchRef(12, "UGE-12", "Каскад Нижнебозсуйских ГЭС", "Нижний Бозсу", "cascade", 50,
              41.1200, 69.2000, 0.6),
    BranchRef(13, "UGE-13", "Тупалангская ГЭС", "Тупаланг", "ges", 175, 38.5200, 67.9200, 0.6),
    BranchRef(14, "UGE-14", "Каскад Шахриханских ГЭС", "Шахрихан", "cascade", 20, 40.7100,
              72.0500, 0.5),
    BranchRef(15, "UGE-15", "Гиссаракская ГЭС", "Гиссарак", "ges", 45, 38.9600, 67.2600, 0.4),
    BranchRef(16, "UGE-16", "Ахангаранская ГЭС", "Ахангаран", "ges", 21, 40.9500, 70.0700, 0.4),
)
HQ_ID = 1
BRANCH_BY_ID = {b.id: b for b in BRANCHES}
WEIGHT_SUM = sum(b.weight for b in BRANCHES)
BANKS = ("Узпромстройбанк", "Национальный банк", "Асакабанк", "Ипотека-банк", "Халк банк")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CashItem:
    code: str
    name: str
    direction: str  # in | out
    base: float  # butun kompaniya bo'yicha oylik, mln so'm
    hq_share: float  # bosh tashkilot ulushi (prototip `hq`)
    activity: str  # centralized | operating | investing
    lo: float = 0.8  # oylik tebranish diapazoni
    hi: float = 1.2
    lumpy: bool = False  # tartibsiz tushumlar (kredit, byudjet)


INCOME_ITEMS: tuple[CashItem, ...] = (
    CashItem("PKM54-I01", "Реализация электроэнергии", "in", 520000, 0.25, "operating", 0.9, 1.1),

             "investing", lumpy=True),

def share(branch: BranchRef, hq_share: float) -> float:
    """Prototip `share()`: HQ — o'z ulushi, stansiyalar — qolgani vazn bo'yicha."""
    if branch.id == HQ_ID:
        return hq_share
    return (1 - hq_share) * branch.weight / WEIGHT_SUM
```

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/rng.py`:
```python
"""Deterministik tasodifiy oqimlar: bir xil (seed, kalit) → bir xil qiymatlar.

Har (filial, statya, oy) o'z oqimiga ega — shuning uchun o'tgan oylar ma'lumoti `as_of`
sanasiga bog'liq emas (inkremental qayta yuklash hech narsani o'zgartirmaydi).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import random


def rnd(seed: int, *parts: object) -> random.Random:
    key = ":".join(str(p) for p in (seed, *parts)).encode()
    return random.Random(int.from_bytes(hashlib.sha256(key).digest()[:8], "big"))
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implementatsiya — model (pul oqimi, qoldiqlar, shartnomalar)**

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/model.py`:
```python
"""Sintetik g'aznachilik modeli: bank проводкалари, hisob qoldiqlari, shartnomalar.

Qoldiqlar проводкалардan hisoblanadi: yopilish = ochilish + ЧДП + «Разница», bu yerda
|Разница| ≤ 0,4% oborot (prototip `diff`) — sifat tekshiruvi 2 har doim o'tadi.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal
from functools import cached_property
from random import Random

from app.dwh.dates import add_months, month_end, month_id, month_start
from app.etl.synthetic.reference import (
    BANKS,
    BRANCHES,
    EXPENSE_ITEMS,
    HQ_ID,
    INCOME_ITEMS,
    OBJECTS,
    OTHER_COUNTERPARTIES,
    SUPPLIER_CODES,
    BranchRef,
    CashItem,
    share,
)
from app.etl.synthetic.rng import rnd

HISTORY_START = date(2024, 1, 1)
CONTRACT_EPOCH = date(2024, 1, 1)  # shartnoma sanalari as_of'ga bog'liq bo'lmasligi uchun
YEAR_FACTOR = {2024: 0.82, 2025: 0.91}  # 2026 = 1.0 (prototip "2025" ≈ 0,86–0,96)


def money(value: float | Decimal) -> Decimal:
    return Decimal(f"{float(value):.2f}")


def workdays(month: date) -> list[date]:
    days = (month.replace(day=d) for d in range(1, month_end(month).day + 1))
    return [d for d in days if d.weekday() < 5]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Posting:
    branch_id: int
    item_code: str
    direction: str
    day: date
    amount: Decimal
    bank: int
    cp_code: str | None
    contract_code: str | None
    gl_account: str
    src_id: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BalanceRow:
    branch_id: int
    bank: int
    day: date
    balance: Decimal


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ContractRef:
    code: str
    number: str
    branch_id: int
    cp_code: str
    kind: str  # receivable | payable
    contract_date: date
    end_date: date
    due_date: date
    amount: Decimal
    late: bool
    overdue_ratio: float
    object_code: str | None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CashModel:
    postings: list[Posting]
    full_amounts: dict[tuple[int, int, str], float]  # (filial, oy, statya) → to'liq oy summasi
    balances: list[BalanceRow]


def _season(seed: int, item: CashItem, ym: int) -> float:
    r = rnd(seed, "season", item.code, ym)
    if item.lumpy:
        return 0.0 if r.random() < 0.3 else r.uniform(0.6, 1.9)
    return r.uniform(item.lo, item.hi)


def _counterparty(r: Random, item: CashItem, branch: BranchRef) -> str | None:
    if item.direction == "out":
        return r.choice(SUPPLIER_CODES)
    if item.code == "PKM54-I01":
        return "CP-013" if branch.id == HQ_ID else r.choice(("CP-013", "CP-014"))
    return {"PKM54-I02": "CP-016", "PKM54-I03": "CP-015"}.get(item.code)


def _gl_account(item: CashItem) -> str:
    # 5210 — valyuta hisobi (XMI kreditlari), 5530 — maxsus hisob (byudjet), qolgani 5110.
    return {"PKM54-I02": "5210", "PKM54-I03": "5530"}.get(item.code, "5110")


class SynthModel:
    def __init__(self, seed: int, as_of: date, history_start: date = HISTORY_START) -> None:
        self.seed = seed
        self.as_of = as_of
        self.history_start = history_start

    def months(self) -> list[date]:
        out: list[date] = []
        cur = month_start(self.history_start)
        while cur <= self.as_of:
            out.append(cur)
            cur = add_months(cur, 1)
        return out

    def period_end(self, month: date) -> date:
        return min(month_end(month), self.as_of)

    @cached_property
    def bank_shares(self) -> dict[int, list[float]]:
        out: dict[int, list[float]] = {}
        for branch in BRANCHES:
            r = rnd(self.seed, "bank", branch.id)
            raw = [r.random() ** 2 + 0.05 for _ in BANKS]
            total = sum(raw)
            out[branch.id] = [x / total for x in raw]
        return out

    @cached_property
    def contracts(self) -> list[ContractRef]:
        out: list[ContractRef] = []
        for branch in BRANCHES:
            monthly_out = sum(e.base * share(branch, e.hq_share) for e in EXPENSE_ITEMS)
            objects = [f"OBJ-{i + 1:02d}" for i, o in enumerate(OBJECTS) if o[1] + 1 == branch.id]
            for n in range(1, 4):
                r = rnd(self.seed, "contract", branch.id, n)
                kind = "receivable" if r.random() < 0.45 else "payable"
                if kind == "payable" or branch.id == HQ_ID:
                    cp = r.choice(SUPPLIER_CODES)
                else:
                    cp = r.choice(tuple(c for c, _, k in OTHER_COUNTERPARTIES if k == "buyer"))
                signed = CONTRACT_EPOCH + timedelta(days=r.randint(0, 900))
                with_object = kind == "payable" and bool(objects) and r.random() < 0.5
                obj = r.choice(objects) if with_object else None
                out.append(ContractRef(
                    code=f"CTR-{branch.id:02d}-{n}",
                    number=f"№ {r.randint(12, 480)}/{signed.year}",
                    branch_id=branch.id,
                    cp_code=cp,
                    kind=kind,
                    contract_date=signed,
                    end_date=signed + timedelta(days=r.randint(180, 720)),
                    due_date=signed + timedelta(days=r.randint(60, 240)),
                    amount=money(monthly_out * r.uniform(0.06, 0.28)),
                    late=r.random() < 0.45,
                    overdue_ratio=r.uniform(0.3, 0.9),
                    object_code=obj,
                ))
        return out

    @cached_property
    def cash(self) -> CashModel:
        postings: list[Posting] = []
        full: dict[tuple[int, int, str], float] = {}
        for branch in BRANCHES:
            payables = [c for c in self.contracts
                        if c.branch_id == branch.id and c.kind == "payable"]
            for month in self.months():
                ym = month_id(month)
                factor = YEAR_FACTOR.get(month.year, 1.0)
                for item in (*INCOME_ITEMS, *EXPENSE_ITEMS):
                    item_share = share(branch, item.hq_share)
                    if item_share <= 0:
                        continue
                    r = rnd(self.seed, "amt", branch.id, item.code, ym)
                    amount = (item.base * item_share * _season(self.seed, item, ym) * factor
                              * r.uniform(0.9, 1.1))
                    if amount < 1:
                        continue
                    full[(branch.id, ym, item.code)] = amount
                    postings.extend(self._split(branch, item, month, amount, payables))
        postings.sort(key=lambda p: (p.day, p.src_id))
        return CashModel(postings, full, self._balances(postings))

    def _split(self, branch: BranchRef, item: CashItem, month: date, amount: float,
               payables: list[ContractRef]) -> list[Posting]:
        ym = month_id(month)
        r = rnd(self.seed, "post", branch.id, item.code, ym)
        count = 1 if item.lumpy else r.randint(1, 4)
        days = sorted(r.sample(workdays(month), count))
        weights = [r.uniform(0.5, 1.5) for _ in days]
        parts = [money(amount * w / sum(weights)) for w in weights]
        parts[-1] = money(amount) - sum(parts[:-1], Decimal(0))  # yaxlitlash qoldig'i
        shares = self.bank_shares[branch.id]
        out: list[Posting] = []
        for n, (day, value) in enumerate(zip(days, parts, strict=True), start=1):
            # Tasodifiy qiymatlar filtrdan OLDIN olinadi — o'tgan oylar as_of'ga bog'liq emas.
            bank = r.choices(range(len(BANKS)), weights=shares)[0]
            cp = _counterparty(r, item, branch)
            eligible = [c.code for c in payables if c.contract_date <= day]
            contract = (r.choice(eligible)
                        if item.code in ("PKM54-O01", "PKM54-O02") and eligible else None)
            if day > self.as_of or value <= 0:
                continue
            out.append(Posting(branch.id, item.code, item.direction, day, value, bank, cp,
                               contract, _gl_account(item),
                               f"CF-{branch.id:02d}-{item.code}-{ym}-{n}"))
        return out

    def _balances(self, postings: list[Posting]) -> list[BalanceRow]:
        net: dict[tuple[int, int, int], Decimal] = defaultdict(Decimal)
        turnover: dict[tuple[int, int], Decimal] = defaultdict(Decimal)
        for p in postings:
            ym = month_id(p.day)
            net[(p.branch_id, p.bank, ym)] += p.amount if p.direction == "in" else -p.amount
            turnover[(p.branch_id, ym)] += p.amount
        opening_day = self.history_start - timedelta(days=1)
        rows: list[BalanceRow] = []
        for branch in BRANCHES:
            r0 = rnd(self.seed, "open", branch.id)
            open0 = (r0.uniform(820000, 900000) if branch.id == HQ_ID
                     else r0.uniform(18000, 32000) * branch.weight)
            shares = self.bank_shares[branch.id]
            balances = [money(open0 * s) for s in shares]
            main = max(range(len(shares)), key=shares.__getitem__)
            rows += [BalanceRow(branch.id, k, opening_day, balances[k]) for k in range(len(BANKS))]
            for month in self.months():
                ym = month_id(month)
                for k in range(len(BANKS)):
                    balances[k] += net.get((branch.id, k, ym), Decimal(0))
                rd = rnd(self.seed, "diff", branch.id, ym)
                ratio = -rd.uniform(0, 0.004) if rd.random() < 0.75 else rd.uniform(0, 0.0015)
                balances[main] += money(float(turnover.get((branch.id, ym), Decimal(0))) * ratio)
                end = self.period_end(month)
                rows += [BalanceRow(branch.id, k, end, balances[k]) for k in range(len(BANKS))]
        return rows
```

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/dims.py`:
```python
"""Barcha o'lchovlar (oyna ularga ta'sir qilmaydi — har yuklashda to'liq)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

from app.etl.synthetic.model import SynthModel, money
from app.etl.synthetic.reference import (
    BANKS,
    BRANCH_BY_ID,
    BRANCHES,
    EXPENSE_ITEMS,
    FUNDS,
    INCOME_ITEMS,
    LOANS,
    NOMENCLATURE,
    OBJECTS,
    OTHER_COUNTERPARTIES,
    POSITIONS,
    STOCK_CATEGORIES,
    SUPPLIER_CODES,
    SUPPLIERS,
    WORK_TYPES,
)
from app.etl.types import StagingSink, Window


def generate(model: SynthModel, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> dict[str, int]:
    counts: dict[str, int] = {}

    def emit(target: str, rows: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> None:
        for row in rows:
            sink.add(target, row)
        counts[target] = len(rows)

    emit("dim_branch", [
        {"code": b.code, "name": b.name, "short_name": b.short, "inn": f"2011{b.id:05d}",
         "kind": b.kind, "capacity_mw": b.capacity, "lat": b.lat, "lon": b.lon,
         "sort_order": b.id}
        for b in BRANCHES
    ])
    emit("dim_item", [
        {"code": i.code, "name": i.name, "activity": i.activity, "direction": i.direction,
         "sort_order": (100 if i.direction == "in" else 200) + n}
        for items in (INCOME_ITEMS, EXPENSE_ITEMS) for n, i in enumerate(items, start=1)
    ])
    counterparties = [(c, n, "supplier") for c, n in zip(SUPPLIER_CODES, SUPPLIERS, strict=True)]
    counterparties += list(OTHER_COUNTERPARTIES)
    emit("dim_counterparty", [
        {"code": code, "name": name, "kind": kind, "inn": f"{300000000 + n * 7919}"}
        for n, (code, name, kind) in enumerate(counterparties, start=1)
    ])
    emit("dim_bank", [
        {"code": f"ACC-{b.id:02d}-{k + 1}", "bank_name": bank, "currency": "UZS",
         "account_number": f"20210000{b.id:02d}{k + 1}{(b.id * 7 + k) * 13:09d}"}
        for b in BRANCHES for k, bank in enumerate(BANKS)
    ])
    emit("dim_nomenclature", [
        {"code": f"NOM-{n:03d}", "name": name, "category": STOCK_CATEGORIES[cat], "unit": unit,
         "enbt_code": f"ЕНБТ-{cat + 1:02d}-{n:03d}"}
        for n, (name, cat, unit, _price) in enumerate(NOMENCLATURE, start=1)
    ])
    emit("dim_source", [{"code": c, "name": n, "kind": k} for c, n, k in FUNDS])
    emit("dim_object", [
        {"code": f"OBJ-{n:02d}", "name": name, "program_code": f"АИП-{deadline}-{n:02d}",
         "work_type": WORK_TYPES[kind], "commissioning_year": deadline,
         "total_cost": money(total * 1000), "source_code": FUNDS[src][0]}
        for n, (name, _b, kind, src, total, _plan, deadline) in enumerate(OBJECTS, start=1)
    ])
    emit("dim_contract", [
        {"code": c.code, "number": c.number, "contract_date": c.contract_date,
         "amount": c.amount, "end_date": c.end_date, "kind": c.kind,
         "branch_code": BRANCH_BY_ID[c.branch_id].code, "cp_code": c.cp_code,
         "object_code": c.object_code}
        for c in model.contracts
    ])
    emit("dim_loan", [
        {"code": f"LOAN-{n:02d}", "creditor": creditor, "project": project, "currency": cur,
         "rate": rate, "grace_until": date(grace, 1, 1), "maturity": date(end, 12, 31),
         "amount_uzs": money(amount * 1000), "branch_code": BRANCHES[b].code}
        for n, (creditor, project, b, cur, amount, rate, grace, end) in enumerate(LOANS, start=1)
    ])
    emit("dim_position", [{"code": c, "category": cat, "department": dep}
                          for c, cat, dep in POSITIONS])
    return counts
```

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/fx.py`:
```python
"""ЦБ РУз kurslari (sintetik): USD, EUR, CNY → so'm, har kun."""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from datetime import timedelta

from app.dwh.dates import date_id
from app.etl.synthetic.model import SynthModel, money
from app.etl.synthetic.rng import rnd
from app.etl.types import StagingSink, Window


def generate(model: SynthModel, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> dict[str, int]:
    count = 0
    day = window.date_from
    while day <= window.date_to:
        t = (day - model.history_start).days
        noise = rnd(model.seed, "fx", day.isoformat()).uniform(-12, 12)
        usd = 12500 + 380 * math.sin(2 * math.pi * t / 365) + 0.25 * t + noise
        rates = {"USD": usd, "EUR": usd * (1.08 + 0.02 * math.sin(t / 90)), "CNY": usd / 7.2}
        for currency, rate in rates.items():
            sink.add("fx_rate", {"currency": currency, "date_id": date_id(day),
                                 "rate": money(rate)})
            count += 1
        day += timedelta(days=1)
    return {"fx_rate": count}
```

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/cash.py`:
```python
"""fact_cashflow (bank проводкалари) va fact_balance (hisob × sana qoldig'i)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import timedelta

from app.dwh.dates import date_id
from app.etl.synthetic.model import SynthModel
from app.etl.synthetic.reference import BRANCH_BY_ID
from app.etl.types import StagingSink, Window


def generate(model: SynthModel, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> dict[str, int]:
    cash = model.cash
    counts = {"fact_cashflow": 0, "fact_balance": 0}
    for p in cash.postings:
        if not window.contains(p.day):
            continue
        branch = BRANCH_BY_ID[p.branch_id].code
        sink.add("fact_cashflow", {
            "doc_type": "bank_entry", "src_id": p.src_id, "date_id": date_id(p.day),
            "branch_code": branch, "item_code": p.item_code, "direction": p.direction,
            "amount": p.amount, "gl_account": p.gl_account, "cp_code": p.cp_code,
            "contract_code": p.contract_code, "bank_code": f"ACC-{p.branch_id:02d}-{p.bank + 1}",
        })
        counts["fact_cashflow"] += 1
    # Oyna boshidagi ochilish qoldig'i ham kerak — bir kun oldingi qatorlar ham beriladi.
    first = window.date_from - timedelta(days=1)
    for row in cash.balances:
        if not first <= row.day <= window.date_to:
            continue
        bank = f"ACC-{row.branch_id:02d}-{row.bank + 1}"
        sink.add("fact_balance", {
            "doc_type": "statement", "src_id": f"BAL-{bank}-{date_id(row.day)}",
            "date_id": date_id(row.day), "branch_code": BRANCH_BY_ID[row.branch_id].code,
            "bank_code": bank, "balance": row.balance,
        })
        counts["fact_balance"] += 1
    return counts
```

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/payments.py`:
```python
"""fact_payment_plan (statya × oy × filial) va fact_payment_order (to'lov topshiriqlari)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict
from datetime import date, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal

from app.dwh.dates import date_id, month_id
from app.etl.synthetic.model import SynthModel, money, workdays
from app.etl.synthetic.reference import (
    BANKS,
    BRANCHES,
    EXPENSE_ITEMS,
    HQ_ID,
    PO_STATUSES,
    SUPPLIER_CODES,
    BranchRef,
    share,
)
from app.etl.synthetic.rng import rnd
from app.etl.types import StagingSink, Window

_STATUS_NAMES = [s for s, _ in PO_STATUSES]
_STATUS_WEIGHTS = [w for _, w in PO_STATUSES]


def generate(model: SynthModel, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> dict[str, int]:
    counts = {"fact_payment_plan": 0, "fact_payment_order": 0}
    current = month_id(model.as_of)
    outflow: dict[tuple[int, int], Decimal] = defaultdict(Decimal)
    for p in model.cash.postings:
        if p.direction == "out":
            outflow[(p.branch_id, month_id(p.day))] += p.amount
    for branch in BRANCHES:
        for month in model.months():
            ym = month_id(month)
            if window.contains(month):
                for item in EXPENSE_ITEMS:
                    full = model.cash.full_amounts.get((branch.id, ym, item.code))
                    if full is None:
                        continue
                    r = rnd(model.seed, "plan", branch.id, item.code, ym)
                    if ym == current:
                        plan = full * r.uniform(0.95, 1.08)
                    else:
                        plan = full / r.uniform(0.82, 1.02)
                    sink.add("fact_payment_plan", {
                        "doc_type": "payment_plan",
                        "src_id": f"PP-{branch.id:02d}-{item.code}-{ym}",
                        "date_id": date_id(month), "branch_code": branch.code,
                        "item_code": item.code, "amount": money(plan),
                    })
                    counts["fact_payment_plan"] += 1
            counts["fact_payment_order"] += _orders(model, branch, month,
                                                    outflow[(branch.id, ym)], window, sink)
    return counts


def _orders(model: SynthModel, branch: BranchRef, month: date, total: Decimal, window: Window,
            sink: StagingSink) -> int:
    ym = month_id(month)
    r = rnd(model.seed, "po", branch.id, ym)
    count = round((55 if branch.id == HQ_ID else branch.weight * 42) * r.uniform(0.85, 1.15))
    current = ym == month_id(model.as_of)
    if current:
        count = max(1, round(count * 0.55))
    reject = r.uniform(0.02, 0.06)
    days = [d for d in workdays(month) if d <= model.as_of]
    if count == 0 or total <= 0 or not days:
        return 0
    item_weights = [e.base * share(branch, e.hq_share) for e in EXPENSE_ITEMS]
    shares = model.bank_shares[branch.id]
    added = 0
    for n in range(1, count + 1):
        day = r.choice(days)
        amount = money(float(total) / count * r.uniform(0.7, 1.3))
        item = r.choices(EXPENSE_ITEMS, weights=item_weights)[0]
        cp = r.choice(SUPPLIER_CODES)
        bank = r.choices(range(len(BANKS)), weights=shares)[0]
        if current:
            status = r.choices(_STATUS_NAMES, weights=_STATUS_WEIGHTS)[0]
        else:
            status = "отклонено" if r.random() < reject else "исполнено"
        status_day = min(day + timedelta(days=r.randint(0, 3)), model.as_of)
        if not window.contains(day):
            continue
        sink.add("fact_payment_order", {
            "doc_type": "payment_order", "src_id": f"PO-{branch.id:02d}-{ym}-{n:03d}",
            "date_id": date_id(day), "branch_code": branch.code, "item_code": item.code,
            "cp_code": cp, "bank_code": f"ACC-{branch.id:02d}-{bank + 1}", "amount": amount,
            "status": status, "status_date_id": date_id(status_day),
        })
        added += 1
    return added
```

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/debt.py`:
```python
"""fact_debt: shartnoma × sana (oy oxirlari va as_of) — debitor/kreditor, muddati o'tgan."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

from app.dwh.dates import add_months, date_id, month_end, month_start
from app.etl.synthetic.model import ContractRef, SynthModel, money
from app.etl.synthetic.reference import BRANCH_BY_ID
from app.etl.types import StagingSink, Window


def _snapshot_days(model: SynthModel, contract: ContractRef) -> list[date]:
    days: list[date] = []
    cur = month_start(contract.contract_date)
    while cur <= model.as_of:
        day = model.period_end(cur)
        if day >= contract.contract_date:
            days.append(day)
        cur = add_months(cur, 1)
    last_active = month_end(contract.end_date)
    return [d for d in days if contract.late or d <= last_active]


def generate(model: SynthModel, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> dict[str, int]:
    count = 0
    for c in model.contracts:
        for day in _snapshot_days(model, c):
            if not window.contains(day):
                continue
            months_left = (model.as_of.year - day.year) * 12 + model.as_of.month - day.month
            outstanding = float(c.amount) * (1 + 0.025 * months_left)  # prototip debAt()
            overdue_days = (day - c.due_date).days if c.late and day > c.due_date else 0
            overdue = outstanding * c.overdue_ratio if overdue_days else 0.0
            sink.add("fact_debt", {
                "doc_type": "debt_snapshot", "src_id": f"DEBT-{c.code}-{date_id(day)}",
                "date_id": date_id(day), "branch_code": BRANCH_BY_ID[c.branch_id].code,
                "contract_code": c.code, "cp_code": c.cp_code,
                "receivable": money(outstanding) if c.kind == "receivable" else 0,
                "payable": money(outstanding) if c.kind == "payable" else 0,
                "overdue": money(overdue), "overdue_days": overdue_days,
            })
            count += 1
    return {"fact_debt": count}
```

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/funds.py`:
```python
"""fact_target_funds: manba × oy × filial — ajratilgan va ishlatilgan (prototip `funds`)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.dwh.dates import date_id, month_id
from app.etl.synthetic.model import SynthModel, money
from app.etl.synthetic.reference import BRANCHES, EXPENSE_ITEMS, FUNDS, HQ_ID, share
from app.etl.synthetic.rng import rnd
from app.etl.types import StagingSink, Window


def generate(model: SynthModel, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> dict[str, int]:
    count = 0
    for branch in BRANCHES:
        monthly_out = sum(e.base * share(branch, e.hq_share) for e in EXPENSE_ITEMS)
        for n, (code, _name, _kind) in enumerate(FUNDS, start=1):
            r = rnd(model.seed, "fund", branch.id, n)
            if not (n == 4 or r.random() > 0.45):
                continue
            allocated = monthly_out * r.uniform(0.8, 3) * (2.5 if branch.id == HQ_ID else 1) / 12
            for month in model.months():
                if not window.contains(month):
                    continue
                ym = month_id(month)
                used = allocated * rnd(model.seed, "fund", branch.id, n, ym).uniform(0.55, 0.97)
                sink.add("fact_target_funds", {
                    "doc_type": "target_funds", "src_id": f"TF-{branch.id:02d}-{n}-{ym}",
                    "date_id": date_id(month), "branch_code": branch.code, "source_code": code,
                    "allocated": money(allocated), "used": money(used),
                })
                count += 1
    return {"fact_target_funds": count}
```

"""Sintetik fakt oilalari reyestri. Keyingi rejalar (generation, energy_sales, procurement,
stock, stock_move, investment, loan_schedule, hr) o'z modulini yozib shu ro'yxatga qo'shadi."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable

from app.etl.synthetic.families import cash, debt, dims, funds, fx, payments
from app.etl.synthetic.model import SynthModel
from app.etl.types import StagingSink, Window

Family = Callable[[SynthModel, Window, StagingSink], dict[str, int]]

FAMILIES: list[tuple[str, Family]] = [
    ("dims", dims.generate),
    ("fx", fx.generate),
    ("cash", cash.generate),
    ("payments", payments.generate),
    ("debt", debt.generate),
    ("funds", funds.generate),
]

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/__init__.py`:
```python
"""SyntheticConnector (spec 5.2): dev/test/yuklama testi uchun deterministik manba."""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections import defaultdict
from datetime import date, datetime, time
from decimal import Decimal

from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.dwh.dates import TASHKENT, month_id, today_tashkent
from app.etl.synthetic.families import FAMILIES
from app.etl.synthetic.model import HISTORY_START, SynthModel
from app.etl.types import (
    ControlTotals,
    ExtractStats,
    HealthResult,
    StagingSink,
    Watermark,
    Window,
)


class SyntheticConnector:
    def __init__(self, *, seed: int | None = None, as_of: date | None = None,
                 history_start: date = HISTORY_START, source: str = "synthetic") -> None:
        self.source = source
        self.seed = get_settings().synthetic_seed if seed is None else seed
        self.as_of = as_of or today_tashkent()
        self.model = SynthModel(self.seed, self.as_of, history_start)

    def healthcheck(self) -> HealthResult:
        return HealthResult(True, "synthetic")

    def watermark(self) -> Watermark:
        return Watermark(datetime.combine(self.as_of, time(0), tzinfo=TASHKENT))

    def extract(self, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> ExtractStats:
        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        for _name, family in FAMILIES:
            counts.update(family(self.model, window, sink))
        return ExtractStats(sum(counts.values()), counts)

    def control_totals(self, window: Window) -> ControlTotals | None:
        totals: dict[int, Decimal] = defaultdict(Decimal)
        for posting in self.model.cash.postings:
            if window.contains(posting.day):
                totals[month_id(posting.day)] += posting.amount
        return ControlTotals(dict(totals))
```

### Task 10: `FileConnector` (CSV) va konnektorlar reyestri

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/etl/file_connector.py`, `backend/app/etl/connectors.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_file_connector.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `TARGETS`, `Window`, `ExtractStats`, `ControlTotals`, `HealthResult`, `Watermark`, `StagingSink`, `date_id`, `Settings`, `ConfigError`, `SyntheticConnector`, `Runner`, `Mode`.
- Produces:
  - `app.etl.file_connector`: `NUMERIC_COLUMNS: frozenset[str]`, `FileConnector(directory: Path, *, source="file")` — `SourceConnector`; katalogda `<maqsad>.csv` (masalan `fact_cashflow.csv`) va ixtiyoriy `control_totals.csv` (`month;amount`). Qoidalar: UTF-8 (BOM ixtiyoriy), `;`, `date`/`*_date` ustunlari ISO 8601 → `date_id`/`*_date_id`, raqamlarda razryad ajratkichi taqiqlangan (`ValueError`, qayta urinilmaydi), kasr ajratkichi `.` yoki `,`.
  - `app.etl.connectors`: `KNOWN_SOURCES = ("synthetic", "file")`, `make_connector(source, settings=None) -> SourceConnector` (prod'da `synthetic` → `ConfigError`, noma'lum → `ValueError`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Testlarni yozish**

`backend/tests/test_file_connector.py`:
```python
import os
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import UTC, datetime
from pathlib import Path

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core.config import ConfigError, get_settings
from app.etl.connectors import make_connector
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.etl.file_connector import FileConnector
from app.etl.runner import RunResult, Runner
from app.etl.types import Mode
from tests.etl_helpers import TEST_AS_OF, new_source, new_tag, purge_source

HEADER = "doc_type;src_id;date;branch_code;item_code;direction;amount"


@pytest.fixture
def source() -> Iterator[str]:
    name = new_source()
    yield name
    purge_source(name)


def _write(path: Path, lines: list[str], *, bom: bool = False) -> None:
    path.write_text(("﻿" if bom else "") + "\n".join(lines) + "\n", encoding="utf-8")


def _branch(directory: Path, tag: str) -> str:
    _write(directory / "dim_branch.csv",
           ["code;name;short_name;kind;capacity_mw", f"T-{tag};Тестовая ГЭС;Т;ges;12,5"])
    return f"T-{tag}"


def _run(directory: Path, source: str, sleeps: list[float] | None = None) -> RunResult:
    return Runner(FileConnector(directory, source=source), mode=Mode.FULL, today=TEST_AS_OF,
                  sleep=(sleeps if sleeps is not None else []).append).run()


def test_csv_load_end_to_end(tmp_path: Path, source: str) -> None:
    code = _branch(tmp_path, new_tag())
    _write(tmp_path / "fact_cashflow.csv", [
        HEADER,
        f"bank_entry;f1;2026-08-05;{code};PKM54-O09;out;1234.50",
        f"bank_entry;f2;2026-08-06;{code};PKM54-O09;out;10,25",
        f"bank_entry;old;2023-01-01;{code};PKM54-O09;out;5",
    ], bom=True)
    _write(tmp_path / "control_totals.csv", ["month;amount", "202608;1244.75"])
    result = _run(tmp_path, source)
    assert result.status == "success", result
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        rows = conn.execute(text(
            "SELECT src_id, date_id, amount FROM dwh.fact_cashflow WHERE src_system = :s "
            "ORDER BY src_id"), {"s": source}).all()
        capacity = conn.execute(text("SELECT capacity_mw FROM dwh.dim_branch WHERE code = :c"),
                                {"c": code}).scalar_one()
    assert [(r[0], r[1], float(r[2])) for r in rows] == [
        ("f1", 20260805, 1234.5), ("f2", 20260806, 10.25)]
    assert float(capacity) == 12.5


def test_thousands_separator_fails_without_retry(tmp_path: Path, source: str) -> None:
    code = _branch(tmp_path, new_tag())
    _write(tmp_path / "fact_cashflow.csv",
           [HEADER, f"bank_entry;f1;2026-08-05;{code};PKM54-O09;out;1 234,50"])
    sleeps: list[float] = []
    result = _run(tmp_path, source, sleeps)
    assert result.status == "failed" and sleeps == []
    assert "разделитель разрядов" in (result.error or "")
    assert "fact_cashflow.csv:2" in (result.error or "")


def test_inn_with_spaces_is_not_a_number_error(tmp_path: Path, source: str) -> None:
    _write(tmp_path / "dim_counterparty.csv",
           ["code;name;kind;inn", f"CP-{new_tag()};ООО «Тест»;supplier;305 123 456"])
    assert _run(tmp_path, source).status == "success"


def test_bad_date_fails(tmp_path: Path, source: str) -> None:
    code = _branch(tmp_path, new_tag())
    _write(tmp_path / "fact_cashflow.csv",
           [HEADER, f"bank_entry;f1;05.08.2026;{code};PKM54-O09;out;1"])
    result = _run(tmp_path, source)
    assert result.status == "failed" and "неверная дата" in (result.error or "")


def test_non_utf8_file_fails(tmp_path: Path, source: str) -> None:
    (tmp_path / "dim_branch.csv").write_bytes(
        "code;name;short_name;kind\nT-1;Тест;Т;ges\n".encode("cp1251"))
    result = _run(tmp_path, source)
    assert result.status == "failed" and "UTF-8" in (result.error or "")


def test_missing_directory_is_unavailable(tmp_path: Path, source: str) -> None:
    missing = tmp_path / "nope"
    assert FileConnector(missing).healthcheck().ok is False
    sleeps: list[float] = []
    result = _run(missing, source, sleeps)
    assert result.status == "failed" and len(sleeps) == 3


def test_watermark_is_newest_file_mtime(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    connector = FileConnector(tmp_path)
    assert connector.watermark().value is None
    _write(tmp_path / "dim_item.csv", ["code;name;activity;direction"])
    os.utime(tmp_path / "dim_item.csv", (1_790_000_000, 1_790_000_000))
    assert connector.watermark().value == datetime.fromtimestamp(1_790_000_000, tz=UTC)


def test_make_connector_rules(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    prod = get_settings().model_copy(update={"env": "prod"})
    with pytest.raises(ConfigError):
        make_connector("synthetic", prod)
    with pytest.raises(ValueError, match="noma'lum manba"):
        make_connector("oracle")
    dev = get_settings().model_copy(update={"env": "dev", "etl_file_dir": str(tmp_path)})
    assert isinstance(make_connector("file", dev), FileConnector)
    assert make_connector("synthetic", dev).source == "synthetic"
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implementatsiya**

`backend/app/etl/file_connector.py`:
```python
"""FileConnector (spec 5.2, TZ 8 "Общие правила обмена"): CSV, UTF-8, ';', ISO 8601 sanalar,
summalarda razryad ajratkichi yo'q.

Katalogda har staging maqsadi uchun `<maqsad>.csv` (sarlavha — payload kalitlari: `code`,
`branch_code`, `item_code`, ...). `date` → `date_id`, `status_date` → `status_date_id`.
Ixtiyoriy `control_totals.csv` (`month;amount`) — 1C oborotlari (5110/5210/5530).
Format xatosi — ValueError (qayta urinilmaydi), fayl/katalog yo'qligi — vaqtinchalik xato.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import csv
import os
import re
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import UTC, date, datetime
from decimal import Decimal, InvalidOperation
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from app.dwh.dates import date_id
from app.dwh.targets import TARGETS, Target
from app.etl.types import (
    ControlTotals,
    ExtractStats,
    HealthResult,
    StagingSink,
    Watermark,
    Window,
)

NUMERIC_COLUMNS = frozenset({
    "amount", "balance", "capacity_mw", "lat", "lon", "rate", "amount_uzs", "total_cost",
    "receivable", "payable", "overdue", "generation", "own_needs", "supply", "plan",
    "work_hours", "accrued", "paid", "volume", "start_price", "contract_price", "qty", "value",
    "min_qty", "fact", "physical_pct", "advance", "principal", "interest", "headcount_plan",
    "headcount_fact", "payroll", "overtime_hours", "allocated", "used",
})
_THOUSANDS = re.compile(r"^-?\d{1,3}([   ']\d{3})+([.,]\d+)?$")
_DECIMAL_COMMA = re.compile(r"^-?\d+,\d+$")


def _number(value: str, where: str) -> str:
    if _THOUSANDS.match(value):
        raise ValueError(f"{where}: разделитель разрядов в числе «{value}» не допускается")
    if _DECIMAL_COMMA.match(value):
        value = value.replace(",", ".")
    try:
        Decimal(value)
    except InvalidOperation as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{where}: неверное число «{value}»") from exc
    return value


def _iso_date(value: str, where: str) -> int:
    try:
        return date_id(date.fromisoformat(value))
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ValueError(f"{where}: неверная дата «{value}» (ожидается ГГГГ-ММ-ДД)") from exc


class FileConnector:
    def __init__(self, directory: Path, *, source: str = "file") -> None:
        self.directory = directory
        self.source = source

    def healthcheck(self) -> HealthResult:
        if not self.directory.is_dir():
            return HealthResult(False, f"каталог не найден: {self.directory}")
        if not os.access(self.directory, os.R_OK):
            return HealthResult(False, f"нет доступа к каталогу: {self.directory}")
        return HealthResult(True, str(self.directory))

    def watermark(self) -> Watermark:
        files = list(self.directory.glob("*.csv")) if self.directory.is_dir() else []
        if not files:
            return Watermark(None)
        return Watermark(datetime.fromtimestamp(max(f.stat().st_mtime for f in files), tz=UTC))

    def _rows(self, path: Path) -> Iterator[tuple[int, dict[str, str]]]:
        try:
            with path.open(encoding="utf-8-sig", newline="") as handle:
                reader = csv.DictReader(handle, delimiter=";")
                for line, raw in enumerate(reader, start=2):
                    if None in raw:
                        raise ValueError(f"{path.name}:{line}: лишние поля в строке")
                    yield line, {k.strip(): (v or "").strip() for k, v in raw.items()}
        except UnicodeDecodeError as exc:
            raise ValueError(f"{path.name}: файл не в кодировке UTF-8") from exc

    def _convert(self, target: Target, raw: dict[str, str], where: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        row: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for key, value in raw.items():
            is_date = key == "date" or key.endswith("_date")
            out_key = f"{key}_id" if is_date and f"{key}_id" in target.columns else key
            if value == "":
                row[out_key] = None
            elif out_key != key:
                row[out_key] = _iso_date(value, where)
            elif key in NUMERIC_COLUMNS:
                row[key] = _number(value, where)
            else:
                row[key] = value
        return row

    def extract(self, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> ExtractStats:
        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        for name, target in TARGETS.items():
            path = self.directory / f"{name}.csv"
            if not path.is_file():
                continue
            counts[name] = 0
            for line, raw in self._rows(path):
                row = self._convert(target, raw, f"{path.name}:{line}")
                if target.kind == "fact" and row.get("date_id") is not None \
                        and not window.contains_id(int(row["date_id"])):
                    continue
                sink.add(name, row)
                counts[name] += 1
        return ExtractStats(sum(counts.values()), counts)

    def control_totals(self, window: Window) -> ControlTotals | None:
        path = self.directory / "control_totals.csv"
        if not path.is_file():
            return None
        months = set(window.month_ids())
        totals: dict[int, Decimal] = {}
        for line, raw in self._rows(path):
            month = int(raw["month"])
            if month in months:
                totals[month] = Decimal(_number(raw["amount"], f"{path.name}:{line}"))
        return ControlTotals(totals)
```

"""Manba nomi → konnektor. 1C, DirectBank va boshqalar keyingi rejalarda qo'shiladi."""

from __future__ import annotations

from pathlib import Path

from app.core.config import ConfigError, Settings, get_settings
from app.etl.file_connector import FileConnector
from app.etl.synthetic import SyntheticConnector
from app.etl.types import SourceConnector

KNOWN_SOURCES = ("synthetic", "file")


def make_connector(source: str, settings: Settings | None = None) -> SourceConnector:
    settings = settings or get_settings()
    if source == "synthetic":
        if settings.env == "prod":
            raise ConfigError("SyntheticConnector prod muhitida taqiqlangan")
        return SyntheticConnector(seed=settings.synthetic_seed)
    if source == "file":
        return FileConnector(Path(settings.etl_file_dir))
    raise ValueError(f"noma'lum manba: {source}")

### Task 11: Kesh — Redis (versiya × scope), single-flight, ETag/304, `Cache` dependency

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/core/cache.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_cache.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `get_settings().redis_url`, `get_logger`, `Ctx`/`AuthContext`, `get_db`, `builds` (testda), `tests.helpers.login`.
- Produces: `app.core.cache`: `CACHE_TTL = 3600`, `LOCK_TTL_MS = 5000`, `WAIT_STEP = 0.05`, `current_version(db) -> int`, `scope_hash(branch_ids) -> str`, `filters_hash(filters) -> str`, `cache_key(version, endpoint, scope, filters) -> str` (`"ugk:v{version}:{endpoint}:{scope}:{filters}"`), `etag_for(version, endpoint, scope, filters) -> str`, `render(payload) -> bytes`, `cached_body(db, *, endpoint, scope, filters, loader, sleep=time.sleep) -> tuple[bytes, int]`, `ResponseCache(request, ctx, db)` + `.respond(endpoint, filters, loader) -> Response`, `get_cache(...)`, `Cache = Annotated[ResponseCache, Depends(get_cache)]`, `_client_override` (testlar uchun). Bo'lim endpointi bitta qatorda: `return cache.respond("kazna.svod", filters, lambda: service.svod(db, filters))`.
