```python
import threading
import time
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator
from typing import Any

import pytest
import redis
from fastapi import FastAPI, Response
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.core import cache
from app.core.cache import (
    Cache,
    cache_key,
    cached_body,
    current_version,
    filters_hash,
    scope_hash,
)
from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.db import new_session
from app.dwh import builds
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.main import create_app
from tests.helpers import SeedUser, login


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _clean_cache() -> Iterator[None]:
    client = redis.Redis.from_url(get_settings().redis_url)
    for key in client.scan_iter("ugk:*"):
        client.delete(key)
    yield


def _counting(result: Any = None, delay: float = 0.0) -> tuple[Callable[[], Any], list[int]]:
    calls = [0]
    guard = threading.Lock()

    def loader() -> Any:
        with guard:
            calls[0] += 1
        if delay:
            time.sleep(delay)
        return result if result is not None else {"value": 42}

    return loader, calls


def _publish_new_build() -> int:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        build_id = builds.create_build(conn, None)
        builds.mark_ready(conn, build_id)
        builds.publish(conn, build_id, keep=3)
    return build_id


def test_key_and_hash_formats() -> None:
    assert cache_key(5, "meta.branches", "abc", "def") == "ugk:v5:meta.branches:abc:def"
    assert scope_hash(None) != scope_hash(frozenset({3}))
    assert scope_hash(frozenset({3, 7})) == scope_hash(frozenset({7, 3}))
    assert filters_hash({"a": 1, "b": 2}) == filters_hash({"b": 2, "a": 1})
    assert filters_hash({"a": 1}) != filters_hash({"a": 2})


def test_second_call_hits_cache() -> None:
    loader, calls = _counting()
    with new_session() as db:
        first, _ = cached_body(db, endpoint="t.hit", scope="s", filters={}, loader=loader)
        second, _ = cached_body(db, endpoint="t.hit", scope="s", filters={}, loader=loader)
    assert first == second == b'{"value":42}'
    assert calls[0] == 1


def test_scope_and_filters_are_isolated() -> None:
    loader, calls = _counting()
    with new_session() as db:
        cached_body(db, endpoint="t.iso", scope="s1", filters={"a": 1}, loader=loader)
        cached_body(db, endpoint="t.iso", scope="s2", filters={"a": 1}, loader=loader)
        cached_body(db, endpoint="t.iso", scope="s1", filters={"a": 2}, loader=loader)
    assert calls[0] == 3


def test_new_data_version_uses_new_key() -> None:
    loader, calls = _counting()
    with new_session() as db:
        _, v1 = cached_body(db, endpoint="t.ver", scope="s", filters={}, loader=loader)
    new_build = _publish_new_build()
    with new_session() as db:
        _, v2 = cached_body(db, endpoint="t.ver", scope="s", filters={}, loader=loader)
        assert current_version(db) == new_build
    assert v2 == new_build != v1
    assert calls[0] == 2


def test_single_flight_computes_once() -> None:
    loader, calls = _counting(delay=0.3)
    bodies: list[bytes] = []
    errors: list[BaseException] = []

    def worker() -> None:
        try:
            with new_session() as db:
                body, _ = cached_body(db, endpoint="t.sf", scope="s", filters={}, loader=loader)
                bodies.append(body)
        except BaseException as exc:
            errors.append(exc)

    threads = [threading.Thread(target=worker) for _ in range(5)]
    for t in threads:
        t.start()
    for t in threads:
        t.join()
    assert errors == []
    assert calls[0] == 1
    assert len(set(bodies)) == 1 and len(bodies) == 5


def test_redis_down_fails_open(monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(cache, "_last_failure", 0.0)
    dead = redis.Redis(host="127.0.0.1", port=1, socket_timeout=0.2, socket_connect_timeout=0.2)
    monkeypatch.setattr(cache, "_client_override", dead)
    loader, calls = _counting()
    with new_session() as db:
        for _ in range(2):
            body, _ = cached_body(db, endpoint="t.down", scope="s", filters={}, loader=loader)
            assert body == b'{"value":42}'
    assert calls[0] == 2


def test_version_change_during_compute_is_not_cached() -> None:
    client = redis.Redis.from_url(get_settings().redis_url)

    def publishing_loader() -> dict[str, int]:
        _publish_new_build()  # publish aynan hisoblash paytida
        return {"stale": 1}

    with new_session() as db:
        _, version = cached_body(db, endpoint="t.race", scope="s", filters={},
                                 loader=publishing_loader)
    assert client.get(cache_key(version, "t.race", "s", filters_hash({}))) is None


def _app() -> FastAPI:
    app = create_app()

    @app.get("/api/v1/_t/cached")
    def cached(cache_dep: Cache) -> Response:
        return cache_dep.respond("t.cached", {"x": 1}, lambda: {"ok": True})

    return app


def test_etag_304_and_headers(make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser]) -> None:
    with TestClient(_app(), base_url="https://testserver") as client:
        login(client, make_user("admin"))
        r = client.get("/api/v1/_t/cached")
        assert r.status_code == 200 and r.json() == {"ok": True}
        assert r.headers["Cache-Control"] == "private, no-cache"
        assert r.headers["X-Content-Type-Options"] == "nosniff"
        etag = r.headers["ETag"]
        again = client.get("/api/v1/_t/cached", headers={"If-None-Match": etag})
        assert again.status_code == 304 and again.content == b""
        assert again.headers["ETag"] == etag
    with TestClient(_app(), base_url="https://testserver") as other:
        login(other, make_user("economist_filiala", branch_ids=(3,)))
        assert other.get("/api/v1/_t/cached").headers["ETag"] != etag

- [ ] **Step 3: Implementatsiya**

`backend/app/core/cache.py`:
```python
"""Redis kesh va HTTP ETag (spec 6.2).

Kalit: ugk:v{data_version}:{endpoint}:{scope_hash}:{filters_hash}. data_version — aktiv build;
publish/rollback'da o'zgaradi, eski kalitlar ishlatilmay qoladi (TTL 1 soat). scope_hash
foydalanuvchi filial doirasidan — boshqa doira keshi hech qachon qaytmaydi. Single-flight:
`SET NX PX 5000` lock. Redis ishlamasa — fail-open (to'g'ridan-to'g'ri hisoblanadi).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import hashlib
import json
import threading
import time
import uuid
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from typing import Annotated, Any

import redis
from fastapi import Depends, Request, Response
from fastapi.encoders import jsonable_encoder
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.auth.context import AuthContext, Ctx
from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.core.logging import get_logger

CACHE_TTL = 3600
LOCK_TTL_MS = 5000
WAIT_STEP = 0.05
RETRY_CLIENT_AFTER = 30.0
_RELEASE = "if redis.call('get', KEYS[1]) == ARGV[1] then return redis.call('del', KEYS[1]) end"

log = get_logger("ugekazna.cache")
_client_override: redis.Redis | None = None
_client: redis.Redis | None = None
_lock = threading.Lock()
_last_failure = 0.0


def _get_client() -> redis.Redis | None:
    global _client
    if _client_override is not None:
        return _client_override
    if time.monotonic() - _last_failure < RETRY_CLIENT_AFTER:
        return None
    with _lock:
        if _client is None:
            _client = redis.Redis.from_url(
                get_settings().redis_url, socket_timeout=0.5, socket_connect_timeout=0.5
            )
        return _client


def _failed(exc: Exception) -> None:
    global _last_failure
    _last_failure = time.monotonic()
    log.warning("cache_unavailable", error=type(exc).__name__)


def current_version(db: Session) -> int:
    value = db.execute(
        text("SELECT coalesce(active_build_id, 0) FROM core.data_version WHERE id = 1")
    ).scalar_one_or_none()
    return int(value or 0)


def _digest(value: str) -> str:
    return hashlib.sha256(value.encode()).hexdigest()[:16]


def scope_hash(branch_ids: frozenset[int] | None) -> str:
    return _digest("all" if branch_ids is None else ",".join(str(b) for b in sorted(branch_ids)))


def filters_hash(filters: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    return _digest(json.dumps(filters, sort_keys=True, separators=(",", ":"), default=str,
                              ensure_ascii=False))


def cache_key(version: int, endpoint: str, scope: str, filters: str) -> str:
    return f"ugk:v{version}:{endpoint}:{scope}:{filters}"


def etag_for(version: int, endpoint: str, scope: str, filters: str) -> str:
    digest = hashlib.sha256(f"{version}|{endpoint}|{scope}|{filters}".encode()).hexdigest()
    return f'"{digest[:32]}"'


def render(payload: Any) -> bytes:
    return json.dumps(jsonable_encoder(payload), ensure_ascii=False,
                      separators=(",", ":")).encode()


def _release(client: redis.Redis, lock_key: str, token: str) -> None:
    try:
        client.eval(_RELEASE, 1, lock_key, token)
    except redis.RedisError as exc:
        _failed(exc)


def cached_body(
    db: Session,
    *,
    endpoint: str,
    scope: str,
    filters: Mapping[str, Any],
    loader: Callable[[], Any],
    sleep: Callable[[float], None] = time.sleep,
) -> tuple[bytes, int]:
    """(JSON baytlar, data_version). Loader faqat kesh bo'sh bo'lsa chaqiriladi."""
    version = current_version(db)
    key = cache_key(version, endpoint, scope, filters_hash(filters))
    client = _get_client()
    if client is None:
        return render(loader()), version
    lock_key = f"{key}:lock"
    token = uuid.uuid4().hex
    try:
        hit = client.get(key)
        if hit is not None:
            return bytes(hit), version
        owner = bool(client.set(lock_key, token, nx=True, px=LOCK_TTL_MS))
    except redis.RedisError as exc:
        _failed(exc)
        return render(loader()), version
    if not owner:
        deadline = time.monotonic() + LOCK_TTL_MS / 1000
        while time.monotonic() < deadline:
            sleep(WAIT_STEP)
            try:
                hit = client.get(key)
            except redis.RedisError as exc:
                _failed(exc)
                break
            if hit is not None:
                return bytes(hit), version
        return render(loader()), version
    try:
        body = render(loader())
        # Hisoblash paytida publish bo'lgan bo'lsa, yangi ma'lumot eski versiya kaliti
        # ostida saqlanib qolmasin (rollback'dan keyin noto'g'ri javob bo'lardi).
        if current_version(db) == version:
            try:
                client.set(key, body, ex=CACHE_TTL)
            except redis.RedisError as exc:
                _failed(exc)
        return body, version
    finally:
        _release(client, lock_key, token)


def _etag_matches(header: str | None, etag: str) -> bool:
    if not header:
        return False
    candidates = {part.strip().removeprefix("W/") for part in header.split(",")}
    return "*" in candidates or etag in candidates


class ResponseCache:
    def __init__(self, request: Request, ctx: AuthContext, db: Session) -> None:
        self.request = request
        self.ctx = ctx
        self.db = db

    def respond(self, endpoint: str, filters: Mapping[str, Any],
                loader: Callable[[], Any]) -> Response:
        scope = scope_hash(self.ctx.branch_ids)
        fhash = filters_hash(filters)
        etag = etag_for(current_version(self.db), endpoint, scope, fhash)
        # SecurityHeadersMiddleware setdefault ishlatadi — bu yerdagi qiymat ustun.
        headers = {"ETag": etag, "Cache-Control": "private, no-cache", "Vary": "Cookie"}
        if _etag_matches(self.request.headers.get("if-none-match"), etag):
            return Response(status_code=304, headers=headers)
        body, version = cached_body(self.db, endpoint=endpoint, scope=scope, filters=filters,
                                    loader=loader)
        headers["ETag"] = etag_for(version, endpoint, scope, fhash)
        return Response(content=body, media_type="application/json", headers=headers)


def get_cache(
    request: Request, ctx: Ctx, db: Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)]
) -> ResponseCache:
    return ResponseCache(request, ctx, db)


Cache = Annotated[ResponseCache, Depends(get_cache)]
```

    assert len(admin.get("/api/v1/meta/branches").json()) >= 16  # kesh "hammasi" bilan to'ldi
    login(client, make_user("economist_filiala", branch_ids=(3,)))
    assert [b["id"] for b in client.get("/api/v1/meta/branches").json()] == [3]


def test_pub_views_and_mart_rls_direct_sql(published_synthetic: int) -> None:
    queries = {
        "pub.branches": "SELECT DISTINCT branch_id FROM pub.branches",
        "pub.agg_cash_month": "SELECT DISTINCT branch_id FROM pub.agg_cash_month",
        "pub.agg_payments_month": "SELECT DISTINCT branch_id FROM pub.agg_payments_month",
        "mart.agg_cash_month": "SELECT DISTINCT branch_id FROM mart.agg_cash_month",
    }
    with new_session() as s:
        apply_rls(s, frozenset({3}))
        for name, sql in queries.items():
            assert set(s.execute(text(sql)).scalars()) == {3}, name
    with new_session() as s:
        apply_rls(s, None)
        assert 0 in set(s.execute(text(queries["pub.agg_cash_month"])).scalars())
    with new_session() as s:  # doira o'rnatilmagan — hech narsa
        for sql in queries.values():
            assert s.execute(text(sql)).all() == []


def test_periods_builder() -> None:
    periods = build_periods(date(2026, 9, 24))
    assert [p.code for p in periods] == [
        "ytd", "q1", "q2", "q3", "m01", "m02", "m03", "m04", "m05", "m06", "m07", "m08", "m09"]
    labels = {p.code: p.label for p in periods}
    assert labels["ytd"] == "Январь – сентябрь 2026"
    assert labels["q3"] == "III квартал 2026"
    assert labels["m09"] == "Сентябрь 2026"
    q3 = next(p for p in periods if p.code == "q3")
    assert (q3.date_from, q3.date_to) == (date(2026, 7, 1), date(2026, 9, 24))


def test_periods_on_first_of_january() -> None:
    periods = build_periods(date(2027, 1, 1))
    assert [(p.code, p.label) for p in periods] == [
        ("ytd", "Январь 2027"), ("q1", "I квартал 2027"), ("m01", "Январь 2027")]
    assert all(p.date_to == date(2027, 1, 1) for p in periods)

    body = client.get("/api/v1/meta/periods").json()
    assert body["default"] == "ytd"
    assert body["items"][0]["code"] == "ytd"


def test_menu_filtered_by_role() -> None:
    econ = {"section:obzor:view", "section:kazna:view", "section:proizvodstvo:view",
            "section:sklad:view", "section:kadry:view"}
    menu = build_menu(frozenset(econ))
    assert [g.title for g in menu.groups] == [None, "Финансы", "Обеспечение", "Производство",
                                              "Персонал"]

def test_freshness(client: TestClient, make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser],
                   published_synthetic: int) -> None:
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        current = freshness(conn, datetime.now(UTC))
        assert current.last_success_at is not None
        later = freshness(conn, current.last_success_at + timedelta(hours=7))
        fresh = freshness(conn, current.last_success_at + timedelta(hours=1))
    assert later.stale is True and fresh.stale is False and later.max_age_hours == 6
    login(client, make_user("economist_filiala", branch_ids=(3,)))
    body = client.get("/api/v1/meta/freshness").json()
    assert body["last_success_at"] is not None and body["max_age_hours"] == 6
    assert body["active_build_id"] == published_synthetic

`backend/app/meta/periods.py`:
```python
"""Davr filtri variantlari (TZ 6 «Фильтры»): yil boshidan, choraklar, oylar — joriy yil."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date

from app.dwh.dates import month_end

MONTHS = ("Январь", "Февраль", "Март", "Апрель", "Май", "Июнь", "Июль", "Август",
          "Сентябрь", "Октябрь", "Ноябрь", "Декабрь")
QUARTERS = ("I", "II", "III", "IV")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Period:
    code: str
    kind: str  # ytd | quarter | month
    label: str
    date_from: date
    date_to: date


def build_periods(today: date) -> list[Period]:
    year, month = today.year, today.month
    if month == 1:
        ytd_label = f"{MONTHS[0]} {year}"
    else:
        ytd_label = f"{MONTHS[0]} – {MONTHS[month - 1].lower()} {year}"
    out = [Period("ytd", "ytd", ytd_label, date(year, 1, 1), today)]
    for q in range(1, (month - 1) // 3 + 2):
        start = date(year, 3 * q - 2, 1)
        end = min(month_end(date(year, 3 * q, 1)), today)
        out.append(Period(f"q{q}", "quarter", f"{QUARTERS[q - 1]} квартал {year}", start, end))
    for m in range(1, month + 1):
        start = date(year, m, 1)
        out.append(Period(f"m{m:02d}", "month", f"{MONTHS[m - 1]} {year}", start,
                          min(month_end(start), today)))
    return out
```

`backend/app/meta/menu.py`:
```python
"""9 bo'lim va ularning chap menyusi (TZ 5-bo'lim jadvali), guruhlar (TZ 6 «Навигация»).

Sahifa id'lari tasdiqlangan prototip bilan bir xil (URL: #kazna/svod?br=3&per=m05).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from app.auth.permissions import SECTION_TITLES, Section, section_perm
from app.meta.schemas import MenuGroupOut, MenuOut, MenuPageOut, MenuSectionOut


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class MenuPage:
    id: str
    title: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class MenuSection:
    section: Section
    group: str | None
    pages: tuple[MenuPage, ...]


P = MenuPage
SVOD = P("svod", "Сводка")
GROUP_ORDER: tuple[str | None, ...] = (None, "Финансы", "Обеспечение", "Производство", "Персонал")

MENU: tuple[MenuSection, ...] = (
    MenuSection(Section.OBZOR, None, (
        SVOD, P("vnimanie", "Требует внимания"), P("postup", "Поступления"),
        P("rasxod", "Расходование"), P("excel", "Таблица Excel"))),
    MenuSection(Section.KAZNA, "Финансы", (
        SVOD, P("platezhi", "Исполнение платежей"), P("calendar", "Платёжный календарь"),
        P("dogovory", "Договоры и задолженность"), P("celevye", "Целевые средства"))),
    MenuSection(Section.KREDITY, "Финансы", (
        SVOD, P("reestr", "Реестр кредитов"), P("grafik", "График платежей"))),
    MenuSection(Section.FINMODEL, "Финансы", (
        SVOD, P("dohody", "Доходы и расходы"), P("dolg", "Инвестиции и долг"),
        P("scenarii", "Сценарии"))),
    MenuSection(Section.ZAKUPKI, "Обеспечение", (
        SVOD, P("loty", "Лоты"), P("postavshiki", "Договоры и поставщики"),
        P("ekonomiya", "Экономия"))),
    MenuSection(Section.SKLAD, "Обеспечение", (
        SVOD, P("ostatki", "Остатки по складам"), P("minimum", "Минимальный запас"),
        P("nelikvid", "Неликвиды"))),
    MenuSection(Section.PROIZVODSTVO, "Производство", (
        SVOD, P("stancii", "Выработка по станциям"), P("realiz", "Реализация"))),
    MenuSection(Section.INVESTICII, "Производство", (
        SVOD, P("obekty", "Объекты"), P("istochniki", "Источники и освоение"),
        P("riski", "Сроки и риски"))),
    MenuSection(Section.KADRY, "Персонал", (
        SVOD, P("chislennost", "Численность"), P("fot", "Оплата труда"),
        P("dvizhenie", "Движение кадров"))),
)


def build_menu(permissions: frozenset[str]) -> MenuOut:
    groups: list[MenuGroupOut] = []
    for group in GROUP_ORDER:
        sections = [
            MenuSectionOut(
                id=item.section.value,
                title=SECTION_TITLES[item.section],
                pages=[MenuPageOut(id=p.id, title=p.title) for p in item.pages],
            )
            for item in MENU
            if item.group == group and section_perm(item.section) in permissions
        ]
        if sections:
            groups.append(MenuGroupOut(title=group, sections=sections))
    return MenuOut(groups=groups)
```

`backend/app/dwh/freshness.py`:
```python
"""Ma'lumot yangiligi (TZ 6 «Состояния», 9 «Мониторинг»): oxirgi muvaffaqiyatli yuklash."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import datetime, timedelta

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.engine import Connection

from app.dwh.reference import current_setting


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Freshness:
    last_success_at: datetime | None
    stale: bool
    max_age_hours: int
    active_build_id: int | None


def freshness(conn: Connection, now: datetime) -> Freshness:
    last = conn.execute(
        text("SELECT max(finished_at) FROM core.etl_run WHERE status = 'success'")
    ).scalar_one_or_none()
    active = conn.execute(
        text("SELECT active_build_id FROM core.data_version WHERE id = 1")
    ).scalar_one_or_none()
    max_age = int(current_setting(conn, "freshness.max_age_hours"))
    stale = last is None or now - last > timedelta(hours=max_age)
    return Freshness(last, stale, max_age, None if active is None else int(active))
```

"""Meta ma'lumotlari faqat pub view'lardan (RLS ostida) o'qiladi."""

from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.meta.schemas import BranchOut


def list_branches(db: Session) -> list[BranchOut]:
    rows = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT branch_id, code, name, short_name, kind, capacity_mw, lat, lon "
        "FROM pub.branches ORDER BY sort_order, branch_id")).all()
    return [
        BranchOut(
            id=r.branch_id, code=r.code, name=r.name, short_name=r.short_name, kind=r.kind,
            is_hq=r.kind == "head",
            capacity_mw=None if r.capacity_mw is None else float(r.capacity_mw),
            lat=None if r.lat is None else float(r.lat),
            lon=None if r.lon is None else float(r.lon),
        )
        for r in rows
    ]
```

`backend/app/meta/router.py`:
```python
"""Meta endpointlari: filiallar (RLS + kesh), davrlar, menyu (rolga qarab), freshness."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime
from typing import Annotated

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Response
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.auth.context import Ctx
from app.core.cache import Cache
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.dwh.dates import today_tashkent
from app.dwh.freshness import freshness
from app.meta import service
from app.meta.menu import build_menu
from app.meta.periods import build_periods
from app.meta.schemas import BranchOut, FreshnessOut, MenuOut, PeriodOut, PeriodsOut

router = APIRouter(prefix="/meta", tags=["meta"])
DB = Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)]


@router.get("/branches", response_model=list[BranchOut])
def branches(cache: Cache, db: DB) -> Response:
    # Bo'lim endpointlari pattern'i: router → service → pub view, kesh bitta qatorda.
    return cache.respond("meta.branches", {}, lambda: service.list_branches(db))


@router.get("/periods")
def periods(ctx: Ctx) -> PeriodsOut:
    today = today_tashkent()
    items = [PeriodOut(code=p.code, kind=p.kind, label=p.label, date_from=p.date_from,
                       date_to=p.date_to) for p in build_periods(today)]
    return PeriodsOut(default="ytd", today=today, items=items)


@router.get("/menu")
def menu(ctx: Ctx) -> MenuOut:
    return build_menu(ctx.permissions)


@router.get("/freshness")
def data_freshness(ctx: Ctx, db: DB) -> FreshnessOut:
    state = freshness(db.connection(), datetime.now(UTC))
    return FreshnessOut(last_success_at=state.last_success_at, stale=state.stale,
                        max_age_hours=state.max_age_hours, active_build_id=state.active_build_id)
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Pattern hujjati**

`docs/dev/section-endpoint-pattern.md`:
````markdown
# Bo'lim endpointi pattern'i

Har bo'lim endpointi (`/api/v1/<bo'lim>/...`) bir xil zanjir bo'yicha yoziladi:
`router → service → pub view`, javob `Cache` orqali. Namuna — `app/meta/router.py` (`/meta/branches`).

```python
@router.get("/svod", response_model=SvodOut)
def svod(
    cache: Cache,
    db: DB,
    ctx: Ctx,
    br: Annotated[int | None, Query(ge=1)] = None,
    per: Annotated[str, Query(pattern=r"^(ytd|q[1-4]|m(0[1-9]|1[0-2]))$")] = "ytd",
) -> Response:
    ctx.assert_branch(br)                      # 1-qavat RLS: doiradan tashqari → 403 + audit
    filters = {"br": br, "per": per}           # faqat validatsiyadan o'tgan qiymatlar
    return cache.respond("kazna.svod", filters, lambda: service.svod(db, br, per))
```

(`SvodOut` va `service.svod` — shu bo'lim rejasida yoziladigan sxema va servis funksiyasi.)

Qoidalar:
1. **Faqat `pub.*` view'lar.** Servis `dwh`/`mart` jadvallarini to'g'ridan-to'g'ri o'qimaydi; view'lar
   faqat aktiv build'ni ko'rsatadi va `security_invoker = true` — DB RLS (2-qavat) ishlaydi.
2. **Faktlarga so'rov yo'q** (TZ 9): yangi ko'rsatkich kerak bo'lsa — `app/dwh/aggregates.py` ga
   `register(Aggregate(...))` + migratsiyada `mart.agg_*` jadvali va `pub.*` view.
3. **Kesh kaliti** `ugk:v{data_version}:{endpoint}:{scope}:{filters}` — `endpoint` nomi
   `"<bo'lim>.<sahifa>"`; `filters` ga faqat javobga ta'sir qiluvchi, validatsiyalangan parametrlar.
   Publish/rollback'da kesh o'zi eskiradi; qo'lda tozalash kerak emas.
4. **ETag/304** avtomatik; `Cache-Control: private, no-cache` (umumiy `no-store` ustidan).
5. **Filial filtri**: `br` bo'lsa avval `ctx.assert_branch(br)`; `branch_id = 0` (jami) faqat
   `scope_all` foydalanuvchiga ko'rinadi — filial foydalanuvchisi uchun jami o'z filiali qatorlaridan.
6. **Testlar**: har endpoint uchun (a) filial foydalanuvchisi boshqa filialni ko'rmaydi (API),
   (b) 304, (c) `published_synthetic` fixture bilan haqiqiy agregatlar.
````

`backend/tests/test_admin_dwh_api.py`:
```python
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator

import pytest
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core.db import get_engine
from app.dwh import builds
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.etl.runner import RunResult
from app.main import create_app
from tests.etl_helpers import STEP_NAMES
from tests.helpers import SeedUser, login


@pytest.fixture
def client() -> Iterator[TestClient]:
    with TestClient(create_app(), base_url="https://testserver") as c:
        yield c


@pytest.fixture
def admin(client: TestClient, make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser]) -> SeedUser:
    user = make_user("admin")
    login(client, user)
    return user


def _audit(action: str, object_id: str) -> int:
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        return int(conn.execute(
            text("SELECT count(*) FROM core.audit_log WHERE action = :a AND object_id = :o"),
            {"a": action, "o": object_id}).scalar_one())


def test_permissions_are_enforced(client: TestClient, make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser]) -> None:
    login(client, make_user("rukovodstvo"))
    assert client.get("/api/v1/admin/etl/runs").status_code == 403
    assert client.get("/api/v1/admin/builds").status_code == 403
    assert client.get("/api/v1/admin/alert-rules").status_code == 403
    assert client.get("/api/v1/admin/settings").status_code == 403


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("admin")
def test_trigger_run_enqueues_request(client: TestClient) -> None:
    r = client.post("/api/v1/admin/etl/run", json={"source": "synthetic", "mode": "incremental"})
    assert r.status_code == 202, r.text
    request_id = r.json()["request_id"]
    try:
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            status = conn.execute(text("SELECT status FROM core.etl_requests WHERE id = :i"),
                                  {"i": request_id}).scalar_one()
        assert status == "pending" and r.json()["status"] == "pending"
        assert _audit("admin.etl.run_requested", str(request_id)) == 1
    finally:
        with etl_transaction() as conn:  # worker testlari bu so'rovni bajarmasin
            conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.etl_requests SET status = 'failed' WHERE id = :i"),
                         {"i": request_id})


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("admin")
def test_trigger_unknown_source_or_mode_is_422(client: TestClient) -> None:
    r = client.post("/api/v1/admin/etl/run", json={"source": "oracle", "mode": "full"})
    assert r.status_code == 422 and r.json()["error"]["code"] == "unknown_source"
    r = client.post("/api/v1/admin/etl/run", json={"source": "file", "mode": "hourly"})
    assert r.status_code == 422


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("admin")
def test_runs_list_and_detail(client: TestClient, synthetic_run: RunResult) -> None:
    page = client.get("/api/v1/admin/etl/runs", params={"page_size": 500}).json()
    assert synthetic_run.run_id in [r["id"] for r in page["items"]]
    detail = client.get(f"/api/v1/admin/etl/runs/{synthetic_run.run_id}").json()
    assert detail["run"]["status"] == "success"
    assert [s["name"] for s in detail["steps"]] == list(STEP_NAMES)
    assert client.get("/api/v1/admin/etl/runs/999999999").status_code == 404


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("admin")
def test_builds_list_and_rollback(client: TestClient, published_synthetic: int) -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        newer = builds.create_build(conn, None)
        builds.mark_ready(conn, newer)
        builds.publish(conn, newer, keep=3)
    listed = client.get("/api/v1/admin/builds").json()
    assert next(b for b in listed if b["active"])["build_id"] == newer
    r = client.post("/api/v1/admin/builds/rollback", json={})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    assert r.json()["build_id"] == published_synthetic and r.json()["active"] is True
    assert _audit("admin.build.rolled_back", str(published_synthetic)) >= 1
    missing = client.post("/api/v1/admin/builds/rollback", json={"build_id": 999999999})
    assert missing.status_code == 409 and missing.json()["error"]["code"] == "build_not_available"


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("admin")
def test_alert_rule_update_creates_version_and_audit(client: TestClient) -> None:
    before = next(r for r in client.get("/api/v1/admin/alert-rules").json()
                  if r["code"] == "turnover")
    r = client.patch("/api/v1/admin/alert-rules/turnover", json={"value": 0.1, "level": "critical"})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    after = r.json()
    try:
        assert after["version"] == before["version"] + 1
        assert after["threshold"]["value"] == 0.1 and after["threshold"]["op"] == ">"
        assert after["level"] == "critical"
        history = client.get("/api/v1/admin/alert-rules/turnover/history").json()
        assert [h["version"] for h in history][:2] == [after["version"], before["version"]]
        assert _audit("admin.alert_rule.updated", "turnover") >= 1
        assert client.patch("/api/v1/admin/alert-rules/turnover",
                            json={"op": "!="}).status_code == 422
        assert client.patch("/api/v1/admin/alert-rules/nope", json={"value": 1}).status_code == 404
    finally:
        client.patch("/api/v1/admin/alert-rules/turnover",
                     json={"value": before["threshold"]["value"], "level": before["level"]})


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("admin")
def test_fin_assumptions_update(client: TestClient) -> None:
    item = {"scenario": "tariff", "year": 2027, "key": "tariff_growth"}
    r = client.put("/api/v1/admin/fin-assumptions", json={"items": [{**item, "value": 0.05}]})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    try:
        current = client.get("/api/v1/admin/fin-assumptions", params={"scenario": "tariff"}).json()
        row = next(x for x in current if x["year"] == 2027 and x["key"] == "tariff_growth")
        assert row["value"] == 0.05 and row["version"] >= 2
        bad_key = client.put("/api/v1/admin/fin-assumptions",
                             json={"items": [{**item, "key": "magic", "value": 1}]})
        assert bad_key.status_code == 422
        bad_year = client.put("/api/v1/admin/fin-assumptions",
                              json={"items": [{**item, "year": 2040, "value": 1}]})
        assert bad_year.status_code == 422
    finally:
        client.put("/api/v1/admin/fin-assumptions", json={"items": [{**item, "value": 0.04}]})


@pytest.mark.usefixtures("admin")
def test_settings_update_and_validation(client: TestClient) -> None:
    r = client.put("/api/v1/admin/settings/freshness.max_age_hours", json={"value": 8})
    assert r.status_code == 200 and r.json()["value"] == 8
    try:
        listed = {s["key"]: s for s in client.get("/api/v1/admin/settings").json()}
        assert listed["freshness.max_age_hours"]["value"] == 8
        zero = client.put("/api/v1/admin/settings/freshness.max_age_hours", json={"value": 0})
        assert zero.status_code == 422 and zero.json()["error"]["code"] == "invalid_setting"
        assert client.put("/api/v1/admin/settings/no.such", json={"value": 1}).status_code == 404
        assert _audit("admin.setting.updated", "freshness.max_age_hours") >= 1
    finally:
        client.put("/api/v1/admin/settings/freshness.max_age_hours", json={"value": 6})
```

`backend/app/admin/dwh_schemas.py`:
```python
"""ETL, buildlar va versiyali sozlamalar admin sxemalari."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, datetime
from typing import Any, Literal

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict, Field, field_validator

from app.dwh.reference import FIN_KEYS, FIN_YEARS


class _In(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")


class EtlRunOut(BaseModel):
    id: int
    source: str
    mode: str
    status: str
    started_at: datetime
    finished_at: datetime | None
    window_from: date | None
    window_to: date | None
    rows_in: int
    rows_upserted: int
    build_id: int | None
    triggered_by: int | None
    error: str | None


class EtlStepOut(BaseModel):
    name: str
    status: str
    started_at: datetime
    finished_at: datetime | None
    rows: int
    detail: dict[str, Any]


class EtlErrorOut(BaseModel):
    step: str
    severity: str
    target: str | None
    src_id: str | None
    code: str
    message: str
    created_at: datetime


class EtlRunDetail(BaseModel):
    run: EtlRunOut
    steps: list[EtlStepOut]
    errors: list[EtlErrorOut]


class EtlRunRequest(_In):
    source: str = Field(pattern=r"^[a-z][a-z0-9_]{1,31}$")
    mode: Literal["full", "incremental"] = "incremental"


class EtlRequestOut(BaseModel):
    request_id: int
    status: str


class BuildOut(BaseModel):
    build_id: int
    created_at: datetime
    status: str
    load_id: int | None
    published_at: datetime | None
    active: bool


class RollbackIn(_In):
    build_id: int | None = Field(default=None, gt=0)


class AlertRuleOut(BaseModel):
    code: str
    version: int
    title: str
    section: str
    page: str
    threshold: dict[str, Any]
    level: str
    enabled: bool
    updated_by: int | None
    updated_at: datetime


class AlertRuleUpdate(_In):
    value: float | None = Field(default=None, ge=-1_000_000, le=1_000_000)
    op: Literal["<", "<=", ">", ">="] | None = None
    level: Literal["critical", "warning"] | None = None
    enabled: bool | None = None


class FinAssumptionOut(BaseModel):
    scenario: str
    year: int
    key: str
    value: float
    version: int
    updated_by: int | None
    updated_at: datetime


class FinAssumptionItem(_In):
    scenario: Literal["base", "water", "tariff", "fx"]
    year: int
    key: str
    value: float = Field(ge=-10, le=10)

    @field_validator("year")
    @classmethod
    def _year(cls, value: int) -> int:
        if value not in FIN_YEARS:
            raise ValueError("year")
        return value

    @field_validator("key")
    @classmethod
    def _key(cls, value: str) -> str:
        if value not in FIN_KEYS:
            raise ValueError("key")
        return value


class FinAssumptionsUpdate(_In):
    items: list[FinAssumptionItem] = Field(min_length=1, max_length=200)


class SettingOut(BaseModel):
    key: str
    value: Any
    version: int
    updated_by: int | None
    updated_at: datetime | None


class SettingUpdate(_In):
    value: Any

```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — versiyali sozlamalar servisi**

`backend/app/admin/config_service.py`:
```python
"""Versiyali sozlamalar (spec 4.8): har o'zgarish — yangi version qatori + audit.
Eski versiyalar o'chirilmaydi (is_current = false) — tarix va audit uchun."""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.admin.dwh_schemas import (
    AlertRuleOut,
    AlertRuleUpdate,
    FinAssumptionOut,
    FinAssumptionsUpdate,
    SettingOut,
)
from app.audit.service import AuditAction, log_event
from app.auth.context import AuthContext
from app.core.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationFailed
from app.core.request_meta import ClientMeta
from app.dwh.reference import DEFAULT_SETTINGS, validate_setting

_RULE_COLS = ("code, version, title, section, page, threshold, level, enabled, updated_by, "
              "updated_at")


def _rule(row: Any) -> AlertRuleOut:
    return AlertRuleOut(**dict(row._mapping))


def list_alert_rules(db: Session) -> list[AlertRuleOut]:
    rows = db.execute(text(f"SELECT {_RULE_COLS} FROM core.alert_rules WHERE is_current "
                           "ORDER BY section, code")).all()
    return [_rule(r) for r in rows]


def alert_rule_history(db: Session, code: str) -> list[AlertRuleOut]:
    rows = db.execute(text(f"SELECT {_RULE_COLS} FROM core.alert_rules WHERE code = :c "
                           "ORDER BY version DESC"), {"c": code}).all()
    if not rows:
        raise NotFoundError()
    return [_rule(r) for r in rows]


def update_alert_rule(db: Session, actor: AuthContext, code: str, data: AlertRuleUpdate,
                      meta: ClientMeta) -> AlertRuleOut:
    row = db.execute(text(f"SELECT {_RULE_COLS} FROM core.alert_rules "
                          "WHERE code = :c AND is_current FOR UPDATE"), {"c": code}).first()
    if row is None:
        raise NotFoundError()
    current = _rule(row)
    threshold = dict(current.threshold)
    if data.value is not None:
        threshold["value"] = data.value
    if data.op is not None:
        threshold["op"] = data.op
    level = data.level or current.level
    enabled = current.enabled if data.enabled is None else data.enabled
    if (threshold, level, enabled) == (current.threshold, current.level, current.enabled):
        return current
    version = current.version + 1
    db.execute(text("UPDATE core.alert_rules SET is_current = false "
                    "WHERE code = :c AND is_current"), {"c": code})
    db.execute(text(
        "INSERT INTO core.alert_rules (code, version, title, section, page, threshold, level, "
        "enabled, updated_by) VALUES (:c, :v, :t, :s, :p, CAST(:th AS jsonb), :l, :e, :u)"),
        {"c": code, "v": version, "t": current.title, "s": current.section, "p": current.page,
         "th": json.dumps(threshold), "l": level, "e": enabled, "u": actor.user_id})
    log_event(meta, AuditAction.ALERT_RULE_UPDATED, user_id=actor.user_id,
              object_type="alert_rule", object_id=code,
              details={"version": version,
                       "before": {"threshold": current.threshold, "level": current.level,
                                  "enabled": current.enabled},
                       "after": {"threshold": threshold, "level": level, "enabled": enabled}})
    return alert_rule_history(db, code)[0]


_FIN_COLS = "scenario, year, key, value, version, updated_by, updated_at"


def list_fin_assumptions(db: Session, scenario: str | None) -> list[FinAssumptionOut]:
    rows = db.execute(text(
        f"SELECT {_FIN_COLS} FROM core.fin_assumptions WHERE is_current "
        "AND (CAST(:s AS text) IS NULL OR scenario = :s) ORDER BY scenario, year, key"),
        {"s": scenario}).all()
    return [FinAssumptionOut(**{**dict(r._mapping), "value": float(r.value)}) for r in rows]


def update_fin_assumptions(db: Session, actor: AuthContext, data: FinAssumptionsUpdate,
                           meta: ClientMeta) -> list[FinAssumptionOut]:
    changes: list[dict[str, Any]] = []
    for item in data.items:
        key = {"s": item.scenario, "y": item.year, "k": item.key}
        row = db.execute(text(
            "SELECT version, value FROM core.fin_assumptions WHERE scenario = :s AND year = :y "
            "AND key = :k AND is_current FOR UPDATE"), key).first()
        if row is None:
            raise NotFoundError()
        new_value = Decimal(str(item.value))
        if new_value == row.value:
            continue
        db.execute(text("UPDATE core.fin_assumptions SET is_current = false WHERE scenario = :s "
                        "AND year = :y AND key = :k AND is_current"), key)
        db.execute(text(
            "INSERT INTO core.fin_assumptions (scenario, year, key, version, value, updated_by) "
            "VALUES (:s, :y, :k, :v, :val, :u)"),
            {**key, "v": row.version + 1, "val": new_value, "u": actor.user_id})
        changes.append({"scenario": item.scenario, "year": item.year, "key": item.key,
                        "before": str(row.value), "after": str(new_value),
                        "version": row.version + 1})
    if changes:
        log_event(meta, AuditAction.FIN_ASSUMPTIONS_UPDATED, user_id=actor.user_id,
                  object_type="fin_assumptions", object_id="batch",
                  details={"changes": changes})
    return list_fin_assumptions(db, None)


def _setting_row(db: Session, key: str, lock: bool = False) -> Any:
    suffix = " FOR UPDATE" if lock else ""
    return db.execute(text("SELECT key, value, version, updated_by, updated_at FROM core.settings "
                           f"WHERE key = :k AND is_current{suffix}"), {"k": key}).first()


def list_settings(db: Session) -> list[SettingOut]:
    out: list[SettingOut] = []
    for key, default in DEFAULT_SETTINGS.items():
        row = _setting_row(db, key)
        if row is None:
            out.append(SettingOut(key=key, value=default, version=0, updated_by=None,
                                  updated_at=None))
        else:
            out.append(SettingOut(**dict(row._mapping)))
    return out


def update_setting(db: Session, actor: AuthContext, key: str, value: Any,
                   meta: ClientMeta) -> SettingOut:
    if key not in DEFAULT_SETTINGS:
        raise NotFoundError()
    try:
        normalized = validate_setting(key, value)
    except ValueError as exc:
        raise ValidationFailed("invalid_setting", key=key) from exc
    row = _setting_row(db, key, lock=True)
    if row is not None and row.value == normalized:
        return SettingOut(**dict(row._mapping))
    version = 1 if row is None else row.version + 1
    db.execute(text("UPDATE core.settings SET is_current = false WHERE key = :k AND is_current"),
               {"k": key})
    db.execute(text("INSERT INTO core.settings (key, version, value, updated_by) "
                    "VALUES (:k, :v, CAST(:val AS jsonb), :u)"),
               {"k": key, "v": version, "val": json.dumps(normalized), "u": actor.user_id})
    log_event(meta, AuditAction.SETTING_UPDATED, user_id=actor.user_id, object_type="setting",
              object_id=key, details={"version": version,
                                      "before": None if row is None else row.value,
                                      "after": normalized})
    new_row = _setting_row(db, key)
    return SettingOut(**dict(new_row._mapping))
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implementatsiya — router'lar**

`backend/app/admin/etl_router.py`:
```python
"""ETL jurnali, qo'lda ishga tushirish (navbat orqali — worker bajaradi), buildlar (admin:etl)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Annotated

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.admin.dwh_schemas import (
    BuildOut,
    EtlErrorOut,
    EtlRequestOut,
    EtlRunDetail,
    EtlRunOut,
    EtlRunRequest,
    EtlStepOut,
    RollbackIn,
)
from app.admin.schemas import Page
from app.audit.service import AuditAction, log_event
from app.auth.context import AuthContext, require_perm
from app.auth.permissions import Perm
from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.core.errors import NotFoundError, ValidationFailed
from app.core.request_meta import Meta
from app.dwh import builds
from app.etl.connectors import KNOWN_SOURCES

router = APIRouter(prefix="/admin", tags=["admin"])
DB = Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)]
EtlAdmin = Annotated[AuthContext, Depends(require_perm(Perm.ADMIN_ETL))]
_RUN_COLS = ("id, source, mode, status, started_at, finished_at, window_from, window_to, "
             "rows_in, rows_upserted, build_id, triggered_by, error")


@router.get("/etl/runs")
def list_runs(
    ctx: EtlAdmin,
    db: DB,
    page: Annotated[int, Query(ge=1)] = 1,
    page_size: Annotated[int, Query(ge=1, le=500)] = 50,
) -> Page[EtlRunOut]:
    total = db.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM core.etl_run")).scalar_one()
    rows = db.execute(text(f"SELECT {_RUN_COLS} FROM core.etl_run ORDER BY id DESC "
                           "LIMIT :l OFFSET :o"),
                      {"l": page_size, "o": (page - 1) * page_size}).all()
    items = [EtlRunOut(**dict(r._mapping)) for r in rows]
    return Page[EtlRunOut](items=items, total=int(total), page=page, page_size=page_size)


@router.get("/etl/runs/{run_id}")
def run_detail(run_id: int, ctx: EtlAdmin, db: DB) -> EtlRunDetail:
    row = db.execute(text(f"SELECT {_RUN_COLS} FROM core.etl_run WHERE id = :r"),
                     {"r": run_id}).first()
    if row is None:
        raise NotFoundError()
    steps = db.execute(text("SELECT name, status, started_at, finished_at, rows, detail "
                            "FROM core.etl_step WHERE run_id = :r ORDER BY id"),
                       {"r": run_id}).all()
    errors = db.execute(text("SELECT step, severity, target, src_id, code, message, created_at "
                             "FROM core.etl_error WHERE run_id = :r ORDER BY id LIMIT 500"),
                        {"r": run_id}).all()
    return EtlRunDetail(run=EtlRunOut(**dict(row._mapping)),
                        steps=[EtlStepOut(**dict(s._mapping)) for s in steps],
                        errors=[EtlErrorOut(**dict(e._mapping)) for e in errors])


@router.post("/etl/run", status_code=202)
def request_run(body: EtlRunRequest, ctx: EtlAdmin, db: DB, meta: Meta) -> EtlRequestOut:
    if body.source not in KNOWN_SOURCES:
        raise ValidationFailed("unknown_source", source=body.source)
    if body.source == "synthetic" and get_settings().env == "prod":
        raise ValidationFailed("source_forbidden", source=body.source)
    request_id = int(db.execute(
        text("INSERT INTO core.etl_requests (source, mode, requested_by) "
             "VALUES (:s, :m, :u) RETURNING id"),
        {"s": body.source, "m": body.mode, "u": ctx.user_id}).scalar_one())
    log_event(meta, AuditAction.ETL_RUN_REQUESTED, user_id=ctx.user_id, object_type="etl_request",
              object_id=request_id, details={"source": body.source, "mode": body.mode})
    return EtlRequestOut(request_id=request_id, status="pending")


def _build_out(info: builds.BuildInfo) -> BuildOut:
    return BuildOut(build_id=info.build_id, created_at=info.created_at, status=info.status,
                    load_id=info.load_id, published_at=info.published_at, active=info.active)


@router.get("/builds")
def list_builds(ctx: EtlAdmin, db: DB) -> list[BuildOut]:
    return [_build_out(b) for b in builds.list_builds(db.connection())]


@router.post("/builds/rollback")
def rollback_build(body: RollbackIn, ctx: EtlAdmin, db: DB, meta: Meta) -> BuildOut:
    conn = db.connection()
    previous = builds.active_build(conn)
    target = builds.rollback(conn, body.build_id)
    log_event(meta, AuditAction.BUILD_ROLLED_BACK, user_id=ctx.user_id, object_type="build",
              object_id=target, details={"from": previous, "to": target})
    return next(_build_out(b) for b in builds.list_builds(conn) if b.build_id == target)
```

`backend/app/admin/config_router.py`:

"""«Требует внимания» chegaralari, финмодель допущения, tizim sozlamalari (admin:settings)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Annotated

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Query
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.admin import config_service as service
from app.admin.dwh_schemas import (
    AlertRuleOut,
    AlertRuleUpdate,
    FinAssumptionOut,
    FinAssumptionsUpdate,
    SettingOut,
    SettingUpdate,
)
from app.auth.context import AuthContext, require_perm
from app.auth.permissions import Perm
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.core.request_meta import Meta

router = APIRouter(prefix="/admin", tags=["admin"])
DB = Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)]
SettingsAdmin = Annotated[AuthContext, Depends(require_perm(Perm.ADMIN_SETTINGS))]


@router.get("/alert-rules")
def list_alert_rules(ctx: SettingsAdmin, db: DB) -> list[AlertRuleOut]:
    return service.list_alert_rules(db)


@router.get("/alert-rules/{code}/history")
def alert_rule_history(code: str, ctx: SettingsAdmin, db: DB) -> list[AlertRuleOut]:
    return service.alert_rule_history(db, code)


@router.patch("/alert-rules/{code}")
def update_alert_rule(code: str, body: AlertRuleUpdate, ctx: SettingsAdmin, db: DB,
                      meta: Meta) -> AlertRuleOut:
    return service.update_alert_rule(db, ctx, code, body, meta)


@router.get("/fin-assumptions")
def list_fin_assumptions(
    ctx: SettingsAdmin,
    db: DB,
    scenario: Annotated[str | None, Query(pattern=r"^(base|water|tariff|fx)$")] = None,
) -> list[FinAssumptionOut]:
    return service.list_fin_assumptions(db, scenario)


@router.put("/fin-assumptions")
def update_fin_assumptions(body: FinAssumptionsUpdate, ctx: SettingsAdmin, db: DB,
                           meta: Meta) -> list[FinAssumptionOut]:
    return service.update_fin_assumptions(db, ctx, body, meta)


@router.get("/settings")
def list_settings(ctx: SettingsAdmin, db: DB) -> list[SettingOut]:
    return service.list_settings(db)


@router.put("/settings/{key}")
def update_setting(key: str, body: SettingUpdate, ctx: SettingsAdmin, db: DB,
                   meta: Meta) -> SettingOut:
    return service.update_setting(db, ctx, key, body.value, meta)

Job id'lari: `etl_synthetic_full` (faqat `ENV=dev`), `etl_requests`, `partitions`, `cleanup`, `retire_keys`, `freshness`, `heartbeat`.

`backend/tests/test_worker.py`:
```python
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import UTC, date, datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app import cli, worker
from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.db import get_engine
from app.dwh import builds, partitions
from app.dwh.freshness import freshness
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.etl.runner import RunResult
from app.maintenance import cleanup_staging
from tests.etl_helpers import (
    TEST_AS_OF,
    ListConnector,
    RecordingNotifier,
    branch_row,
    cash_row,
    new_source,
    new_tag,
    open_run,
    purge_source,
)

JOBS = {"etl_synthetic_full", "etl_requests", "partitions", "cleanup", "retire_keys",
        "freshness", "heartbeat"}


@pytest.fixture
def source() -> Iterator[str]:
    name = new_source()
    with etl_transaction() as conn:  # boshqa testlardan qolgan so'rovlar bajarilmasin
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.etl_requests SET status = 'failed' "
                          "WHERE status = 'pending'"))
    yield name
    purge_source(name)


def _request(source: str) -> int:
    with get_engine("app").begin() as conn:
        return int(conn.execute(text(
            "INSERT INTO core.etl_requests (source, mode, requested_by) "
            "VALUES (:s, 'full', 1) RETURNING id"), {"s": source}).scalar_one())


def _request_row(request_id: int) -> Any:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        return conn.execute(text("SELECT status, run_id, error FROM core.etl_requests "
                                 "WHERE id = :i"), {"i": request_id}).one()


def _rows() -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    b = branch_row(new_tag())
    return {"dim_branch": [b], "fact_cashflow": [cash_row("w1", 20260805, b["code"], 10)]}


def test_scheduler_jobs_dev_and_prod() -> None:
    notifier = RecordingNotifier()
    dev = worker.build_scheduler(get_settings().model_copy(update={"env": "dev"}), notifier)
    assert {j.id for j in dev.get_jobs()} == JOBS
    prod = worker.build_scheduler(get_settings().model_copy(update={"env": "prod"}), notifier)
    assert {j.id for j in prod.get_jobs()} == JOBS - {"etl_synthetic_full"}


def test_process_requests_runs_pending_request(source: str) -> None:
    request_id = _request(source)
    done = worker.process_requests(RecordingNotifier(),
                                   factory=lambda s: ListConnector(s, _rows()), today=TEST_AS_OF)
    assert done == 1
    status, run_id, error = _request_row(request_id)
    assert (status, error) == ("done", None) and run_id is not None
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        run_status = conn.execute(text("SELECT status FROM core.etl_run WHERE id = :r"),
                                  {"r": run_id}).scalar_one()
    assert run_status == "success"


def test_process_requests_leaves_pending_when_busy(source: str) -> None:
    request_id = _request(source)
    with get_engine("etl").connect() as raw:
        other = raw.execution_options(isolation_level="AUTOCOMMIT")
        other.execute(text("SELECT pg_advisory_lock(hashtext('ugk_etl'))"))
        try:
            assert worker.process_requests(
                RecordingNotifier(), factory=lambda s: ListConnector(s, _rows()),
                today=TEST_AS_OF) == 0
        finally:
            other.execute(text("SELECT pg_advisory_unlock(hashtext('ugk_etl'))"))
    assert _request_row(request_id)[0] == "pending"


def test_process_requests_unknown_source_fails(source: str) -> None:
    request_id = _request(source)

    def factory(name: str) -> ListConnector:
        raise ValueError(f"noma'lum manba: {name}")

    assert worker.process_requests(RecordingNotifier(), factory=factory) == 1
    status, run_id, error = _request_row(request_id)
    assert status == "failed" and run_id is None and "noma'lum manba" in error


def test_maintain_partitions_creates_ahead_and_drops_old() -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        partitions.ensure_partitions(conn, "fact_stock", date(2017, 1, 1), date(2017, 12, 31))
    result = worker.maintain_partitions(date(2033, 5, 10))
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        cash = set(partitions.list_partitions(conn, "fact_cashflow"))
        stock = set(partitions.list_partitions(conn, "fact_stock"))
    assert {"fact_cashflow_p203305", "fact_cashflow_p203308"} <= cash
    assert "fact_stock_p2017" not in stock
    assert result["dropped"] >= 1
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        audited = conn.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM core.audit_log "
                                    "WHERE action = 'dwh.partitions.dropped'")).scalar_one()
    assert audited >= 1


def test_check_freshness_notifies_once_per_stale_period(
    monkeypatch: pytest.MonkeyPatch, synthetic_run: RunResult
) -> None:
    monkeypatch.setattr(worker, "_alerted_for", worker._NEVER)
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        last = freshness(conn, datetime.now(UTC)).last_success_at
    assert last is not None
    notifier = RecordingNotifier()
    assert worker.check_freshness(notifier, now=last + timedelta(hours=7)) is True
    assert worker.check_freshness(notifier, now=last + timedelta(hours=8)) is True
    assert notifier.kinds == ["stale_data"]
    assert worker.check_freshness(notifier, now=last + timedelta(hours=1)) is False
    assert worker.check_freshness(notifier, now=last + timedelta(hours=9)) is True
    assert notifier.kinds == ["stale_data", "stale_data"]


def test_cleanup_staging_removes_old_loads(source: str) -> None:
    run_id = open_run(source)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        conn.execute(text("INSERT INTO stg.rows (load_id, target, payload) "
                          "VALUES (:l, 'dim_item', '{}'::jsonb)"), {"l": run_id})
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.etl_run SET started_at = now() - interval '10 days', "
                          "status = 'failed' WHERE id = :l"), {"l": run_id})
    assert cleanup_staging(7) >= 1
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        left = conn.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM stg.rows WHERE load_id = :l"),
                            {"l": run_id}).scalar_one()
    assert left == 0


def test_heartbeat(tmp_path: Path) -> None:
    path = tmp_path / "hb"
    worker.heartbeat(path)
    assert path.exists()


def test_cli_etl_run_exit_codes(capsys: pytest.CaptureFixture[str]) -> None:
    assert cli.main(["etl-run", "--source", "oracle", "--mode", "full"]) == 2
    # etl_file_dir (/data/inbox) test konteynerida yo'q → manba mavjud emas, qayta urinishsiz.
    assert cli.main(["etl-run", "--source", "file", "--mode", "full", "--no-retry"]) == 1
    assert "status=failed" in capsys.readouterr().out


def test_cli_rollback_build(published_synthetic: int) -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        newer = builds.create_build(conn, None)
        builds.mark_ready(conn, newer)
        builds.publish(conn, newer, keep=3)
    assert cli.main(["rollback-build"]) == 0
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        assert builds.active_build(conn) == published_synthetic
    assert cli.main(["rollback-build", "--build", "999999999"]) == 1

`backend/app/audit/service.py` — `AuditAction` ichida `RLS_VIOLATION` qatoridan oldin qo'shing:
```python
    PARTITIONS_DROPPED = "dwh.partitions.dropped"

`backend/app/maintenance.py`:
```python
"""Davriy xizmat vazifalari — CLI (`python -m app.cli`) va worker ikkalasi ishlatadi."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import UTC, datetime, timedelta
from typing import Any, cast

from sqlalchemy import CursorResult, delete, or_, text

from app.auth.models import MfaChallenge, UserSession
from app.core.crypto import Purpose
from app.core.db import get_engine, new_session
from app.core.reencrypt import retire_unused
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction


def cleanup_sessions(now: datetime | None = None) -> tuple[int, int]:
    """Eskirgan 2FA challenge (1 kun) va sessiyalar (30 kun) — (challenge, sessiya) soni."""
    now = now or datetime.now(UTC)
    with new_session() as db:
        challenges = cast(CursorResult[Any], db.execute(
            delete(MfaChallenge).where(MfaChallenge.expires_at < now - timedelta(days=1))
        )).rowcount
        old = now - timedelta(days=30)
        stale = cast(CursorResult[Any], db.execute(
            delete(UserSession).where(
                or_(UserSession.absolute_expires_at < old, UserSession.revoked_at < old))
        )).rowcount
        db.commit()
    return challenges, stale


def retire_keys(grace: timedelta) -> dict[str, list[int]]:
    out: dict[str, list[int]] = {}
    with get_engine("app").begin() as conn:
        for purpose in Purpose:
            retired = retire_unused(conn, purpose, grace)
            if retired:
                out[purpose.value] = retired
    return out


def cleanup_staging(days: int = 7) -> int:
    """`days` kundan eski (yoki jurnalda yo'q) yuklashlarning staging qatorlari."""
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        removed = conn.execute(text(
            "DELETE FROM stg.rows r WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM core.etl_run e "
            "WHERE e.id = r.load_id AND e.started_at > now() - make_interval(days => :d))"),
            {"d": days}).rowcount
        conn.execute(text(
            "DELETE FROM stg.batch b WHERE NOT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM core.etl_run e "
            "WHERE e.id = b.load_id AND e.started_at > now() - make_interval(days => :d))"),
            {"d": days})
    return int(removed)
```

`backend/app/cli.py` — o'zgarishlar:

1) Modul docstring'iga ikki qator qo'shing:
```
etl-run        — ETL yuklash: --source synthetic|file --mode full|incremental [--no-retry]
rollback-build — aktiv build'ni oldingisiga (yoki --build ID ga) qaytarish
```

2) `_retire` va `_cleanup` funksiyalarini almashtiring:
```python
def _retire(grace_minutes: int) -> int:
    for purpose, retired in retire_keys(timedelta(minutes=grace_minutes)).items():
        _out(f"{purpose}: yo'q qilindi {retired}")
    return 0


def _cleanup() -> int:
    challenges, stale = cleanup_sessions()
    staged = cleanup_staging()
    _out(f"o'chirildi: challenge={challenges}, sessiya={stale}, staging={staged}")
    return 0


def _etl_run(source: str, mode: str, no_retry: bool) -> int:
    try:
        connector = make_connector(source)
    except (ValueError, ConfigError) as exc:
        _out(f"Xato: {exc}")
        return 2
    runner = Runner(connector, mode=Mode(mode), notifier=build_notifier(get_settings()),
                    delays=() if no_retry else RETRY_DELAYS)
    try:
        result = runner.run()
    except EtlBusyError:
        _out("Xato: boshqa yuklash bajarilmoqda")
        return 3
    _out(f"run={result.run_id} status={result.status} build={result.build_id}")
    if result.error:
        _out(f"xato: {result.error}")
    return 0 if result.status == "success" else 1


def _rollback(build_id: int | None) -> int:
    try:
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            previous = builds.active_build(conn)
            target = builds.rollback(conn, build_id)
    except ConflictError:
        _out("Xato: bunday build yo'q yoki unga qaytib bo'lmaydi")
        return 1
    record(AuditEvent(action=AuditAction.BUILD_ROLLED_BACK, object_type="build",
                      object_id=str(target), details={"from": previous, "to": target,
                                                      "via": "cli"}))
    _out(f"Aktiv build: {previous} → {target}")
    return 0
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — worker**

`backend/app/worker.py`:
```python
"""ETL worker (spec 5.1): APScheduler BlockingScheduler. `python -m app.worker`.

Joblar (Toshkent vaqti): dev'da kechasi to'liq sintetik yuklash (prod'da haqiqiy konnektorlar
keyingi rejalarda), admin navbatidagi so'rovlar (30 s), partitsiyalar (03:30), sessiya/staging
tozalash (04:00), eski kalitlarni yo'q qilish (04:15), freshness (30 daqiqa), heartbeat.
Har xizmat jobi o'z advisory lock'i ostida; ETL runner'ning o'z global lock'i bor.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator
from contextlib import contextmanager
from datetime import UTC, date, datetime, timedelta
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

from apscheduler.schedulers.blocking import BlockingScheduler
from apscheduler.triggers.cron import CronTrigger
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.audit.service import AuditAction, AuditEvent, record
from app.bootstrap import bootstrap_runtime
from app.core.config import ConfigError, Settings, get_settings
from app.core.db import get_engine
from app.core.logging import configure_logging, get_logger
from app.dwh import partitions
from app.dwh.dates import TASHKENT, today_tashkent
from app.dwh.freshness import freshness
from app.dwh.reference import current_setting
from app.etl.connectors import make_connector
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from app.etl.notify import Notification, Notifier, build_notifier
from app.etl.runner import EtlBusyError, RunResult, Runner
from app.etl.types import Mode, SourceConnector
from app.maintenance import cleanup_sessions, cleanup_staging, retire_keys

log = get_logger("ugekazna.worker")
HEARTBEAT = Path("/tmp/worker-heartbeat")  # noqa: S108 — tmpfs, faqat healthcheck uchun
_NEVER: Any = object()
_alerted_for: Any = _NEVER


@contextmanager
def job_lock(name: str) -> Iterator[bool]:
    key = f"ugk_job:{name}"
    with get_engine("etl").connect() as raw:
        conn = raw.execution_options(isolation_level="AUTOCOMMIT")
        got = bool(conn.execute(text("SELECT pg_try_advisory_lock(hashtext(:k))"),
                                {"k": key}).scalar_one())
        try:
            yield got
        finally:
            if got:
                conn.execute(text("SELECT pg_advisory_unlock(hashtext(:k))"), {"k": key})


def _guarded(name: str, fn: Callable[[], object]) -> Callable[[], None]:
    def job() -> None:
        with job_lock(name) as got:
            if not got:
                log.info("job_skipped_locked", job=name)
                return
            try:
                fn()
            except Exception as exc:  # job xatosi scheduler'ni to'xtatmasin
                log.error("job_failed", job=name, error=type(exc).__name__, exc_info=exc)

    return job


def run_etl_job(source: str, mode: Mode, notifier: Notifier) -> RunResult | None:
    try:
        return Runner(make_connector(source), mode=mode, notifier=notifier).run()
    except EtlBusyError:
        log.info("etl_skipped_busy", source=source)
        return None


def _set_request(request_id: int, status: str, run_id: int | None = None,
                 error: str | None = None) -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.etl_requests SET status = :s, run_id = :r, error = :e "
                          "WHERE id = :i"),
                     {"s": status, "r": run_id, "e": None if error is None else error[:500],
                      "i": request_id})


def process_requests(
    notifier: Notifier,
    *,
    factory: Callable[[str], SourceConnector] = make_connector,
    today: date | None = None,
) -> int:
    """Admin navbatidagi so'rovlarni ketma-ket bajaradi; bajarilganlar sonini qaytaradi."""
    done = 0
    while True:
        with etl_transaction() as conn:
            row = conn.execute(text(
                "SELECT id, source, mode, requested_by FROM core.etl_requests "
                "WHERE status = 'pending' ORDER BY id LIMIT 1 FOR UPDATE SKIP LOCKED")).first()
            if row is None:
                return done
            conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.etl_requests SET status = 'running' WHERE id = :i"),
                         {"i": row.id})
        try:
            connector = factory(row.source)
            result = Runner(connector, mode=Mode(row.mode), notifier=notifier,
                            triggered_by=row.requested_by, today=today).run()
        except EtlBusyError:
            _set_request(row.id, "pending")  # keyingi tekshiruvda qayta urinamiz
            return done
        except Exception as exc:
            _set_request(row.id, "failed", error=f"{type(exc).__name__}: {exc}")
            done += 1
            continue
        _set_request(row.id, "done" if result.status == "success" else "failed",
                     result.run_id, result.error)
        done += 1


def maintain_partitions(today: date) -> dict[str, int]:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        months_ahead = int(current_setting(conn, "partitions.months_ahead"))
        keep_years = int(current_setting(conn, "retention.fact_years"))
        audit_months = int(current_setting(conn, "retention.audit_months"))
        created = partitions.ensure_ahead(conn, today, months_ahead)
        dropped = partitions.drop_old(conn, keep_years)
        conn.execute(text("SELECT core.ensure_audit_partitions(3)"))
        audit_dropped = int(conn.execute(text("SELECT core.drop_old_audit_partitions(:m)"),
                                         {"m": audit_months}).scalar_one())
    result = {"created": created, "dropped": dropped, "audit_dropped": audit_dropped}
    log.info("partitions_maintained", **result)
    if dropped or audit_dropped:
        record(AuditEvent(action=AuditAction.PARTITIONS_DROPPED, object_type="partitions",
                          details={**result, "keep_years": keep_years,
                                   "audit_months": audit_months}))
    return result


def cleanup_job() -> None:
    challenges, stale = cleanup_sessions()
    staged = cleanup_staging()
    log.info("cleanup_done", challenges=challenges, sessions=stale, staging=staged)


def retire_keys_job() -> None:
    log.info("keys_retired", retired=retire_keys(timedelta(minutes=10)))


def check_freshness(notifier: Notifier, now: datetime | None = None) -> bool:
    """>6 soat yangilanmagan bo'lsa xabar; bitta eskirish holati uchun bitta xabar."""
    global _alerted_for
    with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
        state = freshness(conn, now or datetime.now(UTC))
    if not state.stale:
        _alerted_for = _NEVER
        return False
    if _alerted_for is _NEVER or _alerted_for != state.last_success_at:
        last = "никогда" if state.last_success_at is None else \
            state.last_success_at.astimezone(TASHKENT).strftime("%d.%m.%Y %H:%M")
        notifier.notify(Notification(
            "stale_data", "Данные витрины устарели",
            f"Последняя успешная загрузка: {last}. Порог: {state.max_age_hours} ч."))
        _alerted_for = state.last_success_at
    return True


def heartbeat(path: Path = HEARTBEAT) -> None:
    path.touch()


def build_scheduler(settings: Settings, notifier: Notifier, *,
                    scheduler: BlockingScheduler | None = None) -> BlockingScheduler:
    scheduler = scheduler or BlockingScheduler(timezone=TASHKENT)
    opts: dict[str, Any] = {"max_instances": 1, "coalesce": True, "misfire_grace_time": 3600}
    if settings.env == "dev":
        with get_engine("app").connect() as conn:
            cron = str(current_setting(conn, "etl.synthetic_cron"))
        scheduler.add_job(run_etl_job, CronTrigger.from_crontab(cron, timezone=TASHKENT),
                          args=("synthetic", Mode.FULL, notifier), id="etl_synthetic_full",
                          **opts)
    scheduler.add_job(process_requests, "interval", seconds=30, args=(notifier,),
                      id="etl_requests", **opts)
    scheduler.add_job(_guarded("partitions", lambda: maintain_partitions(today_tashkent())),
                      CronTrigger(hour=3, minute=30, timezone=TASHKENT), id="partitions", **opts)
    scheduler.add_job(_guarded("cleanup", cleanup_job),
                      CronTrigger(hour=4, minute=0, timezone=TASHKENT), id="cleanup", **opts)
    scheduler.add_job(_guarded("retire_keys", retire_keys_job),
                      CronTrigger(hour=4, minute=15, timezone=TASHKENT), id="retire_keys", **opts)
    scheduler.add_job(check_freshness, "interval", minutes=30, args=(notifier,),
                      id="freshness", **opts)
    scheduler.add_job(heartbeat, "interval", seconds=60, id="heartbeat", **opts)
    return scheduler


def main() -> None:
    configure_logging()
    settings = get_settings()
    problems = settings.problems()
    if problems:
        raise ConfigError("; ".join(problems))
    bootstrap_runtime()
    heartbeat()
    scheduler = build_scheduler(settings, build_notifier(settings))
    log.info("worker_started", env=settings.env, jobs=sorted(j.id for j in scheduler.get_jobs()))
    scheduler.start()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

Run: `$TEST pytest -v`
Expected: barcha testlar passed (1-rejaning `test_cli_create_admin_and_audit_verify` — `cleanup` endi `staging=` ni ham chiqaradi).

- [ ] **Step 6: Dev stack — worker servisi, Makefile, README**

`deploy/compose.yml` — `secrets:` bo'limiga qo'shing:
```yaml
  smtp_password: {file: ./secrets/smtp_password}
```
`services:` ga (`api` dan keyin) qo'shing:
```yaml
  worker:
    <<: *hardening
    image: ugekazna-backend:dev
    user: "10001:10001"
    environment:
      ENV: dev
      ETL_FILE_DIR: /data/inbox
      # SMTP_HOST / SMTP_TO / SMTP_FROM — ichki relay bo'lsa shu yerda beriladi.
    secrets: [db_app_password, db_etl_password, redis_password, master_kek, blind_index_key,
              session_pepper, smtp_password]
    volumes:
      - ../backend/app:/app/app:ro
      - etl_inbox:/data/inbox:ro
    tmpfs: [/tmp]
    command: ["python", "-m", "app.worker"]
    networks: [backend]
    healthcheck:
      test: ["CMD", "python", "-c", "import os, time, sys; sys.exit(0 if time.time() - os.stat('/tmp/worker-heartbeat').st_mtime < 180 else 1)"]
      interval: 60s
      timeout: 5s
      retries: 3
      start_period: 60s
    depends_on:
      migrate: {condition: service_completed_successfully}
      redis: {condition: service_healthy}
```
`volumes:` bo'limiga `etl_inbox:` qo'shing.

`Makefile` — `.PHONY` qatorini almashtiring va oxiriga target'lar qo'shing:
```makefile
.PHONY: secrets lock build up down logs create-admin test lint typecheck etl-run seed-synthetic rollback-build
```
```makefile
etl-run: ## make etl-run SOURCE=synthetic MODE=full
	$(COMPOSE) exec worker python -m app.cli etl-run --source "$(SOURCE)" --mode "$(MODE)"

seed-synthetic: ## dev: 2024-01-01 dan bugungacha sintetik ma'lumot
	$(COMPOSE) exec worker python -m app.cli etl-run --source synthetic --mode full

rollback-build: ## make rollback-build [BUILD=id]
	$(COMPOSE) exec worker python -m app.cli rollback-build $(if $(BUILD),--build $(BUILD),)
```

`README.md` — "Tez boshlash" bo'limidagi `create-admin` buyrug'idan keyin qo'shing:
````markdown
Sintetik ma'lumot (dev): `docker compose -f deploy/compose.yml exec worker python -m app.cli etl-run --source synthetic --mode full`
(worker dev'da buni har kecha 02:00 da o'zi bajaradi). Aktiv build'ni qaytarish: `python -m app.cli rollback-build`.
CSV yuklash: fayllarni `etl_inbox` volume'iga (`<maqsad>.csv`, UTF-8, `;`, ISO sanalar) qo'yib, `etl-run --source file`.
````

Expected: `worker` holati `healthy` (60–120 s ichida), `etl-run` chiqishi `status=success build=<N>`, loglarda `worker_started` va `jobs` ro'yxatida `etl_synthetic_full`. Agar `worker` `read_only` sababli ishga tushmasa — `logs worker` ni ko'rib sababni tuzating, hardening'ni olib tashlamang.

- [ ] **Step 8: Lint, tiplar va spec'ga chetlanishlar**

Run: `$TEST ruff check app tests && $TEST mypy app`
Expected: `All checks passed!` va `Success: no issues found`. Topilganlarni tuzatib `$TEST pytest` ni qayta ishga tushiring.

`docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-24-poydevor-design.md` — quyidagi almashtirishlar:
1. 5.2: "Inkremental oyna: `max(watermark − 45 kun, joriy oyning 1-kuni)` dan hozirgacha" → "Inkremental oyna: `min(watermark − 45 kun, joriy oyning 1-kuni)` dan hozirgacha (eski watermark bo'lsa oraliq ham qamraladi)".
2. 4.2: "- Tarixiy o'lchovlar SCD-2 (`valid_from`, `valid_to`, `is_current`): `dim_branch` (quvvat), `dim_loan` (stavka)." → "- Tarixiy o'lchovlar SCD-2 alohida versiya jadvallarida (`valid_from`, `valid_to`, `is_current`): `dim_branch_version` (quvvat), `dim_loan_version` (stavka); `dim_branch`/`dim_loan` — FK nishoni (joriy qiymat)."
3. 4.2: "`UNIQUE (src_system, doc_type, src_id)` — idempotentlik." → "`UNIQUE (src_system, doc_type, src_id)` — idempotentlik (partitsiyalangan faktlarda `+ date_id`, PostgreSQL talabi; sana o'zgarsa loader eski nusxani o'chiradi). O'lchovlarda — `code UNIQUE`."
4. 4.6: "`DETACH` + arxiv (pg_dump, shifrlangan) + `DROP`" → "`DETACH` + `DROP` (arxiv — pgBackRest zaxiralari)".
5. 5.1: "Qo'lda ishga tushirish: admin UI yoki `make etl-run SOURCE=… MODE=full|incremental`." → "Qo'lda ishga tushirish: admin UI (`core.etl_requests` navbati, worker bajaradi) yoki `make etl-run SOURCE=… MODE=full|incremental`."
6. 4.1 `stg` qatori: "manbadan xom yuklangan ma'lumot (har yuklash `load_id` bilan)" → "manbadan xom yuklangan ma'lumot: umumiy `stg.rows` (jsonb, UNLOGGED) + `stg.batch`, har yuklash `load_id` bilan; sezgir maydonlar staging'dan oldin shifrlanadi".
